CLASS lcl_material_balance IMPLEMENTATION.

  METHOD main.

    IF r_gen IS NOT INITIAL.

      me->generate_material_balance( ).

    ELSEIF r_disp IS NOT INITIAL.

      me->fetch_ztable_data( ).
      me->create_dyn_tab( ).
      me->process_material_balance( ).

    ELSEIF r_del IS NOT INITIAL.

      me->fetch_ztable_data( ).
      me->create_dyn_tab( ).
      me->process_material_balance( ).

    ENDIF.

    me->display_alv( ).

  ENDMETHOD.                    "main

*-> this method will be executed only in development, once the code becomes stable
*   there is no use of this method
  METHOD upd_mock_data_gen_matbal.

    DATA:
      lt_tab_list  TYPE TABLE OF abap_attrname,
      lv_file_path TYPE string.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS : <lfs_snpopiek> TYPE gty_snpopiek,
                    <lfs_snpoppro> TYPE gty_snpoppro.

    gv_total_steps = 20.
    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Uploading Mock Data...'(068)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
    ).

    APPEND 'GT_ECC_MAT_MASTER' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_LOC' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_MATKEY' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_PB_DATA' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_REG_OFF_DESC' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPARM_STOCK' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPBCT' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPDMD' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPDMN' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPIAM' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPIAR' TO lt_tab_list.
*    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPIAR_STOCK' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPLMA' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPLPR' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPMLO' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPPMA' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPPMO' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPPRO' TO lt_tab_list.
    APPEND 'GT_SNPOPIEK' TO lt_tab_list.

**    prd log 1 csv file location
*    lv_file_path = 'Z:\MATBAT Test\PRD\chng 20191118\P10068560012019.09.28-14 06 51\csv'.
*
**    prd log 2 csv file location
*    lv_file_path = 'Z:\MATBAT Test\PRD\20190829 164429 csv'.
*
**    uat log 1 csv file location
*    lv_file_path = 'Z:\MATBAT Test\UAT\P10045970012017.06.29-180158\csv'.

    "Start - CSV mock load only in dev when folder path is maintained
    IF c_dev = abap_true AND p_file IS NOT INITIAL.
      lv_file_path = p_file && '\csv'.
      ycl_helper_csv_upd_dwn=>upload_csv_to_internal_table(
        EXPORTING
          iv_upload_path       = lv_file_path
          iv_tbl_contains_srno = abap_true
          it_internal_tbl_list = lt_tab_list
          io_class_ref         = me
      ).
    ENDIF.
    "End - CSV mock load

    gt_pb_data_all = gt_pb_data.
    SORT gt_pb_data BY matnr planshp ASCENDING kf_bgbh DESCENDING.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_pb_data COMPARING matnr planshp.

    gt_snpopiar_stock = gt_snpopiar.

    SORT:
      gt_snpopmat BY matid,
      gt_snpoplma BY matid locid,
      gt_snpoplpr BY matid locid bucke,
      gt_snpopmlo BY matid locid bucke,
      gt_snpopiek BY inkey.

    IF gt_snpoppro[] IS NOT INITIAL.

      LOOP AT gt_snpoppro ASSIGNING <lfs_snpoppro>.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_snpopiek>.
        READ TABLE gt_snpopiek ASSIGNING <lfs_snpopiek> WITH KEY sessionid = <lfs_snpoppro>-sessionid
                                                                 inkey     = <lfs_snpoppro>-proid BINARY SEARCH.
        IF <lfs_snpopiek> IS ASSIGNED.
          IF <lfs_snpopiek>-exkey CS 'CONVERT'.
            <lfs_snpoppro>-ind_con = 'C'.
          ELSE.
            <lfs_snpoppro>-ind_con = 'P'.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

      ENDLOOP.

    ENDIF.


  ENDMETHOD.                    "upd_mock_data_gen_matbal

  METHOD filter_dev.

    DATA:
      ltr_locno TYPE RANGE OF /sapapo/locno.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_r_locno> LIKE LINE OF ltr_locno,
      <lfs_pbd>     TYPE gty_pb_data.

    IF c_dev = abap_true.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    SORT gt_pb_data BY matnr planshp ASCENDING kf_bgbh DESCENDING.

    IF s_matnr[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      DELETE:
        gt_input_dmd  WHERE NOT ( matnr IN s_matnr[] ),
        gt_output_dmd WHERE NOT ( matnr IN s_matnr[] ),
        gt_demand_loc WHERE NOT ( matnr IN s_matnr[] ),
        gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc WHERE NOT ( matnr IN s_matnr[]  ),
        gt_supply_dmn_cust_ttl WHERE NOT ( matnr IN s_matnr[]  ),
        gt_depo_supply WHERE NOT ( matnr IN s_matnr[] ),
        gt_pb_data WHERE NOT ( matnr IN s_matnr[] ),
        gt_prod_qty WHERE NOT ( matnr IN s_matnr[] ).
    ENDIF.

    IF s_clmth[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      DELETE:
        gt_input_dmd  WHERE NOT ( month IN s_clmth[] ),
        gt_output_dmd WHERE NOT ( month IN s_clmth[] ),
        gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc WHERE NOT ( cal_month IN s_clmth[] ),
        gt_supply_dmn_cust_ttl WHERE NOT ( month IN s_clmth[] ).
    ENDIF.

    IF s_regoff[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      DELETE:
        gt_pb_data      WHERE NOT reg_off IN s_regoff[],
        gt_reg_off_desc WHERE NOT reg_off IN s_regoff[].
      UNASSIGN <lfs_pbd>.
      LOOP AT gt_pb_data ASSIGNING <lfs_pbd>.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_r_locno>.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO ltr_locno ASSIGNING <lfs_r_locno>.
        IF <lfs_r_locno> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_r_locno>-sign    = 'I'.
          <lfs_r_locno>-option  = 'EQ'.
          <lfs_r_locno>-low     = <lfs_pbd>-planshp.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        DELETE:
          gt_input_dmd WHERE locno NOT IN ltr_locno,
          gt_output_dmd WHERE locno NOT IN ltr_locno.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF s_locno IS NOT INITIAL.
      DELETE:
        gt_input_dmd WHERE locno NOT IN s_locno,
        gt_output_dmd WHERE locno NOT IN s_locno.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "filter_dev

  METHOD generate_material_balance.

    DATA(lv_is_mock_mode) = xsdbool( c_dev = abap_true AND p_orun CP 'TEST*' ).

*    fetch param table data
    me->fetch_param_data( ).

    IF lv_is_mock_mode = abap_true.
*      load mock data from CSV file in given location (dev only)
      me->upd_mock_data_gen_matbal( ).
    ELSE.
*      fetch all log required data
      me->fetch_log_data( ).
    ENDIF.

*    process the bucket of log & change find its starting & ending date
    me->process_buckets( ).
*    calculate the intermediate quantites for producing material
*    balance report
    me->calulate_mat_bal_demand( ).

*    create dynamic table
    me->create_dyn_tab( ).

*    populate the supply qty for the customer based on region for its
*    corresponding source & supply location
    me->populate_supply( ).

    me->get_subtol_for_dmd( ).

    me->populate_demand_qty( ).

    me->populate_other_qty( ).

    me->get_non_prime_stock( ).

    me->save_to_table( ).

  ENDMETHOD.                    "generate_material_balance

  METHOD get_non_prime_stock.
    DATA : lt_param   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zapoparam,
           lt_buckets TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_bucket.

    DATA: lt_rfc TYPE TABLE OF zapo_rfc_struct,
          lw_rfc TYPE zapo_rfc_struct.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS : <lfs_matbal_hrd>            LIKE LINE OF gt_matbal_hrd,
                    <lfs_matbal_np_stk>         TYPE gty_matbal_np_stock,
                    <lfs_param>                 TYPE zapoparam,
                    <lfs_buckets>               TYPE gty_bucket,
                    <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs> TYPE gty_matbal_np_stock_clabs,
                    <lfs_table>                 TYPE ANY TABLE,
                    <lfs_final>                 TYPE any,
                    <lfs_comp>                  TYPE any,
                    <lv_meins>                  TYPE meins,
                    <lv_clabs>                  TYPE znonprime_stk.

    DATA : ev_zplgclass TYPE zparam2,
           ev_nonprime  TYPE zparam2,
           ls_fin_qty   TYPE gty_fin_qty.

    lt_param = gt_param.
    lt_buckets = gt_buckets.
    SORT lt_buckets BY cal_month.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_param>.
    READ TABLE lt_param ASSIGNING <lfs_param> WITH KEY param3 = 'NONPRIME_CLASS'.
    IF <lfs_param> IS ASSIGNED.
      ev_zplgclass = <lfs_param>-value1.
    ENDIF.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_param>.
    READ TABLE lt_param ASSIGNING <lfs_param> WITH KEY param3 = 'NONPRIME_IND'.
    IF <lfs_param> IS ASSIGNED.
      ev_nonprime = <lfs_param>-value1.
    ENDIF.
    SORT lt_param BY param3.
    DELETE lt_param WHERE param3 NE 'NONPRIME_STK'.
    IF gt_matbal_hrd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      LOOP AT gt_matbal_hrd ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_hrd>.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_matbal_np_stock ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_np_stk>.
        <lfs_matbal_np_stk>-div          = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-div.
        <lfs_matbal_np_stk>-matnr        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-matnr.
        <lfs_matbal_np_stk>-supply_plant = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-supply_plant.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
        READ TABLE lt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets> INDEX 1.
        IF <lfs_buckets> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_matbal_np_stk>-cal_month = <lfs_buckets>-cal_month.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_param>.
        LOOP AT lt_param ASSIGNING <lfs_param>.
          IF <lfs_param>-value1 CS <lfs_matbal_np_stk>-div.
            <lfs_matbal_np_stk>-indicator = <lfs_param>-param4.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.

      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_matbal_np_stock COMPARING ALL FIELDS.

    ENDIF.

    IF gt_matbal_np_stock[] IS NOT INITIAL.

      "Fetch ECC RFC destination
      CALL FUNCTION 'ZAPO_GET_RFC'
        EXPORTING
          iv_system             = 'ECC'
        TABLES
          t_rfc                 = lt_rfc
        EXCEPTIONS
          rfc_dest_not_found    = 1
          invalid_param         = 2
          no_data_found         = 3
          own_logical_not_found = 4
          OTHERS                = 5.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        READ TABLE lt_rfc INTO lw_rfc INDEX 1.
      ENDIF.

      IF lw_rfc-rfcdest IS NOT INITIAL.
        CALL FUNCTION 'Z_APO_MATBAL_NP_STOCK' DESTINATION lw_rfc-rfcdest    "'RD2CLNT212'
          EXPORTING
            it_matbal_np_stock = gt_matbal_np_stock
            iv_zplgclass       = ev_zplgclass
            iv_nonprime        = ev_nonprime
          IMPORTING
            et_matbal_np_stock = gt_matbal_np_stock_clabs.

        IF gt_matbal_np_stock_clabs[] IS NOT INITIAL.
          SORT gt_matbal_np_stock_clabs BY div  matnr supply_plant cal_month.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_matbal_hrd>.
          LOOP AT gt_matbal_hrd ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_hrd>.

            UNASSIGN <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs>.
            READ TABLE gt_matbal_np_stock_clabs ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs> WITH KEY div          = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-div
                                                                                               matnr        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-matnr
                                                                                               supply_plant = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-supply_plant
                                                                                               cal_month    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-cal_month BINARY SEARCH.
            IF <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs> IS ASSIGNED.
              IF <lfs_matbal_hrd>-meins = 'KG'.
                <lfs_matbal_hrd>-nonprime_stock = <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs>-clabs / 1000.
              ELSE.
                <lfs_matbal_hrd>-nonprime_stock = <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs>-clabs.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.

          ENDLOOP.

          ASSIGN lo_outtab->* TO <lfs_table>.
          IF <lfs_table> IS NOT INITIAL.
            LOOP AT gt_matbal_np_stock_clabs ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs>.

              READ TABLE <lfs_table> ASSIGNING <lfs_final> WITH KEY ('DIV')          = <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs>-div
                                                                    ('MATNR')        = <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs>-matnr
                                                                    ('SUPPLY_PLANT') = <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs>-supply_plant
                                                                    ('CAL_MTH')    = <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs>-cal_month.
              IF <lfs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
                UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
                ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
                IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
                  ls_fin_qty = <lfs_comp>.
                ENDIF.
                ASSIGN COMPONENT 'MEINS' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lv_meins>.
                IF <lv_meins> = 'KG'.
                  ls_fin_qty-nonprime_stock = <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs>-clabs / 1000.
                ELSE.
                  ls_fin_qty-nonprime_stock = <lfs_matbal_np_stock_clabs>-clabs.
                ENDIF.
                UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
                ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
                IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
                  <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
                ENDIF.
              ENDIF.
            ENDLOOP.
          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "get_non_prime_stock

  METHOD fetch_log_data.

*    fetch material master detail from log & pass it to ECC
*    to find out the material whose material type = ZFRT
    me->fetch_material_master_details( ).

*    fetch customer, plant or depo details of the selected log
    me->fetch_log_location( ).

*    fetch demand & supplied quantites for the customer
    me->fetch_demand_details( ).

*    fetch ET_ARCMAT TLane Details
    me->fetch_tlane_details( ).

*    fetch IT_ARCMAT TLane Details
    me->fetch_stock_tlane_details( ).

*    fetch opening & closing stock
    me->fetch_stock_details( ).

*    fetch production qty of a material at plant
    me->fetch_production_qty( ).

** BOC Azure 20231
*    Fetch location Data based on KNA1 and KNVV
    me->fetch_loc_detail_customer( ).
** EOC Azure 20231
  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_log_data

  METHOD fetch_pb_data.

    DATA:
      lt_pb_data   TYPE TABLE OF gty_pb_data,
      ltr_div      TYPE RANGE OF /bic/oip_div,
      lv_do_not_fetch TYPE boolean,
      ls_text TYPE char255.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_param_pb> TYPE gty_param_pb,
      <lfs_r_div> LIKE LINE OF ltr_div.

*    set planning book start & end date
    me->set_pb_start_n_end_date( ).

    LOOP AT gt_param_pb ASSIGNING <lfs_param_pb>.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_r_div>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO ltr_div ASSIGNING <lfs_r_div>.
      IF <lfs_r_div> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_r_div>-sign    = 'I'.
        <lfs_r_div>-option  = 'EQ'.
        <lfs_r_div>-low     = <lfs_param_pb>-div.
      ENDIF.

      AT END OF dv.

        gv_pb = <lfs_param_pb>-pb.
        gv_dv = <lfs_param_pb>-dv.
        gv_pv = '000'.

        gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.
        ls_text = 'Fetching Planning Book Data -'(013) && gv_pb.
        cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
          EXPORTING
            i_text               = ls_text    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
            i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
            i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
            i_output_immediately = abap_true
        ).

        APPEND <lfs_param_pb>-key_fig TO gt_kef.

        me->fill_pb_sel_criteria(
          EXPORTING
            it_filter_div   = ltr_div
          IMPORTING
            ev_do_not_fetch = lv_do_not_fetch
        ).

        IF lv_do_not_fetch = abap_true.
          CLEAR: ltr_div, gt_sel, gt_grp, gt_kef, gt_ret, lv_do_not_fetch.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        me->read_planning_book(
          EXPORTING
            iv_fetch_no_zero = abap_false
          IMPORTING
            et_pb_data    = lt_pb_data
            et_return     = gt_ret
        ).

        APPEND LINES OF lt_pb_data TO gt_pb_data.

        CLEAR: ltr_div, gt_sel, gt_grp, gt_kef, gt_ret, lv_do_not_fetch.

      ENDAT.
    ENDLOOP.

*    SORT gt_pb_data BY locno matnr div planshp ASCENDING kf_bgbh DESCENDING.
    gt_pb_data_all = gt_pb_data.
    SORT gt_pb_data BY matnr planshp ASCENDING kf_bgbh DESCENDING.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_pb_data COMPARING matnr planshp.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_pb_data

  METHOD set_planning_book.

    DATA:
      lt_str_div      TYPE stringtab,
      lt_tmp_param_pb TYPE TABLE OF gty_param_pb.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_str>       TYPE string,
      <lfs_param>     TYPE zapoparam,
      <lfs_param_pb>  TYPE gty_param_pb.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_param>.
    LOOP AT gt_param ASSIGNING <lfs_param> WHERE param3 = 'DIV_PB_DV_KF'.
      CLEAR lt_str_div.
      SPLIT <lfs_param>-value4 AT ',' INTO TABLE lt_str_div.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_str>.
      LOOP AT lt_str_div ASSIGNING <lfs_str>.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_param_pb>.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_param_pb ASSIGNING <lfs_param_pb>.
        IF <lfs_param_pb> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_param_pb>-pb = <lfs_param>-value1.
          <lfs_param_pb>-dv = <lfs_param>-value2.
          <lfs_param_pb>-div = <lfs_str>.
          <lfs_param_pb>-key_fig = <lfs_param>-value3.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.

    IF gt_param_pb IS NOT INITIAL.
      DELETE gt_param_pb WHERE div NOT IN s_div[].
      SORT gt_param_pb BY pb dv key_fig div.
    ELSE.
      MESSAGE 'Please maintain Planning Book & Data View in Param Table'(014) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.

    lt_tmp_param_pb = gt_param_pb.
    SORT lt_tmp_param_pb BY pb dv.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_tmp_param_pb COMPARING pb dv.
    gv_total_steps = gv_total_steps + lines( lt_tmp_param_pb ).

  ENDMETHOD.                    "set_planning_book

  METHOD set_pb_start_n_end_date.

    DATA:
      lv_n_bkt          TYPE i,
      lv_n_year         TYPE i,
      lv_n_month        TYPE i,
      lv_op_log_st_date TYPE d,
      lv_log_st_date    TYPE d,
      lv_log_ed_date    TYPE d,
      lv_date_from 	    TYPE d,
      lv_date_to 	      TYPE d.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_bkt> TYPE gty_bucket.

    lv_op_log_st_date = gs_snpop_log_info-sdate.

    lv_op_log_st_date+6(2) = '01'.

*    reading start date from log
    READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bkt> INDEX 1.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      lv_log_st_date = <lfs_bkt>-datefr.
    ENDIF.

*    reading end date from log
    lv_n_bkt = lines( gt_buckets ).
    READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bkt> INDEX lv_n_bkt.
    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_bkt> IS ASSIGNED.
      lv_log_ed_date = <lfs_bkt>-dateto.
    ENDIF.

    lv_n_year   = lv_log_ed_date+0(4) - lv_log_st_date+0(4).
    lv_n_month  = lv_log_ed_date+4(2) - lv_log_st_date+4(2).
    IF lv_n_month < 0.
      lv_n_month = 12 + lv_n_month.
    ENDIF.

    lv_n_month  = lv_n_year * 12 + lv_n_month.

    lv_date_from = lv_op_log_st_date+0(4) && lv_log_st_date+4(4).

    CALL FUNCTION 'CNV_MBT_ODA_ADD_MONTH_TO_DATE'
      EXPORTING
        months  = lv_n_month
        olddate = lv_date_from
      IMPORTING
        newdate = lv_date_to.

    CALL FUNCTION 'SLS_MISC_GET_LAST_DAY_OF_MONTH'
      EXPORTING
        day_in            = lv_date_to
      IMPORTING
        last_day_of_month = lv_date_to
      EXCEPTIONS
        day_in_not_valid  = 1
        OTHERS            = 2.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gv_pb_date_to    = lv_date_to.
    ENDIF.

    gv_pb_date_from  = lv_date_from.

    lv_n_year   = lv_log_st_date+0(4) - lv_op_log_st_date+0(4).
    lv_n_month  = lv_log_st_date+4(2) - lv_op_log_st_date+4(2).
    IF lv_n_month < 0.
      lv_n_month = 12 + lv_n_month.
    ELSE.
      lv_n_month  = lv_n_year * 12 + lv_n_month.
    ENDIF.

*    gv_pb_mth_diff   = -1 * lv_n_month.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "set_pb_start_n_end_date

  METHOD fill_pb_sel_criteria.

    DATA:
      lt_ecc_mat_master TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_mat_master,
      lt_input_dmd      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_input_demand.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_sel>     TYPE bapi10030pbselection,
      <lfs_ecc_mat>  TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master,
      <lfs_demand>  TYPE gty_input_demand.

    lt_ecc_mat_master = gt_ecc_mat_master.
    lt_input_dmd      = gt_input_dmd.

    SORT lt_ecc_mat_master  BY matnr.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_ecc_mat_master COMPARING matnr.

    IF it_filter_div IS NOT INITIAL.
      DELETE lt_ecc_mat_master WHERE spart NOT IN it_filter_div.
      DELETE lt_input_dmd WHERE div NOT IN it_filter_div.
    ENDIF.

    IF lt_input_dmd IS INITIAL AND lt_ecc_mat_master IS INITIAL.
      ev_do_not_fetch =  abap_true.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

***    "BOC by Ravi M on 14.09.2021 for Tr-AD1K915075
***    LOOP AT lt_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_mat>.
***      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_sel ASSIGNING <lfs_sel>.
***      IF <lfs_sel> IS ASSIGNED.
***        <lfs_sel>-characteristic_name = '9AMATNR'.
***        <lfs_sel>-char_val_sign       = 'I'.
***        <lfs_sel>-char_val_option     = 'EQ'.
***        <lfs_sel>-char_val_low        = <lfs_ecc_mat>-matnr.
***      ENDIF.
***    ENDLOOP.
***    "EOC by Ravi M on 14.09.2021 for Tr-AD1K915075

    SORT lt_input_dmd BY locno.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_input_dmd COMPARING locno.
    LOOP AT lt_input_dmd ASSIGNING <lfs_demand>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_sel ASSIGNING <lfs_sel>.
      IF <lfs_sel> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_sel>-characteristic_name = 'P_PLANSHP'.            "'9ALOCNO'.
        <lfs_sel>-char_val_sign       = 'I'.
        <lfs_sel>-char_val_option     = 'EQ'.
        <lfs_sel>-char_val_low        = <lfs_demand>-locno.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    LOOP AT it_filter_div INTO s_div.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_sel ASSIGNING <lfs_sel>.
      IF <lfs_sel> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_sel>-characteristic_name = 'P_DIV'.
        <lfs_sel>-char_val_sign       = 'I'.
        <lfs_sel>-char_val_option     = 'EQ'.
        <lfs_sel>-char_val_low        = s_div-low.
        <lfs_sel>-char_val_high       = s_div-high.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    APPEND '9AMATNR' TO gt_grp.
    APPEND '9ALOCNO' TO gt_grp.
    APPEND 'P_PLANSHP' TO gt_grp.
    APPEND 'P_DIV' TO gt_grp.
    APPEND 'P_PLANSHP' TO gt_grp.
    APPEND 'P_REG_OFF' TO gt_grp.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fill_pb_sel_criteria

  METHOD read_planning_book.

    DATA :
      lo_pb      TYPE REF TO zcl_pb_helper,
      lv_err_msg TYPE string,
      lt_datatab TYPE REF TO data.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_datatab> TYPE SORTED TABLE.

    IF iv_fetch_no_zero = abap_true.
      gs_read_options-no_initial_values_flag =
      gs_read_options-no_zero_values_flag =  abap_true.
    ELSEIF iv_fetch_no_zero = abap_false.
      gs_read_options-no_initial_values_flag =
    gs_read_options-no_zero_values_flag =  abap_false.
    ENDIF.

*    crating reference variable
    lo_pb = zcl_pb_helper=>factory( ).
*    calling method to fetch planning book data
    CALL METHOD lo_pb->fetch_pb_data
      EXPORTING
        iv_planningbook         = gv_pb
        iv_dataview             = gv_dv
        iv_planningversion      = gv_pv
        iv_period_type          = gv_period
        iv_date_from            = gv_pb_date_from      " Bucket ka month start date
        iv_date_to              = gv_pb_date_to        " Bucket ka month end date
        is_read_options         = gs_read_options
        it_selection            = gt_sel
        it_group_by             = gt_grp
        it_key_figure_selection = gt_kef
      IMPORTING
        et_return               = et_return
        eo_datatab              = lt_datatab.
    READ TABLE gt_ret TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
      WITH KEY type = 'E'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      lv_err_msg = 'Error in Reading Planning book:'(015) && gv_pb.
      MESSAGE lv_err_msg TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
*      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ELSE.
      ASSIGN lt_datatab->* TO <lfs_datatab>.
      IF <lfs_datatab> IS ASSIGNED.
        et_pb_data = <lfs_datatab>.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "read_planning_book

  METHOD calulate_mat_bal_demand.

    DATA(lv_is_mock_mode) = xsdbool( c_dev = abap_true AND p_orun CP 'TEST*' ).

    me->calculate_inp_dmd( ).
    IF lv_is_mock_mode = abap_false.
      me->fetch_pb_data( ).
    ENDIF.
