    me->calc_spld_dmn_cust_loc_wise( ).
    me->calculate_prod_qty( ).
    me->demand_location_details( ).
    me->find_stk_txfr_tlanes( ).
    me->find_mat_prod_plant( ).

  ENDMETHOD.                    "calulate_mat_bal_demand

  METHOD find_mat_prod_plant.

    DATA:
      lv_matnr TYPE /sapapo/matnr,
      lv_locno TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      ls_mat_prod_plant TYPE gty_mat_prod_plant,
      lt_snpoppmo    TYPE TABLE OF gty_snpoppmo.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_matkey> TYPE gty_matkey,
      <lfs_loc> TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_ppmo> TYPE gty_snpoppmo,
      <lfs_mat_prod_plant> TYPE gty_mat_prod_plant.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Finding Material Production Plant List...'(001)    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    lt_snpoppmo = gt_snpoppmo.
    SORT lt_snpoppmo BY locid matid.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_snpoppmo COMPARING locid matid.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_ppmo>.
    LOOP AT lt_snpoppmo ASSIGNING <lfs_ppmo>.

      CLEAR: lv_matnr, lv_locno.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_ppmo>-matid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
      READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_ppmo>-locid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_locno = <lfs_loc>-locno.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_mat_prod_plant>.
      READ TABLE gt_mat_prod_plant ASSIGNING <lfs_mat_prod_plant>
        WITH TABLE KEY matnr = lv_matnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_mat_prod_plant> IS ASSIGNED.
        CONCATENATE <lfs_mat_prod_plant>-plant_list lv_locno INTO <lfs_mat_prod_plant>-plant_list SEPARATED BY ','.
      ELSE.
        ls_mat_prod_plant-matnr = lv_matnr.
        ls_mat_prod_plant-plant_list = lv_locno.
        INSERT ls_mat_prod_plant INTO TABLE gt_mat_prod_plant.
        CLEAR ls_mat_prod_plant.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

** BOC Azure 20231 - populate plant list for from marc Table
    CLEAR: ls_mat_prod_plant .
    DATA: lv_werks TYPE string.
    FIELD-SYMBOLS:<lfs_marc> TYPE gty_ecc_marc.
    SORT gt_marc BY matnr werks.
    LOOP AT gt_marc ASSIGNING <lfs_marc>.
      READ TABLE  gt_mat_prod_plant WITH KEY matnr = <lfs_marc>-matnr TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        CONTINUE.
      ELSE.
        IF lv_werks IS INITIAL.
          lv_werks = <lfs_marc>-werks.
        ELSE.
          lv_werks = lv_werks && ',' && <lfs_marc>-werks.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      AT END OF matnr.
        ls_mat_prod_plant-matnr = <lfs_marc>-matnr.
        ls_mat_prod_plant-plant_list = lv_werks.
        INSERT ls_mat_prod_plant INTO TABLE gt_mat_prod_plant.
        CLEAR: lv_werks.
      ENDAT.
    ENDLOOP.
** EOC Azure 20231

  ENDMETHOD.                    "find_mat_prod_plant

**  collect input demand based on month
  METHOD calculate_inp_dmd.

    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF lty_cust_month_count,
        customer    TYPE /sapapo/locno,
        matnr       TYPE /sapapo/matnr,
        count       TYPE i,
        lt_buckets  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_bucket WITH DEFAULT KEY,
      END OF  lty_cust_month_count.

    DATA:
      lv_matnr            TYPE /sapapo/matnr,
      lv_locno            TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      lv_calmth           TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      lv_n_month          TYPE i,
      ls_inp_dmd          TYPE gty_input_demand,
      ls_cust_month_count TYPE lty_cust_month_count,
      lt_cust_month_count TYPE SORTED TABLE OF lty_cust_month_count
        WITH UNIQUE KEY customer matnr.


    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_cust_month_count>  TYPE lty_cust_month_count,
      <lfs_bucket>            TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_ecc_mat>           TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master,
      <lfs_dmd>               TYPE gty_snpopdmd,
      <lfs_dmd_upd>           TYPE gty_input_demand,
      <lfs_matkey>            TYPE gty_matkey,
      <lfs_loc>               TYPE gty_loc.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Calculating Monthly Input Demand...'(007)    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    SORT gt_ecc_mat_master BY matnr.

*    processing ET_DEMAND to get the demand qty of customer by material & month
    UNASSIGN <lfs_dmd>.
    LOOP AT gt_snpopdmd ASSIGNING <lfs_dmd>.
      CLEAR: lv_matnr, lv_locno.

*      get matnr from its guid
      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_dmd>-matid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

*      get locno from its guid
      UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
      READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_dmd>-locid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_locno = <lfs_loc>-locno.
      ENDIF.

*      get month from bucket
      UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
      CLEAR lv_calmth.
      LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket> WHERE bucke <= <lfs_dmd>-bucke AND bucke_to >= <lfs_dmd>-bucke.
        lv_calmth = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
      ENDLOOP.

*      add demand to the table month wise
      UNASSIGN <lfs_dmd_upd>.
      READ TABLE gt_input_dmd ASSIGNING <lfs_dmd_upd>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          matnr     = lv_matnr
          locno     = lv_locno
          month     = lv_calmth.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_dmd_upd> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_dmd_upd>-deman = <lfs_dmd_upd>-deman +  <lfs_dmd>-deman.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR ls_inp_dmd.
        ls_inp_dmd-sessionid = <lfs_dmd>-sessionid.
        ls_inp_dmd-line_no = <lfs_dmd>-line_no.
        ls_inp_dmd-locid = <lfs_dmd>-locid.
        ls_inp_dmd-matid = <lfs_dmd>-matid.
        ls_inp_dmd-bucke = <lfs_dmd>-bucke.
        ls_inp_dmd-deman = <lfs_dmd>-deman.
        ls_inp_dmd-month = lv_calmth.
        ls_inp_dmd-matnr = lv_matnr.
        ls_inp_dmd-locno  = lv_locno.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_mat>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_mat>
          WITH KEY
            matnr = lv_matnr
          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_mat> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_inp_dmd-div = <lfs_ecc_mat>-spart.
        ENDIF.

        INSERT ls_inp_dmd INTO TABLE gt_input_dmd.

*        adding customer & material to a table for getting the month where there
*        is no demand
        UNASSIGN <lfs_cust_month_count>.
        READ TABLE lt_cust_month_count ASSIGNING <lfs_cust_month_count>
         WITH TABLE KEY
           customer = lv_locno
           matnr = lv_matnr.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_cust_month_count> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_cust_month_count>-count = <lfs_cust_month_count>-count + 1.
*          delete the month where there is demand for the customer
          DELETE <lfs_cust_month_count>-lt_buckets WHERE cal_month = lv_calmth.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR ls_cust_month_count .
          ls_cust_month_count-customer = lv_locno.
          ls_cust_month_count-matnr = lv_matnr.
          ls_cust_month_count-count = 1.
          ls_cust_month_count-lt_buckets = gt_buckets.
          DELETE ls_cust_month_count-lt_buckets WHERE cal_month = lv_calmth.
          INSERT ls_cust_month_count  INTO TABLE lt_cust_month_count.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    DESCRIBE TABLE gt_buckets LINES lv_n_month.
*    deteleting those customers where there is demand in all months
    DELETE lt_cust_month_count WHERE count = lv_n_month.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_cust_month_count>.
*    processing customer where the is not demand in all the months
    LOOP AT lt_cust_month_count ASSIGNING <lfs_cust_month_count>.
*      looping over the months where there is no demand for the customer
      UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
      LOOP AT <lfs_cust_month_count>-lt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket>.
*        checking whether the customer exists in the demand if not then add
*        the customer with the 0 demand in the no demand month
        UNASSIGN <lfs_dmd_upd>.
        READ TABLE gt_input_dmd TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr     = <lfs_cust_month_count>-matnr
            locno     = <lfs_cust_month_count>-customer
            month     = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_dmd_upd>.
          READ TABLE gt_input_dmd ASSIGNING <lfs_dmd_upd>
            WITH TABLE KEY skey COMPONENTS
              matnr     = <lfs_cust_month_count>-matnr
              locno     = <lfs_cust_month_count>-customer.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_dmd_upd> IS ASSIGNED.
            CLEAR ls_inp_dmd.
            ls_inp_dmd = <lfs_dmd_upd>.
            CLEAR: ls_inp_dmd-deman, ls_inp_dmd-line_no, ls_inp_dmd-bucke.
            ls_inp_dmd-month = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
            INSERT ls_inp_dmd INTO TABLE gt_input_dmd.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "CALCULATE_INP_DMD

**  collect output demand based on customer & month
  METHOD calc_spld_dmn_cust_wise.

    DATA :
      lv_matnr      TYPE /sapapo/matnr,
*      lv_sub_matnr  TYPE /sapapo/matnr,
      lv_locno      TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      lv_calmth     TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      ls_supply_dmn TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_ttl,
      ls_out_dmd    TYPE gty_output_demand.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_dmn>         TYPE gty_snpopdmn,
      <lfs_supply_dmn>  TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_ttl,
      <lfs_matkey>      TYPE gty_matkey,
      <lfs_loc>         TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_bucket>      TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_out_dmd>     TYPE gty_output_demand,
      <lfs_ecc_mat>     TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Calculating Monthly Supplied Qty Customer Wise...'(008)    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

*    processing IT_DEMAND to get the demand qty of customer by material & month
    UNASSIGN <lfs_dmn>.
    LOOP AT gt_snpopdmn ASSIGNING <lfs_dmn>.
      CLEAR: lv_matnr, lv_locno, lv_calmth.
*      CLEAR: lv_matnr, lv_sub_matnr, lv_locno, lv_calmth.
*      get material from its guid
      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_dmn>-matid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
*      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
*      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
*        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_dmn>-matdl.
*      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
*        lv_sub_matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
*      ENDIF.

*      get locno from its guid
      UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
      READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_dmn>-locid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND  <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_locno = <lfs_loc>-locno.
      ENDIF.

*      get month from bucket
      UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket> .
      LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket> WHERE bucke <= <lfs_dmn>-bucke AND bucke_to >= <lfs_dmn>-bucke.
        lv_calmth = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
      ENDLOOP.

*      do subtotal for the supplied qty of material and customer month wise
      UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_dmn>.
      READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_ttl ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_dmn>
      WITH TABLE KEY
        matnr     = lv_matnr
*        sub_matnr = lv_sub_matnr
        locno     = lv_locno
        month     = lv_calmth.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_dmn> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_supply_dmn>-deliv = <lfs_supply_dmn>-deliv +  <lfs_dmn>-deliv.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR ls_supply_dmn.
        ls_supply_dmn-sessionid = <lfs_dmn>-sessionid.
        ls_supply_dmn-line_no = <lfs_dmn>-line_no.
        ls_supply_dmn-locid = <lfs_dmn>-locid.
        ls_supply_dmn-matid = <lfs_dmn>-matid.
        ls_supply_dmn-bucke = <lfs_dmn>-bucke.
        ls_supply_dmn-deliv = <lfs_dmn>-deliv.
        ls_supply_dmn-month = lv_calmth.
        ls_supply_dmn-matnr = lv_matnr.
*        ls_supply_dmn-sub_matnr = lv_sub_matnr.
        ls_supply_dmn-locno = lv_locno.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_mat>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_mat>
          WITH KEY
            matnr = lv_matnr
*            matnr = lv_sub_matnr
          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          ls_supply_dmn-div = <lfs_ecc_mat>-spart.
        ENDIF.
        INSERT ls_supply_dmn INTO TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_ttl.
      ENDIF.

*      addind the supplied qty of the material to customer month wise
      UNASSIGN <lfs_out_dmd>.
      READ TABLE gt_output_dmd ASSIGNING <lfs_out_dmd>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          matnr     = lv_matnr
*          sub_matnr = lv_sub_matnr
          locno     = lv_locno
          month     = lv_calmth.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_out_dmd> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_out_dmd>-deliv = <lfs_out_dmd>-deliv +  <lfs_dmn>-deliv.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR ls_out_dmd.
        ls_out_dmd-sessionid = <lfs_dmn>-sessionid.
        ls_out_dmd-line_no = <lfs_dmn>-line_no.
        ls_out_dmd-locid = <lfs_dmn>-locid.
        ls_out_dmd-matid = <lfs_dmn>-matid.
        ls_out_dmd-bucke = <lfs_dmn>-bucke.
        ls_out_dmd-deliv = <lfs_dmn>-deliv.
        ls_out_dmd-month = lv_calmth.
        ls_out_dmd-matnr = lv_matnr.
*        ls_out_dmd-sub_matnr = lv_sub_matnr.
        ls_out_dmd-locno  = lv_locno.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_mat>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_mat>
          WITH KEY
            matnr = lv_matnr
*            matnr = lv_sub_matnr
          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_mat> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_out_dmd-div = <lfs_ecc_mat>-spart.
        ENDIF.
        INSERT ls_out_dmd INTO TABLE gt_output_dmd.
      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "calc_spld_dmn_cust_wise

**  collect output demand based on loc, customer & month
  METHOD calc_spld_dmn_cust_loc_wise.

    DATA :
      lv_bucket         TYPE /sapapo/snpbucke ##needed,
      lv_cal_month      TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      lv_matnr          TYPE /sapapo/matnr,
      lv_locno_fr       TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      lv_locid_fr       TYPE /sapapo/locid,
      lv_locid_to       TYPE /sapapo/locid,
      lv_loctype_fr     TYPE /sapapo/c_loctype,
      lv_loctype_to     TYPE /sapapo/c_loctype,
      lv_locno_to       TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      ls_depo_supply    TYPE gty_depo_supply,
      ls_captive_qty    TYPE gty_captive_qty,
      ls_sply_cust_loc  TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_parm>          TYPE gty_snpoparm,
      <lfs_piar>          TYPE gty_snpopiar,
      <lfs_loc>           TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_bucket>        TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_dmd_cust_loc>  TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_depo_supply>   TYPE gty_depo_supply,
      <lfs_captive_qty>   TYPE gty_captive_qty,
      <lfs_matkey>        TYPE gty_matkey.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Calculating Monthly Supplied Qty Customer & Supplied Location Wise...'(009)    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

*    processing IT_ARCMAT table for the supplied qty, based on the location transfer
    UNASSIGN <lfs_parm>.
    LOOP AT gt_snpoparm_stock ASSIGNING <lfs_parm>.

      CLEAR :lv_bucket,lv_cal_month, lv_matnr, lv_locno_fr, lv_locid_fr, lv_locid_to,lv_locno_to, lv_loctype_fr, lv_loctype_to.

      " read matkey for matnr
      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_parm>-matid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_matnr     = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

*      get bucket & month from the days since 0
      UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
      LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket> WHERE valfr <= <lfs_parm>-valfr AND valto >= <lfs_parm>-valfr.
        lv_bucket    = <lfs_bucket>-bucke.
        lv_cal_month = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
      ENDLOOP.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_piar>.
      READ TABLE gt_snpopiar_stock ASSIGNING <lfs_piar>
       WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
         arcid = <lfs_parm>-arcid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_piar> IS ASSIGNED.


        " read location for locfr and loctp
        UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
        CLEAR lv_locno_fr.
        READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_piar>-locidfr.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
          lv_locid_fr = <lfs_loc>-locid.
          lv_locno_fr     = <lfs_loc>-locno.
          lv_loctype_fr  = <lfs_loc>-loctype.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
        CLEAR lv_locno_to.
        READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_piar>-locidto.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
          lv_locid_to = <lfs_loc>-locid.
          lv_locno_to     = <lfs_loc>-locno.
          lv_loctype_to = <lfs_loc>-loctype.

*          IF lv_loctype_fr = gc_loctyp_plant AND lv_loctype_to = gc_loctyp_depo.
*            add the plant to depo supply transfer qty depot, material & month wise
          UNASSIGN <lfs_depo_supply>.
          READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo_supply>
            WITH TABLE KEY
              depot = <lfs_loc>-locno
              matnr = lv_matnr
              month = lv_cal_month.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_depo_supply> IS ASSIGNED.
            <lfs_depo_supply>-qty = <lfs_depo_supply>-qty + <lfs_parm>-trans.
          ELSE.
            CLEAR ls_depo_supply.
            ls_depo_supply-depot  = <lfs_loc>-locno.
            ls_depo_supply-matnr  = lv_matnr.
            ls_depo_supply-month  = lv_cal_month.
            ls_depo_supply-qty    = <lfs_parm>-trans.
            INSERT ls_depo_supply INTO TABLE gt_depo_supply.
          ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        IF lv_loctype_fr = gc_loctyp_plant AND lv_loctype_to = gc_loctyp_plant.
*          adding the captive demand( plant to plant transert) qty plant,
*          material & month wise
          UNASSIGN <lfs_captive_qty>.
          READ TABLE gt_captive_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_captive_qty>
            WITH TABLE KEY
              plant = lv_locno_to
              matnr = lv_matnr
              month = lv_cal_month.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_captive_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
            <lfs_captive_qty>-d_qty = <lfs_captive_qty>-d_qty + <lfs_parm>-trans.
            <lfs_captive_qty>-s_qty = <lfs_captive_qty>-s_qty + <lfs_parm>-trans.
          ELSE.
            CLEAR ls_captive_qty.
            ls_captive_qty-plant = lv_locno_to.
            ls_captive_qty-matnr = lv_matnr.
            ls_captive_qty-month = lv_cal_month.
            ls_captive_qty-s_qty = ls_captive_qty-d_qty = <lfs_parm>-trans.
            INSERT ls_captive_qty INTO TABLE gt_captive_qty.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*        add the total qty supplied using the particular tlane month wise
        UNASSIGN <lfs_dmd_cust_loc>.
        READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_dmd_cust_loc>
          WITH TABLE KEY
           matnr      = lv_matnr
           locno_fr   = lv_locno_fr
           locno_to   = lv_locno_to
           cal_month  = lv_cal_month.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_dmd_cust_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_dmd_cust_loc>-trans = <lfs_dmd_cust_loc>-trans + <lfs_parm>-trans.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR ls_sply_cust_loc.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-sessionid = <lfs_parm>-sessionid.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-arcid     = <lfs_parm>-arcid.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-matid     = <lfs_parm>-matid.

          ls_sply_cust_loc-matnr     = lv_matnr.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-locid_fr  = lv_locid_fr.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-locno_fr  = lv_locno_fr.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-loctyp_fr  = lv_loctype_fr.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-locid_to  = lv_locid_to.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-locno_to  = lv_locno_to.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-loctyp_to  = lv_loctype_to.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-cal_month = lv_cal_month.

          ls_sply_cust_loc-valto = <lfs_parm>-valto.
          ls_sply_cust_loc-valfr = <lfs_parm>-valfr.

          ls_sply_cust_loc-trans = <lfs_parm>-trans.
        ENDIF.
        INSERT ls_sply_cust_loc INTO TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "calc_spld_dmn_cust_loc_wise

*  calculate production qty based on month
  METHOD calculate_prod_qty.

    DATA :
      lv_bucket     TYPE /sapapo/snpbucke ##needed,
      lv_cal_month  TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      lv_matnr      TYPE /sapapo/matnr,
      lv_locno      TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      ls_prod_quant TYPE gty_prod_quant,
      lv_tabix_ppma TYPE sytabix,
      lv_valfr      TYPE i,
      lv_ioind      TYPE char2,
      lv_outin      TYPE /sapapo/snpoutin.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
*      <lfs_ppmo>        TYPE gty_snpoppmo,
      <lfs_ppro>        TYPE gty_snpoppro,
      <lfs_bucket>      TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_ppma>        TYPE gty_snpoppma,
      <lfs_matkey>      TYPE gty_matkey,
      <lfs_loc>         TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_prod_quant>  TYPE gty_prod_quant,
*      <lfs_iek>         TYPE /sapapo/snpopiek.,
      <lfs_iek>         TYPE gty_snpopiek.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Calculating Monthly Production Quantity...'(011)    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    SORT :
*      gt_snpoppma BY proid matid locid,
      gt_snpoppma BY proid,
      gt_snpoppmo BY proid.

*    processing to the production qty of the material month wise
    UNASSIGN <lfs_ppro>.
    LOOP AT gt_snpoppro ASSIGNING <lfs_ppro>.
*      UNASSIGN <lfs_ppmo>.
*      READ TABLE gt_snpoppmo ASSIGNING <lfs_ppmo>
*        WITH KEY
*          proid  = <lfs_ppro>-proid
*        BINARY SEARCH.
*      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ppmo> IS ASSIGNED.

*        CLEAR: lv_matnr, lv_locno.
*        UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
*        READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
*          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_ppmo>-matid.
*        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
*          lv_matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
*        ELSE.
*          CONTINUE.
*        ENDIF.
*
*        UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
*        READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
*          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_ppmo>-locid.
*        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
*          lv_locno = <lfs_loc>-locno.
*        ENDIF.

      lv_valfr = <lfs_ppro>-valfr.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
      LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket> WHERE valfr <= lv_valfr AND valto >= lv_valfr.
        lv_bucket    = <lfs_bucket>-bucke.
        lv_cal_month = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
      ENDLOOP.

      READ TABLE gt_snpoppma TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
        WITH KEY
          proid     = <lfs_ppro>-proid
*            matid     = <lfs_ppmo>-matid
*            locid     = <lfs_ppmo>-locid
        BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lv_tabix_ppma = sy-tabix.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_ppma>.
        LOOP AT gt_snpoppma ASSIGNING <lfs_ppma> FROM lv_tabix_ppma.
          IF NOT ( <lfs_ppma>-proid     = <lfs_ppro>-proid ).
*              and<lfs_ppma>-matid     = <lfs_ppmo>-matid AND  <lfs_ppma>-locid     = <lfs_ppmo>-locid ).
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
          CLEAR <lfs_ppma>-sessionid.


          CLEAR: lv_matnr, lv_locno, lv_ioind, lv_outin.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
          READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
            WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_ppma>-matid.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
            lv_matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
          ELSE.
            CONTINUE.
          ENDIF.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
          READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
            WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_ppma>-locid.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
            lv_locno = <lfs_loc>-locno.
          ENDIF.

*          UNASSIGN <lfs_ppmo>.
*          READ TABLE gt_snpoppmo ASSIGNING <lfs_ppmo>
*            WITH KEY
*              proid  = <lfs_ppro>-proid
*            BINARY SEARCH.
*          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ppmo> IS ASSIGNED.
*            lv_ioind = 'O'. "output
*          ENDIF.

          lv_outin = <lfs_ppma>-outin.
          IF lv_outin < 0.
            lv_ioind = 'I'. "fg material consumption indicator
            lv_outin = lv_outin * -1.
          ELSE.
            lv_ioind = 'O'. "output
          ENDIF.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_iek>.
          READ TABLE gt_snpopiek ASSIGNING <lfs_iek>
            WITH KEY inkey = <lfs_ppma>-proid
            BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_iek> IS ASSIGNED
             AND <lfs_iek>-exkey CP '*CONVERT*'.
            lv_ioind = 'C' && lv_ioind.
          ENDIF.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_quant>.
          READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_quant>
            WITH TABLE KEY
              matnr = lv_matnr
              io_ind = lv_ioind
              locno = lv_locno
              month = lv_cal_month.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_quant> IS ASSIGNED.
            <lfs_prod_quant>-prod_quant = <lfs_prod_quant>-prod_quant + ( <lfs_ppro>-produ * lv_outin ).
            CLEAR ls_prod_quant.
*              ls_prod_quant = <lfs_prod_quant>.
            ls_prod_quant-proid = <lfs_ppro>-proid.
            ls_prod_quant-locid = <lfs_ppma>-locid.
            ls_prod_quant-matid = <lfs_ppma>-matid.
            ls_prod_quant-matnr = lv_matnr.
            ls_prod_quant-io_ind = lv_ioind.
            ls_prod_quant-locno = lv_locno.
            ls_prod_quant-month = lv_cal_month.
            ls_prod_quant-prod_quant = <lfs_ppro>-produ * lv_outin.
            APPEND ls_prod_quant TO gt_prod_qty_test.
          ELSE.

            CLEAR ls_prod_quant.
            ls_prod_quant-sessionid = <lfs_ppma>-sessionid.
            ls_prod_quant-proid = <lfs_ppro>-proid.
            ls_prod_quant-locid = <lfs_ppma>-locid.
            ls_prod_quant-matid = <lfs_ppma>-matid.
*            ls_prod_quant-produ = <lfs_ppro>-produ.
            ls_prod_quant-matnr = lv_matnr.
            ls_prod_quant-io_ind = lv_ioind.
            ls_prod_quant-locno = lv_locno.
            ls_prod_quant-month = lv_cal_month.
*            ls_prod_quant-outin = <lfs_ppma>-outin.
*            IF <lfs_ppro>-ind_con = 'C'. "If Added on 16.08.2021
*              ls_prod_quant-prod_qty_con = <lfs_ppro>-produ * lv_outin.
*            ELSE.
            ls_prod_quant-prod_quant = <lfs_ppro>-produ * lv_outin.
*            ENDIF.

            IF <lfs_iek> IS ASSIGNED.
              ls_prod_quant-pds_name = <lfs_iek>-exkey.
            ENDIF.
            ls_prod_quant-ind_con = <lfs_ppro>-ind_con.
            INSERT ls_prod_quant INTO TABLE gt_prod_qty.
            APPEND ls_prod_quant TO gt_prod_qty_test.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    me->add_fixed_production( ).

  ENDMETHOD.                    "CALCULATE_PROD_QTY

  METHOD add_fixed_production.

    DATA:
      ls_locprod TYPE gty_locprod,
      ls_prod_quant TYPE gty_prod_quant,
      lt_emp_et_locprod_fxd_prod TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_locprod.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_bct>         TYPE gty_snpopbct,
      <lfs_bucket>      TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_lpr>         TYPE gty_snpoplpr,
      <lfs_loc>         TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_matkey>      TYPE gty_matkey,
      <lfs_prod_quant>  TYPE gty_prod_quant,
      <lfs_locprod>     TYPE gty_locprod.

    SORT gt_snpoppmo BY locid matid.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
    LOOP AT gt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr>.

      READ TABLE gt_snpoppmo TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
        WITH KEY
          locid      = <lfs_lpr>-locid
          matid      = <lfs_lpr>-matid
        BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR ls_locprod.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
      READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
        WITH KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
          locid = <lfs_lpr>-locid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
        IF NOT <lfs_loc>-loctype = gc_loctyp_plant.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.
        ls_locprod-locid      = <lfs_lpr>-locid.
        ls_locprod-locno      = <lfs_loc>-locno.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
        WITH KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_lpr>-matid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_locprod-matid      = <lfs_lpr>-matid.
        ls_locprod-matnr      = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
      LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket> WHERE bucke <= <lfs_lpr>-bucke AND bucke_to >= <lfs_lpr>-bucke.
        ls_locprod-month = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
      ENDLOOP.

      ls_locprod-fprod      = <lfs_lpr>-fprod.
      ls_locprod-flag_added = abap_false.
      COLLECT ls_locprod INTO gt_et_locprod_fxd_prod.
    ENDLOOP.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_quant>.
    LOOP AT gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_quant>.

      IF <lfs_prod_quant>-io_ind = 'I'.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_locprod>.
      READ TABLE gt_et_locprod_fxd_prod ASSIGNING <lfs_locprod>
        WITH KEY skey COMPONENTS
          matnr = <lfs_prod_quant>-matnr
          locno = <lfs_prod_quant>-locno
          month = <lfs_prod_quant>-month.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_locprod> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_prod_quant>-prod_quant = <lfs_prod_quant>-prod_quant + <lfs_locprod>-fprod.
        <lfs_locprod>-flag_added =  abap_true.
      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.

    lt_emp_et_locprod_fxd_prod = gt_et_locprod_fxd_prod.
    DELETE lt_emp_et_locprod_fxd_prod WHERE flag_added = abap_true.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_locprod>.
    LOOP AT lt_emp_et_locprod_fxd_prod ASSIGNING <lfs_locprod>.
      CLEAR ls_prod_quant.
      ls_prod_quant-locid       = <lfs_locprod>-locid.
      ls_prod_quant-matid       = <lfs_locprod>-matid.
      ls_prod_quant-matnr       = <lfs_locprod>-matnr.
      ls_prod_quant-io_ind      = 'O'.
      ls_prod_quant-locno       = <lfs_locprod>-locno.
      ls_prod_quant-month       = <lfs_locprod>-month.
      ls_prod_quant-prod_quant  = <lfs_locprod>-fprod.
      INSERT ls_prod_quant INTO TABLE gt_prod_qty.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "add_fixed_production

*  create supply network
  METHOD demand_location_details.

    DATA :
      lv_bucket       TYPE /sapapo/snpbucke ##needed,
      lv_locno        TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      lv_matnr        TYPE /sapapo/matnr,
      lv_cal_month    TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      ls_depo_supply  TYPE gty_depo_supply.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_piam>        TYPE gty_snpopiam,
      <lfs_piar>        TYPE gty_snpopiar,
      <lfs_loc>         TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_depo_supply> TYPE gty_depo_supply,
      <lfs_bucket>      TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_dmd_loc>     TYPE gty_demand_loc,
      <lfs_matkey>      TYPE gty_matkey.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Finding Input TLanes...'(012)    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

*    builind the supply network based the table ET_ARCMAT

    SORT: gt_snpopiar BY arcid.
    LOOP AT gt_snpopiam ASSIGNING <lfs_piam>.
      CLEAR: lv_bucket, lv_cal_month, lv_matnr.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_piam>-matid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_piar>.
      READ TABLE gt_snpopiar ASSIGNING <lfs_piar>
        WITH KEY
         arcid = <lfs_piam>-arcid
        BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_piar> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_dmd_loc>.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_demand_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_dmd_loc>.
        IF <lfs_dmd_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-sessionid = <lfs_piam>-sessionid.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-arcid     = <lfs_piam>-arcid.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-matid     = <lfs_piam>-matid.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-valto = <lfs_piam>-valto.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-valfr = <lfs_piam>-valfr.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-matnr = lv_matnr.
          CLEAR :lv_bucket,lv_cal_month.
          LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket> WHERE valfr <= <lfs_piam>-valfr AND valto >= <lfs_piam>-valfr.
            lv_bucket    = <lfs_bucket>-bucke.
            lv_cal_month = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
          ENDLOOP.

          <lfs_dmd_loc>-cal_month = lv_cal_month.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-locid_fr = <lfs_piar>-locidfr.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-locid_to = <lfs_piar>-locidto.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
          READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
            WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_piar>-locidfr.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
            <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_fr     = <lfs_loc>-locno.
            <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_fr = <lfs_loc>-loctype.
          ENDIF.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
          READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
            WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_piar>-locidto.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
            lv_locno = <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_to = <lfs_loc>-locno.
            <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_to = <lfs_loc>-loctype.
          ENDIF.

*          IF ( <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_plant ) AND
***          IF ( <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_plant OR <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_depo ) AND
***            ( <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_to = gc_loctyp_depo OR <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_to = gc_loctyp_customer ).

          READ TABLE gt_output_dmd TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
            WITH TABLE KEY skey COMPONENTS
              matnr = lv_matnr
              locno = <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_to.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0 AND <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_to = gc_loctyp_customer.
            UNASSIGN <lfs_depo_supply>.
            READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo_supply>
              WITH TABLE KEY
                depot = <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_to
                matnr = lv_matnr
                month = '000000'.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_depo_supply> IS ASSIGNED.
              IF <lfs_dmd_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
                READ TABLE <lfs_depo_supply>-locno_fr TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                  WITH TABLE KEY table_line = <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_fr.
                IF sy-subrc = 0.
                  CONTINUE.
                ENDIF.
              ENDIF.
              <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count = <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count + 1.
              APPEND <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_fr TO <lfs_depo_supply>-locno_fr.
            ELSE.
              CLEAR ls_depo_supply.
              ls_depo_supply-depot  = <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_to.
              APPEND <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_fr TO ls_depo_supply-locno_fr.
              ls_depo_supply-matnr  = lv_matnr.
              ls_depo_supply-month  = '000000'.
              ls_depo_supply-lane_count = 1.
              INSERT ls_depo_supply INTO TABLE gt_depo_supply.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
*            IF <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_depo and <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_.
*              lv_locno = <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_fr.
*            ENDIF.
*            if the lane is from plant to depo or customer then count the no of lanes
          UNASSIGN <lfs_depo_supply>.
          READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo_supply>
            WITH TABLE KEY
              depot = lv_locno
              matnr = lv_matnr
              month = '000000'.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_depo_supply> IS ASSIGNED.
            IF <lfs_dmd_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
              READ TABLE <lfs_depo_supply>-locno_fr TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                WITH TABLE KEY table_line = <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_fr.
              IF sy-subrc = 0.
                CONTINUE.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
            <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count = <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count + 1.
            APPEND <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_fr TO <lfs_depo_supply>-locno_fr.
          ELSE.
            CLEAR ls_depo_supply.
*              ls_depo_supply-depot  = <lfs_loc>-locno.
            ls_depo_supply-depot  = lv_locno.
*              APPEND <lfs_piar>-ttype to ls_depo_supply-means_of_xport.
            APPEND <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_fr TO ls_depo_supply-locno_fr.
            ls_depo_supply-matnr  = lv_matnr.
            ls_depo_supply-month  = '000000'.
            ls_depo_supply-lane_count = 1.
            INSERT ls_depo_supply INTO TABLE gt_depo_supply.
          ENDIF.

***          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "calculate_location_details

*  create stock transfer lanes
  METHOD find_stk_txfr_tlanes.
*    not used
    DATA :
      lv_bucket         TYPE /sapapo/snpbucke ##needed,
      lv_cal_month      TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      ls_supply_arcmat  TYPE gty_supply_arcmat.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_piam>        TYPE gty_snpoparm,
      <lfs_piar>        TYPE gty_snpopiar,
      <lfs_loc_to>      TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_loc_fr>      TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_bucket>      TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_dmd_loc>     TYPE gty_supply_arcmat,
      <lfs_matkey>      TYPE gty_matkey.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Finding Stock Transfer Lanes...'(056)    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    SORT gt_snpopiar_stock BY sessionid arcid.

    LOOP AT gt_snpoparm_stock ASSIGNING <lfs_piam>.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_piar>.
      READ TABLE gt_snpopiar_stock ASSIGNING <lfs_piar>
       WITH KEY
         sessionid  = <lfs_piam>-sessionid
         arcid      = <lfs_piam>-arcid
       BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_piar> IS ASSIGNED.

        READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_piam>-matid.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_loc_to>.
        READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_to>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_piar>-locidto.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_loc_fr>.
        READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_fr>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS locid = <lfs_piar>-locidfr.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR :lv_bucket,lv_cal_month.
        LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket> WHERE valfr <= <lfs_piam>-valfr AND valto >= <lfs_piam>-valfr.
          lv_bucket    = <lfs_bucket>-bucke.
          lv_cal_month = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
        ENDLOOP.

        READ TABLE gt_supply_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_dmd_loc>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr  = <lfs_matkey>-matnr
            locno_fr = <lfs_loc_fr>-locno
            locno_to = <lfs_loc_to>-locno
            cal_month = lv_cal_month.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_dmd_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-trans     = <lfs_dmd_loc>-trans + <lfs_piam>-trans.
        ELSE.
          ASSIGN ls_supply_arcmat TO <lfs_dmd_loc>.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-sessionid = <lfs_piam>-sessionid.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-arcid     = <lfs_piam>-arcid.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-matid     = <lfs_piam>-matid.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-trans     = <lfs_piam>-trans.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-matnr     = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-valto = <lfs_piam>-valto.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-valfr = <lfs_piam>-valfr.

          <lfs_dmd_loc>-cal_month = lv_cal_month.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-locid_fr = <lfs_piar>-locidfr.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-locid_to = <lfs_piar>-locidto.

          <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_fr     = <lfs_loc_fr>-locno.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_fr = <lfs_loc_fr>-loctype.

          <lfs_dmd_loc>-locno_to     = <lfs_loc_to>-locno.
          <lfs_dmd_loc>-loctyp_to = <lfs_loc_to>-loctype.
          INSERT <lfs_dmd_loc> INTO TABLE gt_supply_arcmat.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "find_stk_txfr_tlanes

*  create dynamic table
  METHOD create_dyn_tab.

    DATA:
      lv_msg              TYPE string,
      lo_ref_struct       TYPE REF TO data,
      lo_desc_tab         TYPE REF TO cl_abap_tabledescr,
      lo_desc_tab_sort    TYPE REF TO cl_abap_tabledescr,
      lox_struct_create   TYPE REF TO cx_sy_struct_creation,
      lox_table_create    TYPE REF TO cx_sy_table_creation,
      lox_par_inv         TYPE REF TO cx_parameter_invalid_range,
      lt_component        TYPE cl_abap_structdescr=>component_table,
      lt_keytab           TYPE abap_keydescr_tab,
      ls_fin_fields       TYPE gty_fin_fields,
      ls_fin_qty          TYPE gty_fin_qty,
      lt_reg_off          TYPE TABLE OF /bic/oip_reg_off,
      lt_tmp_matbal_item  TYPE TABLE OF zapo_matbal_item,
      lt_tmp_pb_data      TYPE TABLE OF gty_pb_data,
      lo_struct_desc      TYPE REF TO cl_abap_structdescr,
      lox_cast            TYPE REF TO cx_sy_move_cast_error,
      lv_is_mock_mode     TYPE abap_bool.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_reg>           TYPE /bic/oip_reg_off,
      <lfs_matbal_item>   TYPE zapo_matbal_item,
      <lfs_pb_data>       TYPE gty_pb_data,
      <lfs_reg_off_desc>  TYPE gty_reg_off_desc,
      <lfs_comp>          TYPE cl_abap_structdescr=>component,
      <lfs_kunnr_final>   TYPE gty_loc_det.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.
    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Creating Dynamic Table...'(078)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    lv_is_mock_mode = xsdbool( c_dev = abap_true AND p_orun CP 'TEST*' ).

    GET REFERENCE OF ls_fin_fields INTO lo_ref_struct.

    TRY .

        lo_struct_desc ?= cl_abap_tabledescr=>describe_by_data_ref( lo_ref_struct ).

        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_component ASSIGNING <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp>-name = 'STRUCT'.
          <lfs_comp>-type = lo_struct_desc.
          <lfs_comp>-as_include = abap_true.
        ENDIF.
      CATCH cx_sy_move_cast_error INTO lox_cast.
        lv_msg = lox_cast->get_text( ).
        MESSAGE lv_msg TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDTRY.

    CASE r_gen.
      WHEN abap_true.
*        get the regions from planning book
        lt_tmp_pb_data = gt_pb_data.
        SORT lt_tmp_pb_data BY reg_off.
        DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_tmp_pb_data COMPARING reg_off.
** BOC Azure 20231 - populate the REG Off value from ECC fetch values
        LOOP AT lt_tmp_pb_data ASSIGNING <lfs_pb_data>.
          APPEND <lfs_pb_data>-reg_off TO lt_reg_off.
        ENDLOOP.
        LOOP AT gt_kunnr_final ASSIGNING <lfs_kunnr_final>.
          APPEND  <lfs_kunnr_final>-vkbur TO lt_reg_off.
        ENDLOOP.
        SORT lt_reg_off .
        DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_reg_off COMPARING ALL FIELDS.
** EOC Azure 20231
        APPEND 'OTHR' TO lt_reg_off.
      WHEN abap_false.
*        get region of the stored table
        lt_tmp_matbal_item = gt_matbal_item.
        SORT lt_tmp_matbal_item BY reg_off.
        DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_tmp_matbal_item COMPARING reg_off.
        LOOP AT lt_tmp_matbal_item ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_item>.
          APPEND <lfs_matbal_item>-reg_off TO lt_reg_off.
        ENDLOOP.
*        display: if only header totals exist (e.g. PROD_QTY rows), still show OTHR columns
        IF lt_reg_off IS INITIAL AND gt_matbal_hrd IS NOT INITIAL.
          APPEND 'OTHR' TO lt_reg_off.
        ENDIF.
    ENDCASE.

*    IF lt_reg_off IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF lt_reg_off IS NOT INITIAL AND lv_is_mock_mode = abap_false.
*      get the retion desctiption
      SELECT pp~/bic/p_reg_off txtsh
        FROM /bic/pp_reg_off AS pp
        INNER JOIN /bic/tp_reg_off AS t
          ON pp~/bic/p_reg_off = t~/bic/p_reg_off AND langu = sy-langu
        INTO TABLE gt_reg_off_desc
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_reg_off
        WHERE pp~/bic/p_reg_off = lt_reg_off-table_line AND objvers = 'A'.
    ENDIF.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_reg_off_desc>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_reg_off_desc ASSIGNING <lfs_reg_off_desc>.
    IF <lfs_reg_off_desc> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_reg_off_desc>-reg_off  = 'OTHR'.
      <lfs_reg_off_desc>-txtsh    = 'Other'(070).
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_is_mock_mode = abap_true AND s_regoff[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      DELETE: lt_reg_off WHERE NOT table_line IN s_regoff[].
    ENDIF.

*    add the demand & supply qty region wise to the component table
    LOOP AT lt_reg_off ASSIGNING <lfs_reg>.

      READ TABLE gt_reg_off_desc TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
        WITH KEY reg_off = <lfs_reg>.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_reg_off_desc>.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_reg_off_desc ASSIGNING <lfs_reg_off_desc>.
        IF <lfs_reg_off_desc> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_reg_off_desc>-reg_off  =
          <lfs_reg_off_desc>-txtsh    = <lfs_reg>.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_component ASSIGNING <lfs_comp>.
      IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_comp>-name = 'D_' && <lfs_reg>.
        TRY.
            <lfs_comp>-type = cl_abap_elemdescr=>get_p(
                p_length   = 16
                p_decimals = 3
            ).
          CATCH cx_parameter_invalid_range INTO lox_par_inv.  " Parameter with Invalid Range( ).
            lv_msg = 'Add Region:'(017) && lox_par_inv->get_text( ).
            MESSAGE lv_msg TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
            LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
        ENDTRY.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_component ASSIGNING <lfs_comp>.
      IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_comp>-name = 'S_' && <lfs_reg>.
        TRY.
            <lfs_comp>-type = cl_abap_elemdescr=>get_p(
                p_length   = 16
                p_decimals = 3
            ).
          CATCH cx_parameter_invalid_range INTO lox_par_inv.  " Parameter with Invalid Range( ).
            lv_msg = 'Add Region:'(017) && lox_par_inv->get_text( ).
            MESSAGE lv_msg TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
            LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
        ENDTRY.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    FREE lo_ref_struct.
    GET REFERENCE OF ls_fin_qty INTO lo_ref_struct.

    TRY .

        lo_struct_desc ?= cl_abap_tabledescr=>describe_by_data_ref( lo_ref_struct ).

        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_component ASSIGNING <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp>-name = 'QTY'.
          <lfs_comp>-type = lo_struct_desc.
          <lfs_comp>-as_include = abap_true.
        ENDIF.
      CATCH cx_sy_move_cast_error INTO lox_cast.
        lv_msg = lox_cast->get_text( ).
        MESSAGE lv_msg TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
        go_desc_struct = cl_abap_structdescr=>create(
            p_components = lt_component
            p_strict     = abap_false
        ).

*        setting the key field for the table
        APPEND 'SRC_PLANT'    TO lt_keytab.
        APPEND 'SUPPLY_PLANT' TO lt_keytab.
        APPEND 'MATNR'        TO lt_keytab.
        APPEND 'CAL_MTH'      TO lt_keytab.

        TRY.

            lo_desc_tab = cl_abap_tabledescr=>create(
                p_line_type  = go_desc_struct
                p_table_kind = cl_abap_tabledescr=>tablekind_std
*                p_unique     = abap_true
                p_key        = lt_keytab
                p_key_kind   = cl_abap_tabledescr=>keydefkind_user
            ).

            lo_desc_tab_sort = cl_abap_tabledescr=>create(
                p_line_type  = go_desc_struct
                p_table_kind = cl_abap_tabledescr=>tablekind_sorted
                p_unique     = abap_true
                p_key        = lt_keytab
                p_key_kind   = cl_abap_tabledescr=>keydefkind_user
            ).

            CREATE DATA lo_tab_sorted TYPE HANDLE lo_desc_tab_sort.
            CREATE DATA lo_outtab TYPE HANDLE lo_desc_tab.

          CATCH cx_sy_table_creation INTO lox_table_create.
            lv_msg = lox_table_create->get_text( ).
            MESSAGE lv_msg TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
            LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
        ENDTRY.

      CATCH cx_sy_struct_creation INTO lox_struct_create.
        lv_msg = lox_struct_create->get_text( ).
        MESSAGE lv_msg TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDTRY.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "create_dyn_tab

*  populy supply qty for each region
  METHOD populate_supply.

    DATA:
      lv_loc              TYPE /sapapo/locno,
*      lv_locid            TYPE /sapapo/locid,
      lv_reg_off          TYPE /bic/oip_reg_off ##needed,
      lv_supply_dmn       TYPE /sapapo/snpdeliv,
      lv_not_lane_found   TYPE boolean,
      lv_supply_dmn1      TYPE /sapapo/snpdeliv,
      lv_n_lane          TYPE i,
      lv_clr_dmd          TYPE boolean,
      lv_tabix1           TYPE sy-tabix,
      ls_depo_supply      TYPE gty_depo_supply,
      ls_subtot           TYPE gty_subtot,
*      ls_dmd TYPE gty_input_demand,
      lv_ind TYPE char3.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_input_dmd>       TYPE gty_input_demand,
      <lfs_output_dmd>      TYPE gty_output_demand,
      <lfs_location>        TYPE gty_demand_loc,
      <lfs_location1>       TYPE gty_demand_loc,
      <lfs_location_d_src>  TYPE gty_demand_loc,
*      <lfs_lane_not_supplied>  TYPE gty_demand_loc,
      <lfs_depo_supply>     TYPE gty_depo_supply,
      <lfs_subtotal>        TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_supply_dmn>      TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_supply_cust>     TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_ttl.
*      <lfs_supply_armat>    TYPE gty_subtot,
*      <lfs_supply_src>      TYPE gty_subtot.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Populating Supply Qty...'(058)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    DATA lt_demand_loc TYPE TABLE OF gty_demand_loc ##needed.
    lt_demand_loc = gt_demand_loc.
    SORT gt_demand_loc BY matnr locno_fr locno_to cal_month.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_demand_loc COMPARING  matnr locno_fr locno_to cal_month.

    SORT:
      gt_demand_loc BY matnr locno_to.

    me->filter_dev( ).

*    DATA: lv_str TYPE string, lt_mat_cust_req TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF string.

    LOOP AT gt_input_dmd ASSIGNING <lfs_input_dmd> USING KEY primary_key.

      CLEAR lv_reg_off.
      lv_reg_off = me->get_region(
          iv_matnr  = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
          iv_cust   = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
      ).

*      CLEAR lv_str.
*      CONCATENATE <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr <lfs_input_dmd>-locno lv_reg_off INTO lv_str SEPARATED BY ','.
*      APPEND lv_str TO lt_mat_cust_req.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_output_dmd>.
*      checking for demand supplied to customer or not
      READ TABLE gt_output_dmd ASSIGNING <lfs_output_dmd>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
          locno = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
          month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_output_dmd> IS ASSIGNED.
*        if demand is supplied to the customer, the do subtotal of the
*        supplied qty region, customer, material & month wise
        lv_ind = 'SNZ'.
        me->do_subtol_for_dmd(
          EXPORTING
            iv_matnr   = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
            iv_cust    = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
            iv_locfrm  = ''
            iv_month   = <lfs_input_dmd>-month
            iv_ind     = lv_ind
            iv_dmd_qty = <lfs_input_dmd>-deman
        ).
      ELSE.
        lv_ind = 'SZ'.
      ENDIF.

*      get the tlane of the customer
      UNASSIGN <lfs_location>.
      READ TABLE gt_demand_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_location>
        WITH KEY
          matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
          locno_to = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
        BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_location> IS ASSIGNED.

        lv_tabix1 = sy-tabix.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_location>.
*        for each tlane found for the customer, populate the supplied qty
        LOOP AT  gt_demand_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_location> FROM lv_tabix1.
          IF  NOT ( <lfs_location>-matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr AND
           <lfs_location>-locno_to = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno ) .
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.

          IF lv_ind = 'SZ'.
*            if there is no demand for the customer, then subtotal the
*            unfulfilled demand based on mateiral, customer, supplied location & month wise
            me->do_subtol_for_dmd(
              EXPORTING
                iv_matnr   = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                iv_cust    = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
                iv_locfrm  = <lfs_location>-locno_fr
                iv_month   = <lfs_input_dmd>-month
                iv_ind     = lv_ind
                iv_dmd_qty = <lfs_input_dmd>-deman
            ).
          ENDIF.

          CLEAR: lv_supply_dmn, lv_not_lane_found.
*          get the supplied qty to the customer
          UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_dmn>.
          READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_dmn>
            WITH TABLE KEY
             matnr     = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
             locno_fr  = <lfs_location>-locno_fr
             locno_to  = <lfs_location>-locno_to
             cal_month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_dmn> IS ASSIGNED.
            lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans.
            <lfs_supply_dmn>-flag = abap_true.
          ELSE.
            lv_not_lane_found = abap_true.
          ENDIF.

*          based on supplied location perform the following to the the
*          from location of the supplied location
          CASE <lfs_location>-loctyp_fr.
            WHEN gc_loctyp_depo.
              UNASSIGN <lfs_location1>.
              READ TABLE gt_demand_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_location1>
                WITH KEY
                  matnr     = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                  locno_to  = <lfs_location>-locno_fr
                BINARY SEARCH.
              IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_location1> IS ASSIGNED.
                lv_tabix1 = sy-tabix.
                UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_cust>.
                READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_ttl ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_cust>
                  WITH TABLE KEY
                    matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                    locno = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
                    month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                LOOP AT gt_demand_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_location_d_src> FROM lv_tabix1.
                  IF NOT ( <lfs_location_d_src>-matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr AND
                      <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_to = <lfs_location>-locno_fr ).
                    EXIT.
                  ENDIF.

                  <lfs_location_d_src>-flag = abap_true.
                  CLEAR: lv_supply_dmn1, lv_clr_dmd.
*                  checking whetner the material has been supplied through the TLane in IT_ARCMAT
                  UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_dmn>.
                  READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_dmn>
                    WITH TABLE KEY
                     matnr     = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                     locno_fr  = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_fr
                     locno_to  = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_to
                     cal_month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                  IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_dmn> IS ASSIGNED." and lv_not_lane_found = abap_false.
                    <lfs_supply_dmn>-flag = abap_true.
                    READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo_supply>
                      WITH TABLE KEY
                        depot = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_to
                        matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                        month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_depo_supply> IS ASSIGNED.
                      IF lv_not_lane_found = abap_true.
                        IF <lfs_supply_cust> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo_supply> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count IS NOT INITIAL.
                          lv_supply_dmn1 = <lfs_supply_cust>-deliv / <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count.
                        ENDIF.
                      ELSE.
                        IF <lfs_depo_supply>-qty IS NOT INITIAL.
                          lv_supply_dmn1 = <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans * lv_supply_dmn / <lfs_depo_supply>-qty.
                        ENDIF.
                      ENDIF.
                    ENDIF.
                  ELSE.
                    lv_clr_dmd = abap_true.

*                    IF lv_not_lane_found = abap_true.
*                      IF <lfs_supply_cust> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo_supply> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count IS NOT INITIAL.
*                        lv_supply_dmn1 = <lfs_supply_cust>-deliv / <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count.
*                        CLEAR lv_clr_dmd.
*                      ENDIF.
*                    ENDIF. "cmtd mn

                    CASE lv_not_lane_found.
                      WHEN abap_true.
                        UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_dmn>.
                        READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_dmn>
                        WITH KEY skey2 COMPONENTS matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                        locno_to = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
                        cal_month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_dmn> IS ASSIGNED.
                        ELSE.
                          UNASSIGN <lfs_depo_supply>.
                          READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo_supply>
                            WITH TABLE KEY
                              depot = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
                              matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                              month = '000000'.
                          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_depo_supply> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_output_dmd> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count IS NOT INITIAL.
                            lv_supply_dmn1 = <lfs_output_dmd>-deliv / <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count.
                            CLEAR lv_clr_dmd.
                          ENDIF.
                        ENDIF.

                      WHEN abap_false.
                        lv_supply_dmn1 = <lfs_output_dmd>-deliv.
                        UNASSIGN <lfs_depo_supply>.
                        READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo_supply>
                          WITH TABLE KEY
                            depot = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_to
                            matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                            month = '000000'.
*                        IF sy-subrc = 0 and <lfs_depo_supply> is ASSIGNED and <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count is not INITIAL.
*                          lv_supply_dmn1 = <lfs_output_dmd>-deliv / <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count.
*                        ENDIF.
                        READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc  TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                          WITH TABLE KEY skey2
                            COMPONENTS
                              matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                              locno_to = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_to
                              cal_month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                        IF sy-subrc <> 0 AND <lfs_output_dmd> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo_supply> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count IS NOT INITIAL.
                          CLEAR lv_clr_dmd.
                          lv_n_lane = <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count.
                          READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_dmn>
                            WITH TABLE KEY
                              matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                              locno_fr = <lfs_location>-locno_fr
                              locno_to = <lfs_location>-locno_to
                              cal_month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_dmn> IS ASSIGNED.
                            UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_cust>.
                            READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_ttl ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_cust>
                              WITH TABLE KEY
                              matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                              locno = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
                              month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_cust> IS ASSIGNED.

                              UNASSIGN <lfs_depo_supply>.
                              READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo_supply>
                                WITH TABLE KEY
                                  depot = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
                                  matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                                  month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                              IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_depo_supply> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo_supply>-qty IS NOT INITIAL.
                                lv_supply_dmn1 = <lfs_output_dmd>-deliv * <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans / <lfs_depo_supply>-qty.
                              ENDIF.
                            ENDIF.
                          ENDIF.
                          IF lv_n_lane IS NOT INITIAL.
                            lv_supply_dmn1 = lv_supply_dmn1 / lv_n_lane.
                          ENDIF.
                        ENDIF.
                    ENDCASE.


                  ENDIF.
                  me->append_to_outtab_dyn(
                    EXPORTING
                      is_input_dmd    = <lfs_input_dmd>
                      iv_src_plant    = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_fr
*                      iv_src_plant_id     = <lfs_location_d_src>-locid_fr

                      iv_supply_plant_id  = <lfs_location_d_src>-locid_to
                      iv_supply       = lv_supply_dmn1
                      iv_clr_dmd = lv_clr_dmd
                  ).
                ENDLOOP.
              ELSE.

*                CLEAR lv_supply_dmn.

                  WITH TABLE KEY

                    matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                    month = '000000'.

                  WITH TABLE KEY skey2
                    COMPONENTS

                      locno_to = <lfs_input_dmd>-locno
                      cal_month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                IF sy-subrc <> 0.
                  IF <lfs_depo_supply> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count IS NOT INITIAL AND
                    <lfs_output_dmd> IS ASSIGNED.
                    lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_output_dmd>-deliv / <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count.
                  ENDIF.
                ENDIF.

                CLEAR: lv_clr_dmd.

                    iv_supply           = lv_supply_dmn
                    iv_clr_dmd          = lv_clr_dmd
                    iv_add_prod_plant   = abap_true
                ).
              ENDIF.
            WHEN gc_loctyp_plant OR gc_loctyp_vendor.

              UNASSIGN <lfs_location1>.
              READ TABLE gt_demand_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_location1>
                WITH KEY
                  matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                  locno_to = <lfs_location>-locno_fr
                BINARY SEARCH.
              IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_location1> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_location1>-loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_plant.

                lv_tabix1 = sy-tabix.
                LOOP AT gt_demand_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_location_d_src> FROM lv_tabix1.
                  IF NOT ( <lfs_location_d_src>-matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr AND
                  <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_to = <lfs_location>-locno_fr ).
                    EXIT.
                  ENDIF.

                  UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_dmn>.
                  READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_dmn>
                    WITH TABLE KEY
                     matnr     = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                     locno_fr  = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_fr
                     locno_to  = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_to
                     cal_month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                  IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_dmn> IS ASSIGNED.
                    IF <lfs_supply_dmn>-flag = abap_true OR <lfs_supply_dmn>-flag = 'P'.
                      CONTINUE.
                    ENDIF.
                    <lfs_supply_dmn>-flag = abap_true.
                    lv_loc = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_fr.
                    READ TABLE gt_demand_loc TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                      WITH KEY
                        matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                        locno_to = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_fr
                      BINARY SEARCH.
                    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
                      <lfs_supply_dmn>-flag = 'P'.
                      lv_loc = <lfs_location_d_src>-locno_to.
                    ENDIF.

                    UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
                    READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_src ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
                      WITH TABLE KEY
                        supply_loc  = lv_loc
                        matnr       = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr
                        month       = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.
                    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
                      <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum + <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans.
                    ELSE.
                      CLEAR ls_subtot.
                      ls_subtot-supply_loc = lv_loc.
                      ls_subtot-matnr = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr.
                      ls_subtot-month      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.
                      ls_subtot-supply_sum =  <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans.
                      INSERT ls_subtot INTO TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_src.
                    ENDIF.

                  ENDIF.
                ENDLOOP.

                lv_supply_dmn1 = lv_supply_dmn.
                CASE lv_not_lane_found.
                  WHEN abap_false.
                    me->append_to_outtab_dyn(
                      EXPORTING
                        is_input_dmd        = <lfs_input_dmd>
                        iv_src_plant        = <lfs_location>-locno_fr
*                        iv_src_plant_id     = <lfs_location>-locid_fr
                        iv_supply_plant     = <lfs_location>-locno_fr
                        iv_supply_plant_id  = <lfs_location>-locid_fr
                        iv_supply           = lv_supply_dmn
                    ).

                ENDCASE.
              ELSE.

*                IF lv_ind = 'SZ'.
                CASE lv_ind.
                  WHEN 'SZ'.
                    UNASSIGN <lfs_depo_supply>.
                    READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo_supply>
                      WITH TABLE KEY
                        depot = <lfs_location>-locno_fr
                        matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr
                        month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                    IF sy-subrc = 0.
                      <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count = <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count + 1.
                    ELSE.
                      CLEAR ls_depo_supply.
                      ls_depo_supply-depot  = <lfs_location>-locno_fr.
                      ls_depo_supply-matnr  = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr.
                      ls_depo_supply-month  = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
                      ls_depo_supply-lane_count = 1.
                      INSERT ls_depo_supply INTO TABLE gt_depo_supply.
                    ENDIF.
                    IF <lfs_output_dmd> IS ASSIGNED.
                      lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_output_dmd>-deliv.
                    ENDIF.
                  WHEN 'SNZ'.

                      WITH TABLE KEY

                        matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr

                    IF lv_not_lane_found = abap_true.

                            cal_month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.

  METHOD populate_captive_qty.

    DATA:
      ls_dmd              TYPE gty_input_demand,
      lv_supply_dmn       TYPE /sapapo/snpdeliv,
      lv_supply_dmn_con_co   TYPE /sapapo/snpdeliv,
      lv_supply_dmn_con_ci    TYPE /sapapo/snpdeliv,
      lv_captive_qty      TYPE /sapapo/snpdeliv,
      lv_opng_stock       TYPE f,
      lv_clsg_stock       TYPE f,
      lv_supply_qty       TYPE p DECIMALS 4,
      lv_fr_supply_qty    TYPE p DECIMALS 4,
      lv_supply_ratio     TYPE f,
      lv_supply_ratio_con TYPE f,
      lv_supply_qty_prev  TYPE p DECIMALS 4,
      lv_locno_fr         TYPE /sapapo/locno,
*      lv_locno_to         TYPE /sapapo/locno,
*      lv_n_lines          TYPE i,
      ls_prod_qty         TYPE gty_prod_quant,
      lt_arcmat_captive   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      lt_tlane_captive    TYPE gtt_tlane_qty.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_arcmat_cap>      TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_arcmat_cap_tmp>  TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_arcmat_subtotal> TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_tlane_captive>   TYPE gty_tlane_captive_qty,
       <lfs_tlane_captive_con>   TYPE gty_tlane_captive_qty,
      <lfs_tlane_captive2>  TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_loc>             TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_locno>           TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      <lfs_prod_qty>        TYPE gty_prod_quant,
      <lfs_subtotal>        TYPE gty_subtot.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Populating Captive Qty...'(002)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

*    do aggregation of the supplied ratio on from and to location level
    me->do_subtot_captive_qty( ).

    lt_arcmat_captive = gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive.

    DELETE lt_arcmat_captive WHERE flag = abap_false.

*    keep only unique from location for each material in the month
    SORT lt_arcmat_captive BY matnr locno_fr cal_month.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_arcmat_captive COMPARING matnr locno_fr cal_month.
*    break abap_team.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_arcmat_cap>.
    LOOP AT lt_arcmat_captive ASSIGNING <lfs_arcmat_cap>.

      CLEAR lt_tlane_captive.

*      get list of destionation plant
      me->get_tlane_trans(
        EXPORTING
          iv_matnr     = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-matnr
          iv_locno_fr  = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-locno_fr
          iv_cal_mth   = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-cal_month
        IMPORTING
          et_tlane_qty = lt_tlane_captive
      ).

*      for each destination plant found add the captive quantity in
*      the supplied ratio
      UNASSIGN <lfs_tlane_captive>.
      LOOP AT lt_tlane_captive ASSIGNING <lfs_tlane_captive>.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_arcmat_cap_tmp>.
        READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive ASSIGNING <lfs_arcmat_cap_tmp>
          WITH KEY skey2 COMPONENTS
            matnr     = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-matnr
            locno_fr  = <lfs_tlane_captive>-locno_to
            cal_month = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-cal_month.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_arcmat_cap_tmp> IS ASSIGNED AND NOT <lfs_arcmat_cap>-loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_vendor.
          <lfs_tlane_captive>-del_flag = abap_true.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR lv_supply_qty_prev.
        lv_supply_ratio = 1.
*        lv_supply_ratio_con = 1.
        lv_locno_fr = <lfs_tlane_captive>-locno_fr.
*        DESCRIBE TABLE <lfs_tlane_captive>-t_intd_loc LINES lv_n_lines.

*        find the ratio of supplied qty for multiple as well as single intermetidate plant
        UNASSIGN <lfs_locno>.
        LOOP AT <lfs_tlane_captive>-t_intd_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_locno>.

          CLEAR: lv_supply_qty.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_tlane_captive2>.
          READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive ASSIGNING <lfs_tlane_captive2>
            WITH TABLE KEY
              matnr     = <lfs_tlane_captive>-matnr
              locno_fr  = lv_locno_fr
              locno_to  = <lfs_locno>
              cal_month = <lfs_tlane_captive>-cal_month.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_tlane_captive2> IS ASSIGNED.
            lv_supply_qty = <lfs_tlane_captive2>-trans.
          ENDIF.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
          READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
            WITH TABLE KEY
              supply_loc = <lfs_locno>
              matnr      = <lfs_tlane_captive>-matnr
              month      = <lfs_tlane_captive>-cal_month
              indicator  = 'FR'.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum IS NOT INITIAL.

            lv_opng_stock = me->get_opening_stock( <lfs_subtotal> ).
            lv_clsg_stock = me->get_closing_stock( <lfs_subtotal> ).

            CLEAR ls_prod_qty.
            READ TABLE gt_prod_qty INTO ls_prod_qty
              WITH TABLE KEY
                matnr   = <lfs_subtotal>-matnr
                io_ind  = 'I'
                locno   = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_loc
                month   = <lfs_subtotal>-month.

*            to balance the supply quanity, do the following calculation
            lv_fr_supply_qty = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum - ( lv_opng_stock - lv_clsg_stock ) + ls_prod_qty-prod_quant.

            IF lv_supply_qty_prev > lv_supply_qty.
              lv_supply_ratio = lv_supply_ratio * 1.
            ELSE.
              lv_supply_ratio = lv_supply_ratio * ( lv_supply_qty / lv_fr_supply_qty ).
            ENDIF.
          ELSE.

            UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
            READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
              WITH TABLE KEY
                supply_loc = <lfs_locno>
                matnr      = <lfs_tlane_captive>-matnr
                month      = <lfs_tlane_captive>-cal_month
                indicator  = 'TO'.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum IS NOT INITIAL.

              lv_fr_supply_qty = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum.

              IF lv_supply_qty_prev > lv_supply_qty.
                lv_supply_ratio = lv_supply_ratio * 1.
              ELSE.
                lv_supply_ratio = 1.
                lv_supply_ratio = lv_supply_ratio * ( lv_supply_qty / lv_fr_supply_qty ).
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

          lv_supply_qty_prev = lv_supply_qty.
          lv_locno_fr = <lfs_locno>.

        ENDLOOP.

        CLEAR ls_dmd.
        ls_dmd-matid = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-matid.
        ls_dmd-matnr = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-matnr.
        ls_dmd-month = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-cal_month.

*        read consuming material production quantity
        CLEAR lv_supply_dmn.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr   = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-matnr
            io_ind  = 'I'
            locno   = <lfs_tlane_captive>-locno_to
*            locno   = <lfs_tlane_captive>-locno_fr "as production is done on source plant
            month   = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-cal_month.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant * lv_supply_ratio.
*        ELSE.
*          lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_tlane_captive>-trans.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
        READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey2 COMPONENTS
            locno = <lfs_tlane_captive>-locno_to.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
*          IF <lfs_arcmat_cap>-loctyp_fr <> gc_loctyp_vendor.
          lv_captive_qty = lv_supply_dmn.
          lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_tlane_captive>-trans.
*          ENDIF.
*          add the captive demand to the output table
          me->append_to_outtab_dyn(
            EXPORTING
              is_input_dmd        = ls_dmd
              iv_src_plant        = <lfs_arcmat_cap>-locno_fr
              iv_supply_plant     = <lfs_tlane_captive>-locno_to
              iv_supply_plant_id  = <lfs_loc>-locid
              iv_supply           = lv_supply_dmn
              iv_captive_qty      = lv_captive_qty
              iv_captive          = abap_true
              iv_added_by_method  = 'CAPTIVE'
          ).
          CLEAR lv_captive_qty.
        ENDIF.

      ENDLOOP.

      DELETE lt_tlane_captive WHERE del_flag = abap_true.

      APPEND LINES OF lt_tlane_captive TO gt_tlane_captive_qty.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "populate_captive_qty
