  METHOD get_opening_stock.

    DATA:
      lv_mth          TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      lv_bsg          TYPE zbsg,
      ls_loc          TYPE gty_loc,
      ls_matkey       TYPE gty_matkey,
      ls_ecc_mat  TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_buckets>     TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_pmlo>        TYPE gty_snpopmlo,
      <lfs_plma>        TYPE gty_snpoplma,
      <lfs_lpr>         TYPE gty_snpoplpr,
      <lfs_bg_div>      TYPE zbg_div.

    READ TABLE gt_loc INTO ls_loc
      WITH KEY skey2 COMPONENTS locno = is_arcmat_subtot-supply_loc.

    READ TABLE gt_matkey INTO ls_matkey
      WITH KEY skey2 COMPONENTS matnr = is_arcmat_subtot-matnr.
    READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master INTO ls_ecc_mat
      WITH KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matnr = is_arcmat_subtot-matnr.
    lv_mth = is_arcmat_subtot-month - 1.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
    READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets>
      WITH KEY cal_month = lv_mth.
    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_buckets> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_pmlo>.
      READ TABLE gt_snpopmlo ASSIGNING <lfs_pmlo>
        WITH KEY
          matid = ls_matkey-matid
          locid = ls_loc-locid
          bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke_to
        BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_pmlo> IS ASSIGNED.
        rv_opng_stock = <lfs_pmlo>-stock.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
      READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets> INDEX 1.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_buckets> IS ASSIGNED.
        CLEAR lv_bsg.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_bg_div>.
        READ TABLE gt_bg_div ASSIGNING <lfs_bg_div>
          WITH KEY div = ls_ecc_mat-spart.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_bg_div> IS ASSIGNED.
          lv_bsg = <lfs_bg_div>-bsg.
        ENDIF.
        CASE lv_bsg.
          WHEN 'CHM' OR 'PBR' OR 'PMR'.
            UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
            READ TABLE gt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr> "et_locprod
              WITH KEY
                matid = ls_matkey-matid
                locid = ls_loc-locid
                bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke
              BINARY SEARCH.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_lpr> IS ASSIGNED.
              rv_opng_stock = <lfs_lpr>-cnf_distr_rcpt.
            ENDIF.
          WHEN OTHERS.
            UNASSIGN <lfs_plma>.
            READ TABLE gt_snpoplma ASSIGNING <lfs_plma> "et_locmat
              WITH KEY
                matid = ls_matkey-matid
                locid = ls_loc-locid
              BINARY SEARCH.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_plma> IS ASSIGNED.
              rv_opng_stock = <lfs_plma>-stock.
            ENDIF.
        ENDCASE.

      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "get_opening_stock

    """Closing Stock

*  private method: finding all the destination plant of materail to which the
*  meterial has been supplied from a single source
  METHOD get_tlane_trans.

    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF lty_loc_visited,
        locno TYPE /sapapo/locno,
        visited TYPE boolean,
      END OF lty_loc_visited.

    DATA:
      lv_tabix            TYPE int4,
      ls_tlane_cost       TYPE gty_tlane_captive_qty,
      lv_tlane_ind        TYPE char3,
*      lv_loc_list         TYPE string,
      lv_locno_to         TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      lv_supplied_ratio   TYPE f,
      lv_supplied_qty     TYPE f,
      lv_ctr              TYPE i,
      lv_level            TYPE i,
      lt_intd_loc         TYPE /sapapo/locno_tab,
      lv_ctr_in           TYPE i,
      ls_loc_visited      TYPE lty_loc_visited,
      lt_loc_visited      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF lty_loc_visited,
*      lt_tlane_cost_intd  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_tlane_captive_qty,
      lt_tlane_cost       TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_tlane_captive_qty
        WITH NON-UNIQUE SORTED KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matnr locno_fr locno_to cal_month.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_loc_visited>   TYPE lty_loc_visited,
      <lfs_et_arcmat>     TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_subtot_arcmat> TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_tlane_cost>    TYPE gty_tlane_captive_qty.

*    CLEAR: lt_tlane_cost, lt_tlane_cost_intd, lt_loc_visited, lv_ctr.
    CLEAR: lt_tlane_cost, lt_loc_visited, lv_ctr.

    ls_loc_visited-locno = iv_locno_fr.
    APPEND ls_loc_visited TO lt_loc_visited.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_loc_visited>.
    READ TABLE lt_loc_visited ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_visited> INDEX 1.

    lv_tlane_ind = 'FR'.
    lv_tlane_ind = 'TO'.

*    for each source location find its detinaton location
    WHILE <lfs_loc_visited> IS ASSIGNED.
      lv_level = lv_level + 1.
      lv_ctr = lv_ctr + 1.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_et_arcmat>.
      READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
        WITH TABLE KEY skey2 COMPONENTS
          matnr     = iv_matnr
          locno_fr  = <lfs_loc_visited>-locno
          cal_month = iv_cal_mth.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lv_tabix = sy-tabix.
        CLEAR: lv_ctr_in, lt_intd_loc.
*        finding the all the destination location for the selected source
        LOOP AT gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive ASSIGNING <lfs_et_arcmat> USING KEY skey2 FROM lv_tabix.
          IF NOT ( <lfs_et_arcmat>-matnr  = iv_matnr AND
              <lfs_et_arcmat>-locno_fr  = <lfs_loc_visited>-locno AND
              <lfs_et_arcmat>-cal_month = iv_cal_mth ).
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.

          lv_ctr_in = lv_ctr_in + 1.
          IF lv_ctr = 1.
            lv_tlane_ind = 'TO'.
            lv_locno_to = <lfs_et_arcmat>-locno_to.
          ELSE.
            lv_locno_to = <lfs_et_arcmat>-locno_fr.
            lv_tlane_ind = 'FR'.
          ENDIF.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_subtot_arcmat>.
          READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_subtot_arcmat>
            WITH TABLE KEY
*              supply_loc  = <lfs_et_arcmat>-locno_to
              supply_loc  = lv_locno_to

            lv_supplied_qty = lv_supplied_ratio * <lfs_subtot_arcmat>-supply_sum.
          ELSE.
            lv_supplied_qty = <lfs_et_arcmat>-trans.
          ENDIF.

              INSERT ls_tlane_cost INTO TABLE lt_tlane_cost.
            ENDIF.

          ENDIF.

          APPEND ls_loc_visited TO lt_loc_visited.

        ENDLOOP.
      ENDIF.

        WITH KEY visited = abap_false.

    ENDWHILE.

  METHOD do_subtot_captive_qty.

    DATA:
      ls_arcmat_tot TYPE gty_subtot,
*      lv_tabix      TYPE i,
      lv_matnr      TYPE /sapapo/matnr.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_it_arcmat>     TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_it_arcmat_tmp> TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_subtotal>      TYPE gty_subtot.

    gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive = gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc.

    DELETE:
      gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive
        WHERE NOT ( loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_plant OR loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_vendor ),
      gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive
        WHERE NOT loctyp_to = gc_loctyp_plant.
    IF 1 = 2.
      lv_matnr = 'PTAL'.
      DELETE:
        gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive WHERE NOT matnr = lv_matnr.
    ENDIF.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_it_arcmat>.
    LOOP AT gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive ASSIGNING <lfs_it_arcmat>.
      <lfs_it_arcmat>-flag = abap_false.
      IF <lfs_it_arcmat>-loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_vendor.
        <lfs_it_arcmat>-flag = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
      READ TABLE gt_prod_qty TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
*        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
        WITH TABLE KEY
          matnr   = <lfs_it_arcmat>-matnr
          io_ind  = 'I'
          locno   = <lfs_it_arcmat>-locno_fr
          month   = <lfs_it_arcmat>-cal_month.
*      IF sy-subrc = 0 OR <lfs_it_arcmat>-loctyp_fr = gc_loctyp_vendor.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        <lfs_it_arcmat>-flag = abap_true.
      ELSE.
        <lfs_it_arcmat>-flag = abap_true.
*        <lfs_it_arcmat>-flag = abap_false.
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
        WITH KEY
          matnr = <lfs_it_arcmat>-matnr
          locno_to = <lfs_it_arcmat>-locno_fr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        <lfs_it_arcmat>-flag = abap_false.
*      ELSE.
*        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
*          WITH TABLE KEY
*            matnr   = <lfs_it_arcmat>-matnr
*            io_ind  = 'I'
*            locno = <lfs_it_arcmat>-locno_to
*            month = <lfs_it_arcmat>-cal_month.
*        IF sy-subrc = 0.
*          <lfs_it_arcmat>-flag = abap_true.
*        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
        WITH KEY
          matnr = <lfs_it_arcmat>-matnr
          locno_to = <lfs_it_arcmat>-locno_fr
          loctyp_to = gc_loctyp_vendor.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        <lfs_it_arcmat>-flag = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
      READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
        WITH KEY
          supply_loc = <lfs_it_arcmat>-locno_fr
          matnr      = <lfs_it_arcmat>-matnr
          month      = <lfs_it_arcmat>-cal_month
          indicator  = 'FR'.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum + <lfs_it_arcmat>-trans.
        <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count = <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count + 1.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR ls_arcmat_tot.
        ls_arcmat_tot-indicator = 'FR'.
        ls_arcmat_tot-supply_loc = <lfs_it_arcmat>-locno_fr.
        ls_arcmat_tot-matnr      = <lfs_it_arcmat>-matnr.
        ls_arcmat_tot-month      = <lfs_it_arcmat>-cal_month.
        ls_arcmat_tot-supply_sum = <lfs_it_arcmat>-trans.
        ls_arcmat_tot-lane_count = 1.
        INSERT ls_arcmat_tot INTO TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
      READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
        WITH KEY
          supply_loc = <lfs_it_arcmat>-locno_to
          matnr      = <lfs_it_arcmat>-matnr
          month      = <lfs_it_arcmat>-cal_month
          indicator  = 'TO'.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum + <lfs_it_arcmat>-trans.
        <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count = <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count + 1.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR ls_arcmat_tot.
        ls_arcmat_tot-indicator = 'TO'.
        ls_arcmat_tot-supply_loc = <lfs_it_arcmat>-locno_to.
        ls_arcmat_tot-matnr      = <lfs_it_arcmat>-matnr.
        ls_arcmat_tot-month      = <lfs_it_arcmat>-cal_month.
        ls_arcmat_tot-supply_sum = <lfs_it_arcmat>-trans.
        ls_arcmat_tot-lane_count = 1.
        INSERT ls_arcmat_tot INTO TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat.
      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "do_subtot_captive_qty

  METHOD populate_captive_qty_bkp_v2.

    DATA:
*      lv_loc        TYPE /sapapo/locno,
*      lv_locid      TYPE /sapapo/locid,
      lv_supply_dmn TYPE /sapapo/snpdeliv,
      lv_tabix      TYPE i,
      lv_tabix2      TYPE i,
      ls_dmd        TYPE gty_input_demand,
      lt_prod_qty   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_prod_quant.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_subtotal>      TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_supply_dmn>    TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_supply_dmn2>    TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,

*      <lfs_supply_src>    TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_prod_qty>      TYPE gty_prod_quant,
      <lfs_table> TYPE SORTED TABLE.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Populating Captive Qty...'(002)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive = gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc.
    DELETE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive WHERE flag =  abap_true.
    lt_prod_qty = gt_prod_qty.
    DELETE lt_prod_qty WHERE io_ind = 'O'.

*    logic to subtotal supplied qty based on supply location, material & month
    LOOP AT gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_dmn>.
*      binary search not required, less no of entries
      UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
      READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
        WITH KEY
          supply_loc = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_to
          matnr      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr
          region     = space
          month      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum + <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans.
        <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count = <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count + 1.
      ELSE.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>.
        IF <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_subtotal>-supply_loc = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_to.
          <lfs_subtotal>-region     = space.
          <lfs_subtotal>-matnr      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr.
          <lfs_subtotal>-month      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.
          <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum =  <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans.
          <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count = 1.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

*    IF <lfs_table> is ASSIGNED.

          cal_month = <lfs_prod_qty>-month.

              cal_month = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.

*              lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant * <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans / <lfs_supply_armat>-supply_sum.

              IF <lfs_supply_armat>-supply_sum IS NOT INITIAL.
                lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant * <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans / <lfs_supply_armat>-supply_sum.
              ENDIF.
              IF <lfs_supply_armat>-lane_count IS NOT INITIAL.
                lv_supply_dmn = lv_supply_dmn / <lfs_supply_armat>-lane_count.
              ENDIF.

            ENDIF.

            CLEAR ls_dmd.
            ls_dmd-matid = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matid.
            ls_dmd-matnr = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr.
            ls_dmd-month = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.
            me->append_to_outtab_dyn(
              EXPORTING
                is_input_dmd        = ls_dmd
                iv_src_plant        = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_fr
                iv_supply_plant     = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_to
                iv_supply_plant_id  = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locid_to
                iv_supply           = lv_supply_dmn
                iv_captive          = abap_true
            ).
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
      ELSE.
        lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        CLEAR ls_dmd.
        ls_dmd-matid = <lfs_prod_qty>-matid.
        ls_dmd-matnr = <lfs_prod_qty>-matnr.
        ls_dmd-month = <lfs_prod_qty>-month.
        me->append_to_outtab_dyn(
          EXPORTING
            is_input_dmd        = ls_dmd
            iv_src_plant        = <lfs_prod_qty>-locno
            iv_supply_plant     = <lfs_prod_qty>-locno
            iv_supply_plant_id  = ''"<lfs_supply_dmn>-locid_to
            iv_supply           = lv_supply_dmn
            iv_captive          = abap_true
        ).
      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "populate_captive_qty_bkp_v2

  METHOD populate_captive_qty_bkp_v1.

    DATA:
      lv_loc        TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      lv_locid      TYPE /sapapo/locid,
      lv_supply_dmn TYPE /sapapo/snpdeliv,
*      lv_clr_dmd    TYPE boolean,
      ls_dmd        TYPE gty_input_demand.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
*      <lfs_location1>     TYPE gty_demand_loc,
*      <lfs_depo_supply>   TYPE gty_depo_supply,
      <lfs_subtotal>      TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_supply_dmn>    TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_supply_armat>  TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_supply_src>    TYPE gty_subtot.

    gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive = gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc.
    DELETE gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive WHERE flag =  abap_true.

    LOOP AT gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_dmn>.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
      READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
        WITH KEY
          supply_loc = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_to
          matnr      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr
          region     = ''
          month      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum + <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans.
      ELSE.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>.
        IF <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_subtotal>-supply_loc = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_to.
          <lfs_subtotal>-region     = space.
          <lfs_subtotal>-matnr = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr.
          <lfs_subtotal>-month      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.
          <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum =  <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    LOOP AT gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc_captive ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_dmn>.

      CLEAR: lv_loc, lv_locid, lv_supply_dmn.
      IF <lfs_supply_dmn>-flag = 'P'.
        lv_loc = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_fr.
        lv_locid = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locid_fr.
      ELSE.
        lv_loc = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_to.
        lv_locid = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locid_to.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_armat>.
      READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_armat>
        WITH KEY
        supply_loc = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_to
        matnr      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr
        month      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_armat> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_src>.
        READ TABLE   gt_subtot_dmd_src ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_src>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          supply_loc = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_to
          matnr      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr
          month      = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_armat> IS ASSIGNED.
          IF <lfs_supply_armat>-supply_sum IS NOT INITIAL.
            lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_supply_src>-supply_sum * <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans / <lfs_supply_armat>-supply_sum.
          ENDIF.
        ELSE.
          lv_supply_dmn = <lfs_supply_dmn>-trans.
*          UNASSIGN <lfs_location1>.
          READ TABLE gt_demand_loc TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
            WITH TABLE KEY skey2 COMPONENTS
              matnr = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr
              locno_fr = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_to.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
            lv_loc = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_fr.
            lv_locid = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locid_fr.
          ENDIF.
          READ TABLE gt_demand_loc TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
           WITH TABLE KEY skey3 COMPONENTS
             matnr = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr
             locno_to = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_fr.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            CONTINUE.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.
      CLEAR ls_dmd.
      ls_dmd-matid = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matid.
      ls_dmd-matnr = <lfs_supply_dmn>-matnr.
      ls_dmd-month = <lfs_supply_dmn>-cal_month.
      me->append_to_outtab_dyn(
        EXPORTING
          is_input_dmd        = ls_dmd
          iv_src_plant        = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locno_fr
*          iv_src_plant_id     = <lfs_supply_dmn>-locid_fr
          iv_supply_plant     = lv_loc
          iv_supply_plant_id  = lv_locid
          iv_supply           = lv_supply_dmn
          iv_captive          = abap_true
      ).

    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.                    "populate_captive_qty_bkp_v1

  METHOD populate_no_dmd_opng_stk.

    DATA:
*      ls_demand             TYPE gty_input_demand,
*      lv_reg_off      TYPE /bic/oip_reg_off,
      ls_fin_fields   TYPE gty_fin_fields,
      ls_fin_qty      TYPE gty_fin_qty,
      lo_struct       TYPE REF TO data,
      lv_cal_month TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      lt_no_dmd_snpoplma    TYPE TABLE OF gty_snpoplma,
      lt_snpoplpr    TYPE TABLE OF gty_snpoplpr.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_table>       TYPE SORTED TABLE,
      <lfs_final>       TYPE any,
      <lfs_comp>        TYPE any,
*      <lfs_cust_dmd>    TYPE gty_cust_dmd_inp,
      <lfs_matkey> TYPE gty_matkey,
      <lfs_ecc_mat>     TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master,
      <lfs_mat_prod_plant> TYPE gty_mat_prod_plant,
      <lfs_supply_arcmat> TYPE gty_supply_arcmat,
      <lfs_loc>     TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_buckets>     TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_ecc_prodh>   TYPE gty_ecc_prod_mast,
      <lfs_ecc_material> TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master,
      <lfs_prod_qty>  TYPE gty_prod_quant,
      <lfs_lma> TYPE gty_snpoplma,
      <lfs_lpr> TYPE gty_snpoplpr,
      <lfs_lpr_opng>         TYPE gty_snpoplpr.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Populating No Dmd/Supply Opening Stock Qty...'(069)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    UNASSIGN <lfs_table>.
    ASSIGN lo_tab_sorted->* TO <lfs_table>.
    IF <lfs_table> IS NOT ASSIGNED.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lt_no_dmd_snpoplma = gt_snpoplma. "et_locmat

    DELETE:
      lt_no_dmd_snpoplma WHERE NOT sessionid IS NOT INITIAL,
      lt_no_dmd_snpoplma WHERE NOT stock IS NOT INITIAL.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
    READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets> INDEX 1.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      lv_cal_month = <lfs_buckets>-cal_month.
    ENDIF.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_lma>.
    LOOP AT lt_no_dmd_snpoplma ASSIGNING <lfs_lma>.
      CLEAR: ls_fin_fields, ls_fin_qty.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
          matid = <lfs_lma>-matid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      ls_fin_fields-cal_mth = lv_cal_month.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_mat_prod_plant>.
      READ TABLE gt_mat_prod_plant ASSIGNING <lfs_mat_prod_plant>  " Commented By KPIT_095 CD: 8040819 TR: AD1K912614
        WITH TABLE KEY matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_mat_prod_plant> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-derived_plant_list = <lfs_mat_prod_plant>-plant_list.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
      READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
          locid = <lfs_lma>-locid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-supply_plant = <lfs_loc>-locno.
        CASE <lfs_loc>-loctype.
          WHEN gc_loctyp_plant.
            ls_fin_fields-src_plant   = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
            ls_fin_fields-plant_desc  = <lfs_loc>-descr.
          WHEN gc_loctyp_depo.
*            reading IT_ARCAMT
            READ TABLE gt_supply_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_arcmat>
              WITH TABLE KEY skey COMPONENTS
                matnr     = ls_fin_fields-matnr
                locno_to  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
                cal_month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_arcmat> IS ASSIGNED.
              ls_fin_fields-src_plant = <lfs_supply_arcmat>-locno_fr.
              UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
              READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
                WITH TABLE KEY skey2 COMPONENTS
                  locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
              IF sy-subrc = 0.
                ls_fin_fields-plant_desc = <lfs_loc>-descr.
              ENDIF.
*            else.
*              ls_fin_fields-src_plant   = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
            ENDIF.

        ENDCASE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
      READ TABLE <lfs_table> ASSIGNING <lfs_final>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          ('SRC_PLANT')     = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
          ('SUPPLY_PLANT')  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
          ('MATNR')         = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          ('CAL_MTH')       = lv_cal_month.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_qty = <lfs_comp>.
        ENDIF.
        ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk + <lfs_lma>-stock.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        ls_fin_fields-added_by_method  = 'OPNG_STOCK'.
        ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = <lfs_lma>-stock.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
        READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets>
          WITH KEY cal_month = lv_cal_month.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_buckets> IS ASSIGNED.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
          READ TABLE gt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr> "et_locprod
            WITH KEY
              matid = <lfs_lma>-matid
              locid = <lfs_lma>-locid
              bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke_to
            BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_lpr> IS ASSIGNED.
            CLEAR <lfs_lpr>-sessionid.
            ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = <lfs_lpr>-safty.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'O'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
*          IF <lfs_prod_qty>-ind_con = 'C'.
*            ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
*          ELSE.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
*          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.


        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'CO'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
          ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
*          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'I'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd    =
          ls_fin_qty-captive_supply = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'CI'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_mat>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_mat>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_mat> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-div   = <lfs_ecc_mat>-spart.
          ls_fin_fields-meins = <lfs_ecc_mat>-meins.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_prodh>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_prod_mast ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_prodh>
          WITH KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_prodh> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-merge     = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-bismt.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh1_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh1.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh2_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh2.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh3_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh3.

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr2_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR2'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr2
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM2T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr3_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR3'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr3
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM3T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr4_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR4'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr4
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM4T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr5_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR5'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr5
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM5T'
          ).

        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
        FREE lo_struct.
        CREATE DATA lo_struct TYPE HANDLE go_desc_struct.
        ASSIGN lo_struct->* TO <lfs_final>.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_fields.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.

        INSERT <lfs_final> INTO TABLE <lfs_table>.

      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_lma>.
    lt_snpoplpr = gt_snpoplpr.
    DELETE lt_snpoplpr WHERE sessionid IS INITIAL.
    DELETE lt_snpoplpr WHERE cnf_distr_rcpt IS INITIAL.

    LOOP AT lt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr_opng>.
      CLEAR: ls_fin_fields, ls_fin_qty.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
          matid = <lfs_lpr_opng>-matid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.

      CLEAR lv_cal_month.
      LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets>
          WHERE bucke <= <lfs_lpr_opng>-bucke AND bucke_to >= <lfs_lpr_opng>-bucke.
        lv_cal_month = <lfs_buckets>-cal_month.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
*      lv_cal_month = lv_cal_month + 1.
      ls_fin_fields-cal_mth = lv_cal_month.
*      READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets>
*        with key cal_month = lv_cal_month.
*      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*        CONTINUE.
*      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_mat_prod_plant>.
      READ TABLE gt_mat_prod_plant ASSIGNING <lfs_mat_prod_plant>  " Commented By KPIT_095 CD: 8040819 TR: AD1K912614
        WITH TABLE KEY matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_mat_prod_plant> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-derived_plant_list = <lfs_mat_prod_plant>-plant_list.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
      READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
          locid = <lfs_lpr_opng>-locid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-supply_plant = <lfs_loc>-locno.
        CASE <lfs_loc>-loctype.
          WHEN gc_loctyp_plant.
            ls_fin_fields-src_plant   = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
            ls_fin_fields-plant_desc  = <lfs_loc>-descr.
          WHEN gc_loctyp_depo.
*            reading IT_ARCAMT
            READ TABLE gt_supply_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_arcmat>
              WITH TABLE KEY skey COMPONENTS
                matnr     = ls_fin_fields-matnr
                locno_to  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
                cal_month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_arcmat> IS ASSIGNED.
              ls_fin_fields-src_plant = <lfs_supply_arcmat>-locno_fr.
              UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
              READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
                WITH TABLE KEY skey2 COMPONENTS
                  locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
              IF sy-subrc = 0.
                ls_fin_fields-plant_desc = <lfs_loc>-descr.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.

        ENDCASE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
      READ TABLE <lfs_table> ASSIGNING <lfs_final>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          ('SRC_PLANT')     = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
          ('SUPPLY_PLANT')  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
          ('MATNR')         = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          ('CAL_MTH')       = lv_cal_month.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_qty = <lfs_comp>.
        ENDIF.
        ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk + <lfs_lpr_opng>-cnf_distr_rcpt.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        ls_fin_fields-added_by_method  = 'OPNG_STOCK'.
        ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = <lfs_lpr_opng>-cnf_distr_rcpt.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
        READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets>
          WITH KEY cal_month = lv_cal_month.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_buckets> IS ASSIGNED.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
          READ TABLE gt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr> "et_locprod
            WITH KEY
              matid = <lfs_lpr_opng>-matid
              locid = <lfs_lpr_opng>-locid
              bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke_to
            BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_lpr> IS ASSIGNED.
            CLEAR <lfs_lpr>-sessionid.
            ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = <lfs_lpr>-safty.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'O'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'I'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd    =
          ls_fin_qty-captive_supply = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_mat>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_mat>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_mat> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-div   = <lfs_ecc_mat>-spart.
          ls_fin_fields-meins = <lfs_ecc_mat>-meins.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_prodh>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_prod_mast ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_prodh>
          WITH KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_prodh> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-merge     = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-bismt.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh1_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh1.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh2_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh2.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh3_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh3.

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr2_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR2'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr2
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM2T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr3_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR3'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr3
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM3T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr4_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR4'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr4
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM4T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr5_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR5'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr5
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM5T'
          ).

        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
        FREE lo_struct.
        CREATE DATA lo_struct TYPE HANDLE go_desc_struct.
        ASSIGN lo_struct->* TO <lfs_final>.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_fields.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.

        INSERT <lfs_final> INTO TABLE <lfs_table>.

      ENDIF.


    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "populate_no_dmd_opng_stk

  METHOD populate_no_dmd_clsng_stk.

    DATA:
      lt_snpopmlo    TYPE TABLE OF gty_snpopmlo,
      ltr_bucket  TYPE RANGE OF /sapapo/snpbucke,
      lo_struct       TYPE REF TO data,
      ls_fin_fields   TYPE gty_fin_fields,
      ls_fin_qty      TYPE gty_fin_qty.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_table>       TYPE SORTED TABLE,
      <lfs_final>       TYPE any,
      <lfs_comp>        TYPE any,
      <lfs_matkey> TYPE gty_matkey,
      <lfs_loc>     TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_ecc_material> TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master,
      <lfs_mat_prod_plant> TYPE gty_mat_prod_plant,
      <lfs_supply_arcmat> TYPE gty_supply_arcmat,
      <lfs_prod_qty>  TYPE gty_prod_quant,
      <lfs_snpoplmo> TYPE gty_snpopmlo,
      <lfs_ecc_prodh>   TYPE gty_ecc_prod_mast,
      <lfs_bucket>   TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_r_bucket> LIKE LINE OF ltr_bucket,
      <lfs_buckets> TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_lpr> TYPE gty_snpoplpr.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Populating No Dmd/Supply Closing Stock Qty...'(074)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    UNASSIGN <lfs_table>.
    ASSIGN lo_tab_sorted->* TO <lfs_table>.
    IF <lfs_table> IS NOT ASSIGNED.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lt_snpopmlo = gt_snpopmlo.

    DELETE:
      lt_snpopmlo WHERE sessionid IS INITIAL,
      lt_snpopmlo WHERE stock IS INITIAL AND viosf IS INITIAL.
*      lt_snpopmlo WHERE stock IS INITIAL.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
    LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO ltr_bucket ASSIGNING <lfs_r_bucket>.
      IF <lfs_r_bucket> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_r_bucket>-sign = 'I'.
        <lfs_r_bucket>-option = 'EQ'.
        <lfs_r_bucket>-low = <lfs_bucket>-bucke_to.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    DELETE lt_snpopmlo WHERE bucke NOT IN ltr_bucket.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_snpoplmo>.
    LOOP AT lt_snpopmlo ASSIGNING <lfs_snpoplmo>.

*      IF <lfs_snpoplmo>-stock IS INITIAL AND <lfs_snpoplmo>-viosf IS INITIAL.
*        CONTINUE.
*      ENDIF.

      CLEAR: ls_fin_fields,ls_fin_qty.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
          matid = <lfs_snpoplmo>-matid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
      READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket>
        WITH KEY bucke_to = <lfs_snpoplmo>-bucke.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_bucket> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-cal_mth = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_mat_prod_plant>.
      READ TABLE gt_mat_prod_plant ASSIGNING <lfs_mat_prod_plant>
        WITH TABLE KEY matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_mat_prod_plant> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-derived_plant_list = <lfs_mat_prod_plant>-plant_list.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
      READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
          locid = <lfs_snpoplmo>-locid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-supply_plant = <lfs_loc>-locno.
        CASE <lfs_loc>-loctype.
          WHEN gc_loctyp_plant.
            ls_fin_fields-src_plant = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
            ls_fin_fields-plant_desc = <lfs_loc>-descr.

          WHEN gc_loctyp_depo.
*            reading IT_ARCAMT
            READ TABLE gt_supply_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_arcmat>
              WITH TABLE KEY skey COMPONENTS
                matnr     = ls_fin_fields-matnr
                locno_to  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
                cal_month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_arcmat> IS ASSIGNED.
              ls_fin_fields-src_plant = <lfs_supply_arcmat>-locno_fr.
              UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
              READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
                WITH TABLE KEY skey2 COMPONENTS
                  locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
              IF sy-subrc = 0.
                ls_fin_fields-plant_desc = <lfs_loc>-descr.
              ENDIF.
*            else.
*              ls_fin_fields-src_plant = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
            ENDIF.
        ENDCASE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
      READ TABLE <lfs_table> ASSIGNING <lfs_final>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          ('SRC_PLANT')     = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
          ('SUPPLY_PLANT')  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
          ('MATNR')         = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          ('CAL_MTH')       = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_qty = <lfs_comp>.
        ENDIF.
        ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk + <lfs_snpoplmo>-stock.
        ls_fin_qty-sfty_stk_shortfall = ls_fin_qty-sfty_stk_shortfall + <lfs_snpoplmo>-viosf.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        ls_fin_fields-added_by_method  = 'CLSG_STOCK'.
        ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk           = <lfs_snpoplmo>-stock.
        ls_fin_qty-sfty_stk_shortfall = <lfs_snpoplmo>-viosf.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'O'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty     = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'CO'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con     = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
          ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con     = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'I'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd    =
          ls_fin_qty-captive_supply = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'CI'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
        READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets>
          WITH KEY cal_month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_buckets> IS ASSIGNED.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
          READ TABLE gt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr> "et_locprod
            WITH KEY
              matid = <lfs_snpoplmo>-matid
              locid = <lfs_snpoplmo>-locid
              bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke_to
            BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_lpr> IS ASSIGNED.
            CLEAR <lfs_lpr>-sessionid.
            ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = <lfs_lpr>-safty.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_material>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_material>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_material> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-div   = <lfs_ecc_material>-spart.
          ls_fin_fields-meins = <lfs_ecc_material>-meins.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_prodh>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_prod_mast ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_prodh>
          WITH KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_prodh> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-div       = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-spart.
          ls_fin_fields-merge     = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-bismt.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh1_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh1.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh2_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh2.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh3_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh3.

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr2_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR2'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr2
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM2T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr3_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR3'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr3
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM3T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr4_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR4'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr4
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM4T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr5_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR5'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr5
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM5T'
          ).

        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
        FREE lo_struct.
        CREATE DATA lo_struct TYPE HANDLE go_desc_struct.
        ASSIGN lo_struct->* TO <lfs_final>.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_fields.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.

        INSERT <lfs_final> INTO TABLE <lfs_table>.

      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "populate_no_dmd_clsng_stk

  METHOD populate_no_dmd_trgt_clsng_stk.

    DATA:
      lt_snpoplpr   TYPE TABLE OF gty_snpoplpr,
      ltr_bucket    TYPE RANGE OF /sapapo/snpbucke,
      lo_struct     TYPE REF TO data,
      ls_fin_fields TYPE gty_fin_fields,
      ls_fin_qty    TYPE gty_fin_qty.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_table>           TYPE SORTED TABLE,
      <lfs_final>           TYPE any,
      <lfs_comp>            TYPE any,
      <lfs_matkey>          TYPE gty_matkey,
      <lfs_loc>             TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_ecc_material>    TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master,
      <lfs_mat_prod_plant>  TYPE gty_mat_prod_plant,
      <lfs_supply_arcmat>   TYPE gty_supply_arcmat,
      <lfs_prod_qty>        TYPE gty_prod_quant,
      <lfs_snpoplmo>        TYPE gty_snpopmlo,
      <lfs_ecc_prodh>       TYPE gty_ecc_prod_mast,
      <lfs_bucket>          TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_r_bucket>        LIKE LINE OF ltr_bucket,
      <lfs_buckets>         TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_lpr>             TYPE gty_snpoplpr.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Populating No Dmd/Supply Target Closing Stock Qty...'(083)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    UNASSIGN <lfs_table>.
    ASSIGN lo_tab_sorted->* TO <lfs_table>.
    IF <lfs_table> IS NOT ASSIGNED.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lt_snpoplpr = gt_snpoplpr.

    DELETE:
      lt_snpoplpr WHERE sessionid IS INITIAL,
      lt_snpoplpr WHERE safty IS INITIAL.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
    LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO ltr_bucket ASSIGNING <lfs_r_bucket>.
      IF <lfs_r_bucket> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_r_bucket>-sign   = 'I'.
        <lfs_r_bucket>-option = 'EQ'.
        <lfs_r_bucket>-low    = <lfs_bucket>-bucke_to.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    DELETE lt_snpoplpr WHERE bucke NOT IN ltr_bucket.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
    LOOP AT lt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr>.
      CLEAR: ls_fin_fields,ls_fin_qty.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
          matid = <lfs_lpr>-matid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ELSE.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
      READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
          locid = <lfs_lpr>-locid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-supply_plant = <lfs_loc>-locno.
        CASE <lfs_loc>-loctype.
          WHEN gc_loctyp_plant.
            ls_fin_fields-src_plant = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
            ls_fin_fields-plant_desc = <lfs_loc>-descr.

          WHEN gc_loctyp_depo.
*            reading IT_ARCAMT
            READ TABLE gt_supply_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_arcmat>
              WITH TABLE KEY skey COMPONENTS
                matnr     = ls_fin_fields-matnr
                locno_to  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
                cal_month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_arcmat> IS ASSIGNED.
              ls_fin_fields-src_plant = <lfs_supply_arcmat>-locno_fr.
              UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
              READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
                WITH TABLE KEY skey2 COMPONENTS
                  locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
              IF sy-subrc = 0.
                ls_fin_fields-plant_desc = <lfs_loc>-descr.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          WHEN OTHERS.
            CONTINUE.
        ENDCASE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
      READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket>
        WITH KEY bucke_to = <lfs_lpr>-bucke.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_bucket> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-cal_mth = <lfs_bucket>-cal_month.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_mat_prod_plant>.
      READ TABLE gt_mat_prod_plant ASSIGNING <lfs_mat_prod_plant>
        WITH TABLE KEY matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_mat_prod_plant> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-derived_plant_list = <lfs_mat_prod_plant>-plant_list.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
      READ TABLE <lfs_table> ASSIGNING <lfs_final>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          ('SRC_PLANT')     = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
          ('SUPPLY_PLANT')  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
          ('MATNR')         = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          ('CAL_MTH')       = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_qty = <lfs_comp>.
        ENDIF.
        ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk + <lfs_lpr>-safty.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        ls_fin_fields-added_by_method  = 'TRGT_CLSG_STOCK'.
        ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = <lfs_lpr>-safty.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr   = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind  = 'O'
            locno   = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month   = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr   = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind  = 'CO'
            locno   = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month   = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
          ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'I'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd    =
          ls_fin_qty-captive_supply = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
        READ TABLE gt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            io_ind = 'CI'
            locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_prod_qty> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_prod_qty>-flag_added = abap_true.
          ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_material>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_material>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_material> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-div   = <lfs_ecc_material>-spart.
          ls_fin_fields-meins = <lfs_ecc_material>-meins.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_prodh>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_prod_mast ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_prodh>
          WITH KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_prodh> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-div       = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-spart.
          ls_fin_fields-merge     = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-bismt.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh1_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh1.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh2_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh2.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh3_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh3.

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr2_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR2'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr2
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM2T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr3_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR3'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr3
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM3T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr4_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR4'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr4
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM4T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr5_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR5'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr5
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM5T'
          ).

        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
        FREE lo_struct.
        CREATE DATA lo_struct TYPE HANDLE go_desc_struct.
        ASSIGN lo_struct->* TO <lfs_final>.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_fields.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.

        INSERT <lfs_final> INTO TABLE <lfs_table>.

      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "populate_no_dmd_trgt_clsng_stk

  METHOD populate_no_dmd_prod_qty.

    DATA:
      lt_prod_qty   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_prod_quant,
*      lt_snpopmlo   TYPE TABLE OF gty_snpopmlo,
      ltr_bucket    TYPE RANGE OF /sapapo/snpbucke,
      lo_struct     TYPE REF TO data,
      ls_fin_fields TYPE gty_fin_fields,
      ls_fin_qty    TYPE gty_fin_qty.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_prod_qty>        TYPE gty_prod_quant,
      <lfs_table>           TYPE SORTED TABLE,
      <lfs_final>           TYPE any,
      <lfs_comp>            TYPE any,
      <lfs_matkey>          TYPE gty_matkey,
      <lfs_loc>             TYPE gty_loc,
      <lfs_ecc_material>    TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master,
      <lfs_mat_prod_plant>  TYPE gty_mat_prod_plant,
      <lfs_supply_arcmat>   TYPE gty_supply_arcmat,
      <lfs_snpoplmo>        TYPE gty_snpopmlo,
      <lfs_ecc_prodh>       TYPE gty_ecc_prod_mast,
      <lfs_bucket>          TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_lpr> TYPE gty_snpoplpr,
      <lfs_r_bucket>        LIKE LINE OF ltr_bucket.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Populating No Dmd/Supply Production Qty...'(076)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    UNASSIGN <lfs_table>.
    ASSIGN lo_tab_sorted->* TO <lfs_table>.
    IF <lfs_table> IS NOT ASSIGNED.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lt_prod_qty = gt_prod_qty.
*    DELETE lt_prod_qty WHERE flag_added = abap_true OR io_ind = 'CI' OR io_ind = 'CO'.
    DELETE lt_prod_qty WHERE flag_added = abap_true.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_prod_qty>.
    LOOP AT lt_prod_qty ASSIGNING <lfs_prod_qty>.

      CLEAR: ls_fin_fields,ls_fin_qty.

      ls_fin_fields-src_plant = <lfs_prod_qty>-locno.
      ls_fin_fields-supply_plant = <lfs_prod_qty>-locno.
      ls_fin_fields-matnr = <lfs_prod_qty>-matnr.
      ls_fin_fields-cal_mth = <lfs_prod_qty>-month.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_loc>.
      READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
        WITH TABLE KEY skey2 COMPONENTS
          locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-plant_desc = <lfs_loc>-descr.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_mat_prod_plant>.
      READ TABLE gt_mat_prod_plant ASSIGNING <lfs_mat_prod_plant>
        WITH TABLE KEY matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_mat_prod_plant> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-derived_plant_list = <lfs_mat_prod_plant>-plant_list.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
      READ TABLE <lfs_table> ASSIGNING <lfs_final>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          ('SRC_PLANT')     = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
          ('SUPPLY_PLANT')  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
          ('MATNR')         = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          ('CAL_MTH')       = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_qty = <lfs_comp>.
        ENDIF.

*        IF <lfs_prod_qty>-io_ind = 'I' .
**        IF <lfs_prod_qty>-io_ind CS 'I'.
*          ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd = ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd + <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
*          ls_fin_qty-captive_supply = ls_fin_qty-captive_supply + <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
*        ELSE.
*          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty + <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
*        ENDIF.

        CASE <lfs_prod_qty>-io_ind.
          WHEN 'I'.
            ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd = ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd + <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
            ls_fin_qty-captive_supply = ls_fin_qty-captive_supply + <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
          WHEN 'O'.
            ls_fin_qty-prod_qty = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty + <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
          WHEN 'CI'.
            ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant + ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd_con.
          WHEN 'CO'.
            ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant + ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con.
            ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con    = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant + ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con.
        ENDCASE.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.

        ls_fin_fields-added_by_method  = 'PROD_QTY'.
*        IF <lfs_prod_qty>-io_ind = 'I'.
*          ls_fin_qty-captive_supply =
*          ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd    = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
*        ELSE.
*          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
*        ENDIF.
        CASE <lfs_prod_qty>-io_ind.
          WHEN 'I'.
            ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd    = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
            ls_fin_qty-captive_supply = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
          WHEN 'O'.
            ls_fin_qty-prod_qty       = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
          WHEN 'CI'.
            ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
          WHEN 'CO'.
            ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
            ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con    = <lfs_prod_qty>-prod_quant.
        ENDCASE.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
        READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket>
          WITH KEY cal_month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_bucket> IS ASSIGNED.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
          READ TABLE gt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr> "et_locprod
            WITH KEY
              matid = <lfs_prod_qty>-matid
              locid = <lfs_prod_qty>-locid
              bucke = <lfs_bucket>-bucke_to
            BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_lpr> IS ASSIGNED.
            ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = <lfs_lpr>-safty.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_mat_prod_plant>.
        READ TABLE gt_mat_prod_plant ASSIGNING <lfs_mat_prod_plant>  " Commented By KPIT_095 CD: 8040819 TR: AD1K912614
          WITH TABLE KEY matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_mat_prod_plant> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-derived_plant_list = <lfs_mat_prod_plant>-plant_list.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_material>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_material>
          WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_material> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-div   = <lfs_ecc_material>-spart.
          ls_fin_fields-meins = <lfs_ecc_material>-meins.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_prodh>.
        READ TABLE gt_ecc_prod_mast ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_prodh>
          WITH KEY
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_ecc_prodh> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-div       = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-spart.
          ls_fin_fields-merge     = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-bismt.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh1_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh1.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh2_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh2.
          ls_fin_fields-pdh3_desc = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-despdh3.

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr2_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR2'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr2
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM2T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr3_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR3'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr3
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM3T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr4_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR4'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr4
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM4T'
          ).

          ls_fin_fields-mvgr5_desc = me->read_mat_grp_desc(
              iv_field       = 'MVGR5'
              iv_fieldval    = <lfs_ecc_prodh>-mvgr5
              iv_int_tabname = 'GT_ECC_TVM5T'
          ).

        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
        FREE lo_struct.
        CREATE DATA lo_struct TYPE HANDLE go_desc_struct.
        ASSIGN lo_struct->* TO <lfs_final>.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.

        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_fields.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.

      ENDIF.

*  calculate subtotal based on supply for demand ratio calculation
  METHOD get_subtol_for_dmd.

    DATA:
      ls_fin_fields TYPE gty_fin_fields,
      lv_locno TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      lv_fieldname  TYPE fieldname,
      ls_subtot   TYPE gty_subtot.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_reg_off> TYPE gty_reg_off_desc,
      <lfs_comp> TYPE any,
      <lfs_tab_supply> TYPE SORTED TABLE,
      <lfs_subtot> TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_fin_sort> TYPE any.

    ASSIGN lo_tab_sorted->* TO <lfs_tab_supply>.
    IF <lfs_tab_supply> IS NOT ASSIGNED.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Subtotaling Supply Qty Month & Supply Loc Wise...'(059)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    LOOP AT <lfs_tab_supply> ASSIGNING <lfs_fin_sort>.
      CLEAR: ls_fin_fields.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp>.
      IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields = <lfs_comp>.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_reg_off>.
      LOOP AT gt_reg_off_desc ASSIGNING <lfs_reg_off>.
        CLEAR lv_fieldname.
        lv_fieldname = 'S_' && <lfs_reg_off>-reg_off.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_fieldname OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          CLEAR lv_locno.
*          lv_locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
*          READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
*          WITH TABLE KEY skey3 COMPONENTS locno = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
*          loctype = gc_loctyp_plant.
*          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*            lv_locno = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
*          ENDIF.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_subtot>.
          READ TABLE gt_subtot_reg_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_subtot>
            WITH TABLE KEY
              supply_loc = lv_locno
              matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
              region     = <lfs_reg_off>-reg_off
              month      = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth
              indicator   = 'S'.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtot> IS ASSIGNED.
            <lfs_subtot>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtot>-supply_sum + <lfs_comp>.
          ELSE.
            CLEAR ls_subtot.
            ls_subtot-supply_loc = lv_locno.
            ls_subtot-region     = <lfs_reg_off>-reg_off.
            ls_subtot-matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
            ls_subtot-month      = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
            ls_subtot-indicator   = 'S'.
            ls_subtot-supply_sum =  <lfs_comp>.
            INSERT ls_subtot INTO TABLE gt_subtot_reg_loc.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "get_subtol_for_dmd

  METHOD do_subtol_for_dmd.

    DATA:
      lv_reg_off  TYPE /bic/oip_reg_off,
      ls_subtot   TYPE gty_subtot.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
*      <lfs_pb_data> TYPE gty_pb_data,
      <lfs_subtot>  TYPE gty_subtot.

*    UNASSIGN <lfs_pb_data>.
*    READ TABLE gt_pb_data ASSIGNING <lfs_pb_data>
*      WITH KEY
*        matnr    = iv_matnr
**        div      = is_input_dmd-div
*        planshp  = iv_cust.
*    IF sy-subrc  = 0 AND <lfs_pb_data> IS ASSIGNED.
*      lv_reg_off = <lfs_pb_data>-reg_off.
*    ELSE.
*      lv_reg_off = 'OTHR'.
*    ENDIF.
    lv_reg_off = me->get_region(
        iv_matnr   = iv_matnr
        iv_cust    = iv_cust
    ).
    UNASSIGN <lfs_subtot>.
    READ TABLE gt_subtot_reg_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_subtot>
      WITH TABLE KEY
        supply_loc  = iv_locfrm
        matnr       = iv_matnr
        region      = lv_reg_off
        month       = iv_month
        indicator   = iv_ind.
    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtot> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_subtot>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtot>-supply_sum + iv_dmd_qty.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR ls_subtot.
      ls_subtot-supply_loc = iv_locfrm.
      ls_subtot-region     = lv_reg_off.
      ls_subtot-matnr = iv_matnr.
      ls_subtot-month      = iv_month.
      ls_subtot-indicator      = iv_ind.
      ls_subtot-supply_sum =  iv_dmd_qty.
      INSERT ls_subtot INTO TABLE gt_subtot_reg_loc.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "do_subtol_for_dmd

*  METHOD do_subtotal.

*      ls_subtot TYPE gty_subtot.
*    FIELD-SYMBOLS:

*      <lfs_tab> TYPE SORTED TABLE.
*    ASSIGN (iv_tabname) TO <lfs_tab>.

*      ELSE.
