  METHOD get_region.
** BOC Azure 20231 - Populate the sales Office ffrom the value fetched from ECC
    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
     <lfs_pb_data> TYPE gty_pb_data,
      <lfs_kunnr>   TYPE gty_loc_det,
      <lfs_loc_kn>  TYPE gty_loc_kn.

    READ TABLE gt_loc_kn ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_kn> WITH KEY matnr = iv_matnr "#EC WARNOK
                                                         locno = iv_cust.
    IF sy-subrc  = 0 AND  <lfs_loc_kn> IS ASSIGNED.
      READ TABLE gt_kunnr_final ASSIGNING <lfs_kunnr> WITH KEY kunnr = <lfs_loc_kn>-locno_2. "#EC WARNOK
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_kunnr> IS ASSIGNED.
        rv_reg_off = <lfs_kunnr>-vkbur.
      ELSE.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_pb_data>.
        READ TABLE gt_pb_data ASSIGNING <lfs_pb_data> WITH KEY matnr    = iv_matnr
                                                               planshp  = iv_cust BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_pb_data> IS ASSIGNED.
          rv_reg_off = <lfs_pb_data>-reg_off.
        ELSE.
          rv_reg_off = 'OTHR'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      rv_reg_off = 'OTHR'.
    ENDIF.

** EOC Azure 20231

  ENDMETHOD.                    "get_region

*  populate demand based on the supply ratio
  METHOD populate_demand_qty.

    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF lty_mm_qty,
        matnr TYPE /sapapo/matnr,
        month TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
        qty   TYPE /sapapo/snpdeliv,
      END OF lty_mm_qty,
      BEGIN OF lty_src_mm_qty,
        locno_fr TYPE /sapapo/locno,
        matnr    TYPE /sapapo/matnr,
        month    TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
        qty      TYPE /sapapo/snpdeliv,
      END OF lty_src_mm_qty.

    DATA:
      lv_fieldname        TYPE fieldname,
      lv_locno            TYPE /sapapo/locno,
      ls_fin_fields       TYPE gty_fin_fields,
      ls_fin_qty          TYPE gty_fin_qty,
      ls_subtot           TYPE gty_subtot,
      lv_unfulfilled_dmd  TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 3,
      lv_tmp_unfulfilled_dmd  TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 3,
      lv_month            TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      lv_supply           TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 3,
      lv_prodqty_tot_dmd     TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 3,
      lv_prodqty_tot_supply  TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 3,
      lv_dmd_ratio_used   TYPE abap_bool,
      lt_supply_src_sum   TYPE SORTED TABLE OF lty_src_mm_qty WITH UNIQUE KEY locno_fr matnr month,
      lt_supply_out_sum   TYPE SORTED TABLE OF lty_mm_qty WITH UNIQUE KEY matnr month,
      lt_demand_in_sum    TYPE SORTED TABLE OF lty_mm_qty WITH UNIQUE KEY matnr month,
      ls_supply_src_sum   TYPE lty_src_mm_qty,
      ls_supply_out_sum   TYPE lty_mm_qty,
      ls_demand_in_sum    TYPE lty_mm_qty,
*      ls_subtot           TYPE gty_subtot,
      lo_struct           TYPE REF TO data.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_reg_off>       TYPE gty_reg_off_desc,
      <lfs_tab_supply>    TYPE SORTED TABLE,
      <lfs_outtab>        TYPE ANY TABLE,
      <lfs_outtab_struct> TYPE any,
      <lfs_comp>        TYPE any,
      <lfs_comp2>       TYPE any,
      <lfs_subtotal>    TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_customer>    TYPE gty_cust_dmd_inp,
      <lfs_depo>        TYPE gty_depo_supply,
      <lfs_subtotal_s>  TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_input_dmd>   TYPE gty_input_demand,
      <lfs_output_dmd>  TYPE gty_output_demand,
      <lfs_sply_loc>    TYPE gty_supply_dmn_cust_loc,
      <lfs_supply_src_sum> TYPE lty_src_mm_qty,
      <lfs_supply_out_sum> TYPE lty_mm_qty,
      <lfs_demand_in_sum>  TYPE lty_mm_qty,
      <lfs_fin_sort>    TYPE any.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Populating Demand Qty by Suppled Ratio...'(060)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    ASSIGN lo_tab_sorted->* TO <lfs_tab_supply>.
    IF <lfs_tab_supply> IS NOT ASSIGNED.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    ASSIGN lo_outtab->* TO <lfs_outtab>.
    IF <lfs_outtab> IS NOT ASSIGNED.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

*   Pre-aggregate once to avoid repeated loops per output row.
    CLEAR: lt_supply_src_sum, lt_supply_out_sum, lt_demand_in_sum.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_sply_loc>.
    LOOP AT gt_supply_dmn_cust_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_sply_loc>.
      CLEAR ls_supply_src_sum.
      ls_supply_src_sum-locno_fr = <lfs_sply_loc>-locno_fr.
      ls_supply_src_sum-matnr    = <lfs_sply_loc>-matnr.
      ls_supply_src_sum-month    = <lfs_sply_loc>-cal_month.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_src_sum>.
      READ TABLE lt_supply_src_sum ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_src_sum>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          locno_fr = ls_supply_src_sum-locno_fr
          matnr    = ls_supply_src_sum-matnr
          month    = ls_supply_src_sum-month.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_src_sum> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_supply_src_sum>-qty = <lfs_supply_src_sum>-qty + <lfs_sply_loc>-trans.
      ELSE.
        ls_supply_src_sum-qty = <lfs_sply_loc>-trans.
        INSERT ls_supply_src_sum INTO TABLE lt_supply_src_sum.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_output_dmd>.
    LOOP AT gt_output_dmd ASSIGNING <lfs_output_dmd>.
      CLEAR ls_supply_out_sum.
      ls_supply_out_sum-matnr = <lfs_output_dmd>-matnr.
      ls_supply_out_sum-month = <lfs_output_dmd>-month.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_out_sum>.
      READ TABLE lt_supply_out_sum ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_out_sum>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          matnr = ls_supply_out_sum-matnr
          month = ls_supply_out_sum-month.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_out_sum> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_supply_out_sum>-qty = <lfs_supply_out_sum>-qty + <lfs_output_dmd>-deliv.
      ELSE.
        ls_supply_out_sum-qty = <lfs_output_dmd>-deliv.
        INSERT ls_supply_out_sum INTO TABLE lt_supply_out_sum.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_input_dmd>.
    LOOP AT gt_input_dmd ASSIGNING <lfs_input_dmd>.
      CLEAR ls_demand_in_sum.
      ls_demand_in_sum-matnr = <lfs_input_dmd>-matnr.
      ls_demand_in_sum-month = <lfs_input_dmd>-month.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_demand_in_sum>.
      READ TABLE lt_demand_in_sum ASSIGNING <lfs_demand_in_sum>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          matnr = ls_demand_in_sum-matnr
          month = ls_demand_in_sum-month.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_demand_in_sum> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_demand_in_sum>-qty = <lfs_demand_in_sum>-qty + <lfs_input_dmd>-deman.
      ELSE.
        ls_demand_in_sum-qty = <lfs_input_dmd>-deman.
        INSERT ls_demand_in_sum INTO TABLE lt_demand_in_sum.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    CLEAR gt_subtot_dmd_src.
    LOOP AT <lfs_tab_supply> ASSIGNING <lfs_fin_sort>.
      CLEAR: ls_fin_fields, ls_fin_qty.
      FREE lo_struct.
      CREATE DATA lo_struct TYPE HANDLE go_desc_struct.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_outtab_struct>.
      ASSIGN lo_struct->* TO <lfs_outtab_struct>.
      IF <lfs_outtab_struct> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields =  <lfs_comp>.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_qty =  <lfs_comp>.
          CLEAR: ls_fin_qty-tot_supply, ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_reg_off>.
        LOOP AT gt_reg_off_desc ASSIGNING <lfs_reg_off>.
          CLEAR lv_supply.

          CLEAR lv_fieldname.
          lv_fieldname = 'S_' && <lfs_reg_off>-reg_off.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
          ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_fieldname OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp>.
          IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
            UNASSIGN <lfs_comp2>.
            ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_fieldname OF STRUCTURE <lfs_outtab_struct> TO <lfs_comp2>.
            IF <lfs_comp2> IS ASSIGNED.
              lv_supply = <lfs_comp2> = <lfs_comp>.
            ENDIF.
            ls_fin_qty-tot_supply = ls_fin_qty-tot_supply + <lfs_comp>.
          ENDIF.

          CLEAR lv_fieldname.
          lv_fieldname = 'D_' && <lfs_reg_off>-reg_off.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
          ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_fieldname OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp>.
          IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
            UNASSIGN <lfs_comp2>.
            ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_fieldname OF STRUCTURE <lfs_outtab_struct> TO <lfs_comp2>.
            IF <lfs_comp2> IS ASSIGNED.
              CLEAR lv_dmd_ratio_used.
              UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
              READ TABLE gt_subtot_reg_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
                WITH TABLE KEY
                  supply_loc = space
                  matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
                  region = <lfs_reg_off>-reg_off
                  month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth
                  indicator = 'SNZ'.
              IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED AND lv_supply IS NOT INITIAL.
                UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal_s>.
                READ TABLE gt_subtot_reg_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal_s>
                WITH TABLE KEY
                  supply_loc = space
*                  supply_loc = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
                  matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
                  region = <lfs_reg_off>-reg_off
                  month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth
                  indicator = 'S'.
                IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal_s> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_subtotal_s>-supply_sum IS NOT INITIAL.
                  <lfs_comp2> = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum * lv_supply / <lfs_subtotal_s>-supply_sum.
                  lv_dmd_ratio_used = abap_true.
*                IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal_s> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum IS NOT INITIAL.
*                  <lfs_comp2> = <lfs_comp> * lv_supply / <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum.
                ENDIF.
              ENDIF.

              IF lv_dmd_ratio_used = abap_false.
                <lfs_comp2> = <lfs_comp>.
              ENDIF.

              CLEAR: lv_locno, lv_month.
              lv_locno = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
              lv_month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
*              READ TABLE gt_loc TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
*                WITH TABLE KEY skey3 COMPONENTS
*                  locno = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
*                  loctype = gc_loctyp_plant.
*              IF sy-subrc = 0.
              CLEAR lv_unfulfilled_dmd.
              LOOP AT ls_fin_qty-customer ASSIGNING <lfs_customer>
                  WHERE dmd_supplied = abap_false AND region = <lfs_reg_off>-reg_off.
                UNASSIGN <lfs_depo>.
                READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo>
                WITH TABLE KEY
                  depot = <lfs_customer>-customer
                  matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
                  month = '000000'.
                IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_depo> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo>-lane_count IS NOT INITIAL.
*                  lv_unfulfilled_dmd = lv_unfulfilled_dmd + ( <lfs_customer>-dmd / <lfs_depo>-lane_count ).
                  lv_tmp_unfulfilled_dmd = ( <lfs_customer>-dmd / <lfs_depo>-lane_count ).

                  READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo>
                    WITH TABLE KEY
                      depot = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
                      matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
                      month = '000000'.
                  IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_depo> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo>-lane_count IS NOT INITIAL.
                    lv_tmp_unfulfilled_dmd = lv_tmp_unfulfilled_dmd / <lfs_depo>-lane_count.
                  ENDIF.

                  lv_unfulfilled_dmd = lv_unfulfilled_dmd + lv_tmp_unfulfilled_dmd.
                ELSE.
                  lv_unfulfilled_dmd = lv_unfulfilled_dmd + <lfs_customer>-dmd.
                ENDIF.
              ENDLOOP.
              <lfs_comp2> = <lfs_comp2> + lv_unfulfilled_dmd.
*              ELSE.
*
*                lv_locno = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
*                lv_month = '000000'.
*                UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
*                READ TABLE gt_subtot_reg_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
*                  WITH TABLE KEY
*                    supply_loc = lv_locno
*                    matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
*                    region = <lfs_reg_off>-reg_off
*                    month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth
*                    indicator = 'SZ'.
*                IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
*                  UNASSIGN <lfs_depo>.
*                  READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo>
*                  WITH TABLE KEY
*                    depot = lv_locno
*                    matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
*                    month = lv_month.
*                  IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_depo> IS ASSIGNED AND <lfs_depo>-lane_count IS NOT INITIAL.
*                    <lfs_comp2> = <lfs_comp2> + ( <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum / <lfs_depo>-lane_count ).
*                  ENDIF.
*                ENDIF.
*              ENDIF.
              ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd = ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd + <lfs_comp2>.

            ENDIF.

          ENDIF.

        ENDLOOP.

*       For PROD_QTY-only rows (no regional D_/S_), derive totals from demand/supply tables
*       so Total Demand/Total Supply do not remain zero.
        IF ls_fin_fields-added_by_method = 'PROD_QTY'
           AND ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd IS INITIAL
           AND ls_fin_qty-tot_supply IS INITIAL.

          CLEAR: lv_prodqty_tot_dmd, lv_prodqty_tot_supply.

*         Prefer lane-based supply for this source plant/material/month
          UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_src_sum>.
          READ TABLE lt_supply_src_sum ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_src_sum>
            WITH TABLE KEY
              locno_fr = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
              matnr    = ls_fin_fields-matnr
              month    = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_src_sum> IS ASSIGNED.
            lv_prodqty_tot_supply = <lfs_supply_src_sum>-qty.
          ENDIF.

*         Fallback to customer-level delivered qty when lane split is unavailable
          IF lv_prodqty_tot_supply IS INITIAL.
            UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_out_sum>.
            READ TABLE lt_supply_out_sum ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_out_sum>
              WITH TABLE KEY
                matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
                month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_out_sum> IS ASSIGNED.
              lv_prodqty_tot_supply = <lfs_supply_out_sum>-qty.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

*         Demand from ET_DEMAND aggregation for same material/month
          UNASSIGN <lfs_demand_in_sum>.
          READ TABLE lt_demand_in_sum ASSIGNING <lfs_demand_in_sum>
            WITH TABLE KEY
              matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
              month = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_demand_in_sum> IS ASSIGNED.
            lv_prodqty_tot_dmd = <lfs_demand_in_sum>-qty.
          ENDIF.

*         If demand is not present but supply exists, keep both in sync as a safe fallback
          IF lv_prodqty_tot_dmd IS INITIAL AND lv_prodqty_tot_supply IS NOT INITIAL.
            lv_prodqty_tot_dmd = lv_prodqty_tot_supply.
          ENDIF.

          ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd    = lv_prodqty_tot_dmd.
          ls_fin_qty-tot_supply = lv_prodqty_tot_supply.

*         Mirror totals into OTHR regional columns for ALV display
          UNASSIGN <lfs_comp2>.
          ASSIGN COMPONENT 'D_OTHR' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_outtab_struct> TO <lfs_comp2>.
          IF <lfs_comp2> IS ASSIGNED.
            <lfs_comp2> = lv_prodqty_tot_dmd.
          ENDIF.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_comp2>.
          ASSIGN COMPONENT 'S_OTHR' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_outtab_struct> TO <lfs_comp2>.
          IF <lfs_comp2> IS ASSIGNED.
            <lfs_comp2> = lv_prodqty_tot_supply.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
*        IF ls_fin_fields-added_by_method IS NOT INITIAL AND NOT ls_fin_fields-src_plant = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
        READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_src ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            supply_loc  = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            matnr       = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            month       = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum    = <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum + ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd.
          <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count = <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count + 1.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR ls_subtot.
          ls_subtot-supply_loc  = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
          ls_subtot-matnr       = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
          ls_subtot-month       = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
          ls_subtot-dmd_sum     = ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd.
          ls_subtot-lane_count  = 1.
          INSERT ls_subtot INTO TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_src.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
        READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_src ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            supply_loc  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
            matnr       = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            month       = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count_con = <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count_con + 1.
        ENDIF.

*        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
        READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_src ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            supply_loc = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
*            region     = ''
            month      = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
*          <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum + ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd + ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd.
          <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum + ls_fin_qty-tot_supply + ls_fin_qty-captive_supply.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR ls_subtot.
          ls_subtot-supply_loc = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
          ls_subtot-matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
          ls_subtot-month      = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
          ls_subtot-supply_sum = ls_fin_qty-tot_supply + ls_fin_qty-captive_supply.
          INSERT ls_subtot INTO TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_src.
        ENDIF.


        UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
        READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            supply_loc = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
*            region     = ''
            month      = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
*          <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum + ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd + ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd.
          <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum + ls_fin_qty-tot_supply + ls_fin_qty-captive_supply.
          <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum    = <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum + ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd + ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd.
          <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count = <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count + 1.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR ls_subtot.
          ls_subtot-supply_loc = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant. "ls_fin_fields-src_plant. "" Added by KPIT_095 TR: AD1K912633
          ls_subtot-matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
          ls_subtot-month      = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
          ls_subtot-supply_sum = ls_fin_qty-tot_supply + ls_fin_qty-captive_supply.
          ls_subtot-dmd_sum = ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd + ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd.
          ls_subtot-lane_count  = 1.
*          INSERT ls_subtot INTO TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_src.
          INSERT ls_subtot INTO TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_supply.    "" Added by KPIT_095 TR: AD1K912633


*          APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_subtot_dmd_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>.
*          IF <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
*            <lfs_subtotal>-supply_loc = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
*            <lfs_subtotal>-matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
*            <lfs_subtotal>-month      = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
**            <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd + ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd.
*            <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum = ls_fin_qty-tot_supply + ls_fin_qty-captive_supply.
*          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.

        ls_fin_fields-sessionname = p_orun.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_outtab_struct> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_fields.
        ENDIF.
*        ls_fin_qty-stock = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk - ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk.
*        ls_fin_qty-stock = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk - ls_fin_qty-opng_stk.
        ls_fin_qty-stock = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk - ls_fin_qty-opng_stk.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_outtab_struct> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.

        INSERT <lfs_outtab_struct> INTO TABLE <lfs_outtab>.

      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "populate_demand_qty

*  populate prod qty, opening & closing stock
  METHOD populate_other_qty.

    DATA:
      ls_fin_fields  TYPE gty_fin_fields,
      lv_cal_mth     TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      lv_tmp_cal_mth TYPE /bi0/oicalmonth,
      ls_fin_qty     TYPE gty_fin_qty,
      lv_n_rows TYPE i.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_outtab>        TYPE STANDARD TABLE,
*      <lfs_depo_supply>   TYPE gty_depo_supply,
      <lfs_comp>          TYPE any,
      <lfs_comp_tab>          TYPE ANY TABLE,
      <lfs_buckets>       TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_subtotal>      TYPE gty_subtot,
      <lfs_fin_sort_prev> TYPE any,
      <lfs_fin_sort>      TYPE any.


    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Populating Other Qty by Demand Ratio...'(061)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets> INDEX 1.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      lv_cal_mth = <lfs_buckets>-cal_month.
    ENDIF.

    ASSIGN lo_outtab->* TO <lfs_outtab>.
    IF <lfs_outtab> IS NOT ASSIGNED.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    DELETE <lfs_outtab> WHERE ('MATNR NOT IN S_MATNR[]').
    SORT <lfs_outtab> BY ('SRC_PLANT') ('SUPPLY_PLANT') ('MATNR') ('CAL_MTH').
    LOOP AT <lfs_outtab> ASSIGNING <lfs_fin_sort>.
      CLEAR: ls_fin_fields, ls_fin_qty.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp>.
      IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields =  <lfs_comp>.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp>.
      IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_qty =  <lfs_comp>.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_comp_tab>.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT 'CUSTOMER' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp_tab>.
      IF <lfs_comp_tab> IS ASSIGNED.
        DESCRIBE TABLE <lfs_comp_tab> LINES lv_n_rows.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'CUSTOMER_COUNT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_qty-customer_count =  lv_n_rows.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
      READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_src ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          supply_loc  = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
          matnr       = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          month       = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
        IF <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum IS NOT INITIAL.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty * ( ( ls_fin_qty-tot_supply + ls_fin_qty-captive_supply ) / <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum ).
        ELSEIF <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum IS NOT INITIAL.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty * ( ( ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd + ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd ) / <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum ).
        ELSEIF <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count IS NOT INITIAL.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty / <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
      READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_src ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          supply_loc  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
          matnr       = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          month       = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
        IF <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count_con IS NOT INITIAL.
          ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con / <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count_con.
*          ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con / <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count_con.  "Added by Ravi on 28.08.2021
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
      READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
        WITH TABLE KEY
          supply_loc  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
          matnr       = ls_fin_fields-matnr
          month       = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.

        ELSEIF <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum IS NOT INITIAL.
          ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk * ( ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd + ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd ) / <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum.
*        elseif <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count is NOT INITIAL.
*          ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk / <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count.
*          commented as opng & clsg stk is coming 0 when dmd, supply is 0
*          READ TABLE gt_depo_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_depo_supply>

*              matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr

*            ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk / <lfs_depo_supply>-lane_count.
*          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR lv_tmp_cal_mth.
      IF lv_cal_mth = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        lv_tmp_cal_mth = lv_cal_mth.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_subtotal>.
        READ TABLE gt_subtot_dmd_supply ASSIGNING <lfs_subtotal>
          WITH TABLE KEY
            supply_loc = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
            matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            month      = lv_cal_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_subtotal> IS ASSIGNED.
          IF <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum IS NOT INITIAL.
            ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk * ( ls_fin_qty-tot_supply + ls_fin_qty-captive_supply ) / <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum.
            ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk * ( ls_fin_qty-tot_supply + ls_fin_qty-captive_supply ) / <lfs_subtotal>-supply_sum.
          ELSEIF <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum IS NOT INITIAL.
            ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk * ( ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd + ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd ) / <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum.
            ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk * ( ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd + ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd ) / <lfs_subtotal>-dmd_sum.
*          elseif <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count is NOT INITIAL.
*            ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk / <lfs_subtotal>-lane_count.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        lv_tmp_cal_mth = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth - 1.
        READ TABLE <lfs_outtab> ASSIGNING <lfs_fin_sort_prev>
          WITH KEY
            ('SRC_PLANT')     = ls_fin_fields-src_plant
            ('SUPPLY_PLANT')  = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant
            ('MATNR')         = ls_fin_fields-matnr
            ('CAL_MTH')       = lv_tmp_cal_mth
          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_fin_sort_prev> IS ASSIGNED.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
          ASSIGN COMPONENT 'CLSG_STK' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort_prev> TO <lfs_comp>.
          IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
            ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = <lfs_comp>.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

*      ls_fin_qty-stock = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk - ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk.
      ls_fin_qty-stock = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk - ls_fin_qty-opng_stk.

      ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_derived = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk + ls_fin_qty-prod_qty
                       - ls_fin_qty-tot_supply - ls_fin_qty-captive_supply
                       + ls_fin_qty-vendor_sup_qty.
      IF ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_derived = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk.
        ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_ind = icon_green_light.
      ELSE.
        ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_ind = icon_red_light.
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_fin_sort> TO <lfs_comp>.
      IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
      ENDIF.

      me->append_for_db_upd( <lfs_fin_sort> ).

    ENDLOOP.

        IF iv_captive = abap_true.

          READ TABLE gt_loc ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>
            WITH KEY skey3 COMPONENTS
              locno = iv_src_plant
              loctype = gc_loctyp_vendor.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc> IS ASSIGNED.
            "@Start of changes by Ravi M on 27.08.2021
            UNASSIGN <lfs_supply_arcmat>.
            READ TABLE gt_supply_arcmat ASSIGNING <lfs_supply_arcmat>
              WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS
                matnr     = is_input_dmd-matnr
                locno_fr  = iv_src_plant
                cal_month = is_input_dmd-month.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_supply_arcmat> IS ASSIGNED.
              ls_fin_qty-vendor_sup_qty = <lfs_supply_arcmat>-trans.
            ENDIF.
*            ls_fin_qty-vendor_sup_qty = iv_supply.
            "@End of changes by Ravi M on 27.08.2021
*
            ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd =
            ls_fin_qty-captive_supply = iv_captive_qty.
          ELSE.
            ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd =
          ls_fin_qty-captive_supply = iv_captive_qty.
          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
        READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets>
          WITH KEY cal_month = is_input_dmd-month.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_buckets> IS ASSIGNED.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
          READ TABLE gt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr> "et_locprod
            WITH KEY
              matid = is_input_dmd-matid
              locid = iv_supply_plant_id
              bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke_to
            BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_lpr> IS ASSIGNED.
            CLEAR <lfs_lpr>-sessionid.
            ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = <lfs_lpr>-safty.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        lv_mth = is_input_dmd-month - 1.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
        READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets>
          WITH KEY cal_month = lv_mth.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_buckets> IS ASSIGNED.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
          READ TABLE gt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr> "et_locprod
            WITH KEY
              matid = is_input_dmd-matid
              locid = iv_supply_plant_id
              bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke_to
            BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_lpr> IS ASSIGNED.
            CLEAR <lfs_lpr>-sessionid.
            ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = <lfs_lpr>-cnf_distr_rcpt.
          ENDIF.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_pmlo>.
          READ TABLE gt_snpopmlo ASSIGNING <lfs_pmlo>
            WITH KEY
              matid = is_input_dmd-matid
              locid = iv_supply_plant_id
              bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke_to
            BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_pmlo> IS ASSIGNED.
            ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk + <lfs_pmlo>-stock.
          ENDIF.

        ELSE.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
          READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets> INDEX 1.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_buckets> IS ASSIGNED.
            UNASSIGN <lfs_bg_div>.
            READ TABLE gt_bg_div ASSIGNING <lfs_bg_div>
              WITH KEY
                div = ls_fin_fields-div.
*                div = is_input_dmd-div.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_bg_div> IS ASSIGNED.
              lv_bsg = <lfs_bg_div>-bsg.
            ELSE.
              CLEAR lv_bsg.
            ENDIF.

            UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
            READ TABLE gt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr> "et_locprod
              WITH KEY
                matid = is_input_dmd-matid
                locid = iv_supply_plant_id
                bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke
              BINARY SEARCH.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_lpr> IS ASSIGNED.
              CLEAR <lfs_lpr>-sessionid.
              ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = <lfs_lpr>-cnf_distr_rcpt.
            ENDIF.

            CASE lv_bsg.
              WHEN 'CHM' OR 'PBR' OR 'PMR'. "polymer
*                UNASSIGN <lfs_lpr>.
*                READ TABLE gt_snpoplpr ASSIGNING <lfs_lpr> "et_locprod
*                  WITH KEY
*                    matid = is_input_dmd-matid
*                    locid = iv_supply_plant_id
*                    bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke
*                  BINARY SEARCH.
*                IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_lpr> IS ASSIGNED.
*                  ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = <lfs_lpr>-cnf_distr_rcpt.
*                ENDIF.
              WHEN OTHERS. "polyester
                UNASSIGN <lfs_lma_loc_mat>.
                READ TABLE gt_snpoplma ASSIGNING <lfs_lma_loc_mat> "et_locmat
                  WITH KEY
                    matid = is_input_dmd-matid
                    locid = iv_supply_plant_id
                  BINARY SEARCH.
                IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_lma_loc_mat> IS ASSIGNED.
                  ls_fin_qty-opng_stk = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk + <lfs_lma_loc_mat>-stock.
                  CLEAR <lfs_lma_loc_mat>-sessionid. "mark as entry added
                ENDIF.
            ENDCASE.

          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        """Closing Stock
        UNASSIGN <lfs_buckets>.
        READ TABLE gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_buckets>
          WITH KEY cal_month = is_input_dmd-month.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_buckets> IS ASSIGNED.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_pmlo>.
          READ TABLE gt_snpopmlo ASSIGNING <lfs_pmlo> "IT_LOCMAT
            WITH KEY
              matid = is_input_dmd-matid
              locid = iv_supply_plant_id
              bucke = <lfs_buckets>-bucke_to
            BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_pmlo> IS ASSIGNED.
            ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk           = <lfs_pmlo>-stock.
            ls_fin_qty-sfty_stk_shortfall = <lfs_pmlo>-viosf.

            CLEAR <lfs_pmlo>-sessionid. "mark as entry added
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*        IF iv_add_prod_plant = abap_true." Commented by KPIT_095
        UNASSIGN <lfs_mat_prod_plant>.
        READ TABLE gt_mat_prod_plant ASSIGNING <lfs_mat_prod_plant>
          WITH TABLE KEY matnr = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_mat_prod_plant> IS ASSIGNED.
          ls_fin_fields-derived_plant_list = <lfs_mat_prod_plant>-plant_list.
        ENDIF.
*        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_fields.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.

        INSERT <lfs_final> INTO TABLE <lfs_table>.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "append_to_outtab_dyn

**  append row for custom table update
  METHOD append_for_db_upd.

    DATA :
      lv_fieldname  TYPE fieldname,
      ls_fin_fields TYPE gty_fin_fields,
      ls_fin_qty    TYPE gty_fin_qty,
      lv_demand         TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 3,
      lv_supply         TYPE p LENGTH 16 DECIMALS 3,
      lv_item_written   TYPE abap_bool,
*      ls_tmp_tbl TYPE zst_mn_matbal.
      ls_tmp_tbl TYPE gty_tmp_tbl.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_comp>        TYPE any,
      <lfs_ref_off>     TYPE gty_reg_off_desc,
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>  TYPE zapo_matbal_hrd,
      <lfs_matbal_item> TYPE zapo_matbal_item.
*      <lfs_tmp_tbl> TYPE gty_tmp_tbl.

    CLEAR: ls_fin_fields, ls_fin_qty.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
    ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE is_data TO <lfs_comp>.
    IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
      ls_fin_fields =  <lfs_comp>.
    ENDIF.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
    ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE is_data TO <lfs_comp>.
    IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
      ls_fin_qty =  <lfs_comp>.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR ls_tmp_tbl.
    ls_tmp_tbl-div          = ls_fin_fields-div.
    ls_tmp_tbl-src_plant    = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
    ls_tmp_tbl-supply_plant = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
    ls_tmp_tbl-matnr        = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
    ls_tmp_tbl-cal_mth      = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_matbal_hrd>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_matbal_hrd ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_hrd>.
    IF <lfs_matbal_hrd> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-sessionname      = gs_snpop_log_info-sessionname.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-div              = ls_fin_fields-div.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-plant_desc       = ls_fin_fields-plant_desc.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-derived_plant_list = ls_fin_fields-derived_plant_list.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-meins            = ls_fin_fields-meins.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-scr_plant        = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-supply_plant     = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-matnr            = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-cal_month        = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-old_matnr        = ls_fin_fields-merge.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-lustre           = ls_fin_fields-pdh1_desc.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-denier           = ls_fin_fields-pdh2_desc.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-filment          = ls_fin_fields-pdh3_desc.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr2_desc       = ls_fin_fields-mvgr2_desc.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr3_desc       = ls_fin_fields-mvgr3_desc.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr4_desc       = ls_fin_fields-mvgr4_desc.
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr5_desc       = ls_fin_fields-mvgr5_desc.
      IF ls_fin_fields-meins = 'KG'.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-tota_demand      = ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd         / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-total_supply     = ls_fin_qty-tot_supply      / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-prod_qty         = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty        / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-opng_stk         = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk        / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-clsg_stk         = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk        / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_dmd      = ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd     / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_supply   = ls_fin_qty-captive_supply  / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-stock            = ls_fin_qty-stock           / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-vendor_sup_qty   = ls_fin_qty-vendor_sup_qty  / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-prod_qty_con     = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con    / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_dmd_con  = ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd_con / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-cavtive_sup_con  = ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con / 1000.
***        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-nonprime_stock   = ls_fin_qty-nonprime_stock  / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-target_clsng_stk   = ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk / 1000.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-sfty_stk_shortfall = ls_fin_qty-sfty_stk_shortfall / 1000.
      ELSE.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-tota_demand      = ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-total_supply     = ls_fin_qty-tot_supply.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-prod_qty         = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-opng_stk         = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-clsg_stk         = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_dmd      = ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_supply   = ls_fin_qty-captive_supply.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-stock            = ls_fin_qty-stock.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-vendor_sup_qty   = ls_fin_qty-vendor_sup_qty.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_dmd_con  = ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd_con.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-cavtive_sup_con  = ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-prod_qty_con     = ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con.
***        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-nonprime_stock   = ls_fin_qty-nonprime_stock.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-target_clsng_stk   = ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk.
        <lfs_matbal_hrd>-sfty_stk_shortfall = ls_fin_qty-sfty_stk_shortfall.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
** BOC  Azure 20231
    CLEAR lv_item_written.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_ref_off>.
    LOOP AT gt_reg_off_desc ASSIGNING <lfs_ref_off>.
      CLEAR: lv_demand, lv_supply.
      ls_tmp_tbl-region = <lfs_ref_off>-reg_off.
      lv_fieldname = 'D_' && <lfs_ref_off>-reg_off.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_fieldname OF STRUCTURE is_data TO <lfs_comp>.
      IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_demand       = <lfs_comp>.
        ls_tmp_tbl-qty_type = 'D'.
        ls_tmp_tbl-qty = <lfs_comp>.
        APPEND ls_tmp_tbl TO gt_tmp_tbl.
      ENDIF.

      lv_fieldname = 'S_' && <lfs_ref_off>-reg_off.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_fieldname OF STRUCTURE is_data TO <lfs_comp>.
      IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_supply       = <lfs_comp>.
        ls_tmp_tbl-qty_type = 'S'.
        ls_tmp_tbl-qty = <lfs_comp>.
        APPEND ls_tmp_tbl TO gt_tmp_tbl.
      ENDIF.

      IF lv_demand IS INITIAL AND lv_supply IS INITIAL.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_matbal_item>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_matbal_item ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_item>.
      IF <lfs_matbal_item> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-sessionname      = gs_snpop_log_info-sessionname.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-div              = ls_fin_fields-div.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-scr_plant        = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-supply_plant     = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-matnr            = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-reg_off          = <lfs_ref_off>-reg_off.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-cal_month        = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF ls_fin_fields-meins = 'KG'.
          <lfs_matbal_item>-demand_qty       = lv_demand / 1000.
          <lfs_matbal_item>-supply_qty       = lv_supply / 1000.
        ELSE.
          <lfs_matbal_item>-demand_qty       = lv_demand.
          <lfs_matbal_item>-supply_qty       = lv_supply.
        ENDIF.
        lv_item_written = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

*   PROD_QTY rows: persist OTHR region when only header totals were populated
    IF lv_item_written = abap_false
       AND ls_fin_fields-added_by_method = 'PROD_QTY'
       AND ( ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd IS NOT INITIAL OR ls_fin_qty-tot_supply IS NOT INITIAL ).
      UNASSIGN <lfs_matbal_item>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_matbal_item ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_item>.
      IF <lfs_matbal_item> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-sessionname  = gs_snpop_log_info-sessionname.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-div          = ls_fin_fields-div.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-scr_plant    = ls_fin_fields-src_plant.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-supply_plant = ls_fin_fields-supply_plant.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-matnr        = ls_fin_fields-matnr.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-reg_off       = 'OTHR'.
        <lfs_matbal_item>-cal_month     = ls_fin_fields-cal_mth.
        IF ls_fin_fields-meins = 'KG'.
          <lfs_matbal_item>-demand_qty = ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd / 1000.
          <lfs_matbal_item>-supply_qty = ls_fin_qty-tot_supply / 1000.
        ELSE.
          <lfs_matbal_item>-demand_qty = ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd.
          <lfs_matbal_item>-supply_qty = ls_fin_qty-tot_supply.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "append_for_db_upd

*  save material balance to custom table
  METHOD save_to_table.

    DATA:
      lv_msg     TYPE string,
      lv_lck_hdr TYPE boolean,
      lv_lck_itm TYPE boolean.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Saving Output in Tables...'(062)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

*    DELETE gt_matbal_item WHERE demand_qty IS INITIAL AND supply_qty IS INITIAL.

    CALL FUNCTION 'ENQUEUE_E_TABLE'
      EXPORTING
        tabname        = 'ZAPO_MATBAL_HRD'
      EXCEPTIONS
        foreign_lock   = 1
        system_failure = 2
        OTHERS         = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      lv_lck_hdr = abap_true.
    ELSE.
      lv_msg = 'Table (ZAPO_MATBAL_HRD) Lock not Acquired'(063).
    ENDIF.

    CALL FUNCTION 'ENQUEUE_E_TABLE'
      EXPORTING
        tabname        = 'ZAPO_MATBAL_ITEM'
      EXCEPTIONS
        foreign_lock   = 1
        system_failure = 2
        OTHERS         = 3.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      lv_lck_itm = abap_true.
    ELSE.
      lv_msg = 'Table (ZAPO_MATBAL_ITEM) Lock not Acquired'(064).
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_lck_hdr = abap_true AND lv_lck_itm = abap_true.

      DELETE FROM zapo_matbal_hrd WHERE sessionname = p_orun AND div IN s_div[].
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        DELETE FROM zapo_matbal_item WHERE sessionname = p_orun AND div IN s_div[].
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          COMMIT WORK.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      MODIFY zapo_matbal_hrd FROM TABLE gt_matbal_hrd.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        MODIFY zapo_matbal_item FROM TABLE gt_matbal_item.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          COMMIT WORK.
          lv_msg = 'Data Saved Succesfully'(065).
        ELSE.
          ROLLBACK WORK.
          lv_msg = 'Data not Saved'(066).
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        ROLLBACK WORK.
        lv_msg = 'Data not Saved'(066).
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_lck_itm  = abap_true.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DEQUEUE_E_TABLE'
        EXPORTING
          tabname = 'ZAPO_MATBAL_ITEM'.
    ENDIF.

    IF lv_lck_hdr = abap_true.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DEQUEUE_E_TABLE'
        EXPORTING
          tabname = 'ZAPO_MATBAL_HDR'.
    ENDIF.

    IF sy-batch = abap_true.
      MESSAGE lv_msg TYPE 'S'.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "save_to_table

*  read material group description
  METHOD read_mat_grp_desc.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_tab_matgrp> TYPE STANDARD TABLE,
      <lfs_bezei> TYPE any,
      <lfs_matgrp> TYPE any.

    ASSIGN (iv_int_tabname) TO <lfs_tab_matgrp>.
    IF <lfs_tab_matgrp> IS NOT ASSIGNED.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE <lfs_tab_matgrp> ASSIGNING <lfs_matgrp>
      WITH KEY
        (iv_field) = iv_fieldval
      BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      ASSIGN COMPONENT 'BEZEI' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_matgrp> TO <lfs_bezei>.
      IF <lfs_bezei> IS ASSIGNED.
        rv_bezei = <lfs_bezei>.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "read_mat_grp_desc

*  transposing region fetch from table to columns
  METHOD process_material_balance.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_matbal_hrd>  TYPE zapo_matbal_hrd,
      <lfs_matbal_item> TYPE zapo_matbal_item,
      <lfs_final>       TYPE any,
      <lfs_table>       TYPE STANDARD TABLE,
      <lfs_comp>        TYPE any.

    DATA:
      lo_struct     TYPE REF TO data ##needed,
      ls_fin_fields TYPE gty_fin_fields,
      ls_fin_qty    TYPE gty_fin_qty,
      lv_fieldname  TYPE fieldname,
      lv_reg_off    TYPE /bic/oip_reg_off.


    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.
    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Populating Output Table...'(079)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    ASSIGN lo_outtab->* TO <lfs_table>.
    SORT gt_matbal_hrd BY sessionname div scr_plant supply_plant matnr cal_month.
    SORT gt_matbal_item BY sessionname div scr_plant supply_plant matnr cal_month reg_off.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_matbal_item>.
    LOOP AT gt_matbal_item ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_item>.
*      CLEAR lv_reg_off.
      lv_reg_off = <lfs_matbal_item>-reg_off.
      AT NEW cal_month.
        CLEAR: ls_fin_fields, ls_fin_qty.
        UNASSIGN: <lfs_matbal_hrd>, <lfs_final>.
        READ TABLE gt_matbal_hrd ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_hrd>
          WITH KEY
            sessionname  = <lfs_matbal_item>-sessionname
            div          = <lfs_matbal_item>-div
            scr_plant    = <lfs_matbal_item>-scr_plant
            supply_plant = <lfs_matbal_item>-supply_plant
            matnr        = <lfs_matbal_item>-matnr
            cal_month    = <lfs_matbal_item>-cal_month
          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matbal_hrd> IS ASSIGNED.
          CLEAR: ls_fin_fields, ls_fin_qty.
          CLEAR <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mandt.
          UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
          APPEND INITIAL LINE TO <lfs_table> ASSIGNING <lfs_final>.
          IF <lfs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
            ls_fin_fields-sessionname  = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-sessionname.
            ls_fin_fields-div          = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-div.
            ls_fin_fields-plant_desc   = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-plant_desc.
            ls_fin_fields-src_plant    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-scr_plant.
            ls_fin_fields-supply_plant = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-supply_plant.
            ls_fin_fields-matnr        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-matnr.
            ls_fin_fields-meins        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-meins.
            ls_fin_fields-derived_plant_list  = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-derived_plant_list.
            ls_fin_fields-merge        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-old_matnr.
            ls_fin_fields-pdh1_desc    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-lustre.
            ls_fin_fields-pdh2_desc    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-denier.
            ls_fin_fields-pdh3_desc    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-filment.
            ls_fin_fields-mvgr2_desc   = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr2_desc.
            ls_fin_fields-mvgr3_desc   = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr3_desc.
            ls_fin_fields-mvgr4_desc   = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr4_desc.
            ls_fin_fields-mvgr5_desc   = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr5_desc.

            ls_fin_fields-cal_mth      = <lfs_matbal_item>-cal_month.

            ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd     = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_dmd.
            ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd_con = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_dmd_con.
            ls_fin_qty-captive_supply  = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_supply.
            ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-cavtive_sup_con.
            ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-prod_qty_con.
            ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd         = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-tota_demand.
            ls_fin_qty-tot_supply      = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-total_supply.
            ls_fin_qty-prod_qty        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-prod_qty.
            ls_fin_qty-opng_stk        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-opng_stk.
            ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-clsg_stk.
            ls_fin_qty-stock           = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-stock.
            ls_fin_qty-nonprime_stock  = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-nonprime_stock.
            ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-target_clsng_stk.
            ls_fin_qty-sfty_stk_shortfall = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-sfty_stk_shortfall.
            ls_fin_qty-vendor_sup_qty  = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-vendor_sup_qty.

            ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_derived = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk + ls_fin_qty-prod_qty
                             - ls_fin_qty-tot_supply - ls_fin_qty-captive_supply
                             + ls_fin_qty-vendor_sup_qty.

            IF ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_derived = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk.
              ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_ind = icon_green_light.
            ELSE.
              ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_ind = icon_red_light.
            ENDIF.
            ""BOC BY Kalpesh/Indu 25.07.2024
            CLEAR : gw_mar.
            READ TABLE gt_mar INTO gw_mar WITH KEY matnr = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-matnr BINARY SEARCH.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              ls_fin_fields-matkl = gw_mar-matkl.
            ENDIF.
            ""EOC BY Kalpesh/Indu 25.07.2024
            FREE lo_struct.
            CREATE DATA lo_struct TYPE HANDLE go_desc_struct.

            UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
            ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
            IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
              <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_fields.
            ENDIF.

            UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
            ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
            IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
              <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
            ENDIF.

          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.
      ENDAT.
      IF <lfs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
        lv_fieldname = 'D_' && lv_reg_off.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_fieldname OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = <lfs_matbal_item>-demand_qty.
        ENDIF.

        lv_fieldname = 'S_' && lv_reg_off.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT lv_fieldname OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = <lfs_matbal_item>-supply_qty.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    DELETE gt_matbal_hrd WHERE mandt IS INITIAL.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_matbal_hrd>.
    LOOP AT gt_matbal_hrd ASSIGNING <lfs_matbal_hrd>.
      CLEAR: ls_fin_fields, ls_fin_qty.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO <lfs_table> ASSIGNING <lfs_final>.
      IF <lfs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
        ls_fin_fields-sessionname  = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-sessionname.
        ls_fin_fields-div          = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-div.
        ls_fin_fields-plant_desc   = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-plant_desc.
        ls_fin_fields-src_plant    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-scr_plant.
        ls_fin_fields-supply_plant = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-supply_plant.
        ls_fin_fields-derived_plant_list = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-derived_plant_list.
        ls_fin_fields-matnr        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-matnr.
        ls_fin_fields-meins        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-meins.
        ls_fin_fields-merge        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-old_matnr.
        ls_fin_fields-pdh1_desc    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-lustre.
        ls_fin_fields-pdh2_desc    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-denier.
        ls_fin_fields-pdh3_desc    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-filment.
        ls_fin_fields-mvgr2_desc   = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr2_desc.
        ls_fin_fields-mvgr3_desc   = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr3_desc.
        ls_fin_fields-mvgr4_desc   = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr4_desc.
        ls_fin_fields-mvgr5_desc   = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-mvgr5_desc.

*        ls_fin_fields-cal_mth      = <lfs_matbal_item>-cal_month.
        ls_fin_fields-cal_mth      = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-cal_month.

        ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd     = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_dmd.
        ls_fin_qty-captive_supply  = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_supply.
        ls_fin_qty-tot_dmd         = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-tota_demand.
        ls_fin_qty-tot_supply      = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-total_supply.
        ls_fin_qty-prod_qty        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-prod_qty.
        ls_fin_qty-opng_stk        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-opng_stk.
        ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk        = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-clsg_stk.
        ls_fin_qty-captive_dmd_con = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-captive_dmd_con.
        ls_fin_qty-cavtive_sup_con = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-cavtive_sup_con.
        ls_fin_qty-prod_qty_con    = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-prod_qty_con.
        ls_fin_qty-target_clsng_stk = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-target_clsng_stk.
        ls_fin_qty-sfty_stk_shortfall = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-sfty_stk_shortfall.
        ls_fin_qty-stock           = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-stock.
        ls_fin_qty-nonprime_stock  = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-nonprime_stock.
        ls_fin_qty-vendor_sup_qty  = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-vendor_sup_qty.

        ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_derived = ls_fin_qty-opng_stk + ls_fin_qty-prod_qty
                         - ls_fin_qty-tot_supply - ls_fin_qty-captive_supply
                         + ls_fin_qty-vendor_sup_qty.

        IF ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_derived = ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk.
          ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_ind = icon_green_light.
        ELSE.
          ls_fin_qty-clsg_stk_ind = icon_red_light.
        ENDIF.
        ""BOC BY Kalpesh/Indu 25.07.2024
        CLEAR : gw_mar.
        READ TABLE gt_mar INTO gw_mar WITH KEY matnr = <lfs_matbal_hrd>-matnr BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          ls_fin_fields-matkl = gw_mar-matkl.
        ENDIF.
        ""EOC BY Kalpesh/Indu 25.07.2024
        FREE lo_struct.
        CREATE DATA lo_struct TYPE HANDLE go_desc_struct.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'STRUCT' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_fields.
        ENDIF.

        UNASSIGN <lfs_comp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT 'QTY' OF STRUCTURE <lfs_final> TO <lfs_comp>.
        IF <lfs_comp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_comp> = ls_fin_qty.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "process_material_balance

*  screen validation
  METHOD validate_screen.

    IF NOT ( sy-ucomm = 'ONLI' OR sy-ucomm = space ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    DATA:
      lv_sessionname  TYPE /sapapo/sessionname,
      lv_is_mock_mode TYPE abap_bool.

    lv_is_mock_mode = xsdbool( c_dev = abap_true AND p_orun CP 'TEST*' ).

    IF p_orun IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'Please enter Optimizer Run Name'(018) TYPE 'E'.
    ELSE.
      IF lv_is_mock_mode = abap_true.
        gs_snpop_log_info-sessionname = p_orun.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR lv_sessionname.
        SELECT SINGLE sessionname
          FROM /sapapo/snpopkey
          INTO lv_sessionname
          WHERE sessionname = p_orun.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          MESSAGE 'Invalid Session Name'(052) TYPE 'E'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "validate_screen

*  fetch SNP log master data
  METHOD fetch_material_master_details.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
    EXPORTING
      i_text               = 'Fetching SNP Log Materials...'(003)    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
      i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
      i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
      i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    SELECT key~snpsession sessionname sdate
      FROM /sapapo/snpopkey AS key
      INNER JOIN /sapapo/snpoprun AS run
        ON key~snpsession = run~sessionid
      CLIENT SPECIFIED
      INTO TABLE gt_snpop_log_info
      WHERE run~mandt = sy-mandt AND sessionname = p_orun.

    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT gt_snpop_log_info BY snpsession.
    ELSE.
      MESSAGE 'No Optimizer Log found'(020) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR : gs_snpop_log_info.
    READ TABLE gt_snpop_log_info INTO gs_snpop_log_info INDEX 1.

*    fetch log guids description,
    SELECT *
      FROM /sapapo/snpopiek
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_snpopiek
      WHERE sessionid = gs_snpop_log_info-snpsession.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT gt_snpopiek BY inkey.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT sessionid line_no matid
      FROM /sapapo/snpopmat
      INTO TABLE gt_snpopmat
      WHERE sessionid = gs_snpop_log_info-snpsession.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT gt_snpopmat BY matid.
    ENDIF.

    IF gt_snpopmat IS NOT INITIAL.
      " data from gt_snpopmat is unique no need sort and delete duplicate
      SELECT matid matnr meins
        FROM /sapapo/matkey
        INTO TABLE gt_matkey
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_snpopmat
        WHERE matid = gt_snpopmat-matid.
    ENDIF.

    IF gt_matkey IS NOT INITIAL.
      me->fetch_ecc_master_data( ).
    ENDIF.

    me->fetch_bucket_details( gs_snpop_log_info-snpsession ).

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_material_master_details

*  fetch master details from ECC
  METHOD fetch_ecc_master_data.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Fetching Material Master From ECC...'(004)    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    CALL FUNCTION 'ZAPO_GET_RFC'
      EXPORTING
        iv_system             = 'ECC'
*       IV_ALL_BSG            = ' '
      TABLES
        t_rfc                 = gt_rfc
      EXCEPTIONS
        rfc_dest_not_found    = 1
        invalid_param         = 2
        no_data_found         = 3
        own_logical_not_found = 4
        OTHERS                = 5.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      READ TABLE gt_rfc INTO gw_rfc INDEX 1.
    ELSE.
      MESSAGE 'No RFC Destination Found'(054) TYPE 'I'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    me->fetch_ecc_material_master( ).
    me->fetch_ecc_prod_hrrchy_master( ).
    me->fetch_ecc_mat_grp2( ).
    me->fetch_ecc_mat_grp3( ).
    me->fetch_ecc_mat_grp4( ).
    me->fetch_ecc_mat_grp5( ).

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_material_from_ecc

*  fetch material group 2 descrion from ECC
  METHOD fetch_ecc_mat_grp2.

    DATA:
      lv_ind_frm        TYPE i,
      lv_ind_to         TYPE i,
      lv_n_mat          TYPE i,
      lv_batch_count    TYPE i,
      lt_ecc_prod_mast  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_prod_mast,
      lt_return         TYPE bapiret2_t,
      lt_ecc_tvm2t      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_mat_grp2.

*    lt_ecc_prod_mast = gt_ecc_mat_master.
    lt_ecc_prod_mast = gt_ecc_prod_mast.
    SORT lt_ecc_prod_mast BY mvgr2.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_ecc_prod_mast COMPARING mvgr2.

    me->get_batch_count(
      EXPORTING
        it_tab     = lt_ecc_prod_mast
      IMPORTING
        ev_n_lines = lv_n_mat
        ev_n_batch = lv_batch_count
    ).

    DO lv_batch_count TIMES.
      CLEAR: gt_ecc_prod_mast_batch, lt_ecc_tvm2t, lv_ind_frm, lv_ind_to.
      gt_ecc_prod_mast_batch = lt_ecc_prod_mast.
      lv_ind_frm      = gv_batch_size * ( sy-index - 1 ).
      lv_ind_to       = sy-index * gv_batch_size + 1.

      CASE sy-index.
        WHEN 1.
          DELETE gt_ecc_prod_mast_batch FROM lv_ind_to TO lv_n_mat.
        WHEN OTHERS.
          DELETE:
            gt_ecc_prod_mast_batch FROM lv_ind_to TO lv_n_mat,
            gt_ecc_prod_mast_batch FROM 1 TO lv_ind_frm.
      ENDCASE.

      me->clear_tables( ).

      me->fill_selection_mat_grp(
        EXPORTING
          iv_grp_type = 'MVGR2'
          iv_tab_name = 'TVM2T'
      ).

      me->execute_select_in_ecc(
        IMPORTING
          et_data       = lt_ecc_tvm2t
          et_ret        = lt_return
      ).

      APPEND LINES OF:
        lt_ecc_tvm2t  TO gt_ecc_tvm2t,
        lt_return     TO gt_return.

    ENDDO.

    SORT gt_ecc_tvm2t BY mvgr2.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_ecc_mat_grp2

*  fetch material group 3 descrion from ECC
  METHOD fetch_ecc_mat_grp3.

    DATA:
      lv_ind_frm        TYPE i,
      lv_ind_to         TYPE i,

      lv_batch_count    TYPE i,

      lt_return         TYPE bapiret2_t,
      lt_ecc_tvm3t      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_mat_grp3.

*    lt_ecc_prod_mast = gt_ecc_mat_master.
    lt_ecc_prod_mast = gt_ecc_prod_mast.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_ecc_mat_grp3
