  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_ecc_mat_grp5

  METHOD get_batch_count.

    DATA:
      lv_tmp_batch_count  TYPE p DECIMALS 8.

    ev_n_lines          = lines( it_tab ).
    lv_tmp_batch_count  = ev_n_lines / gv_batch_size.
    ev_n_batch          = ceil( lv_tmp_batch_count ).

  ENDMETHOD.                    "get_batch_count

*  fetch MARA & MARC data from ECC
  METHOD fetch_ecc_material_master.

    DATA:
      lv_ind_frm          TYPE i,
      lv_ind_to           TYPE i,
      lv_n_mat            TYPE i,
      lv_batch_count      TYPE i,
      lt_return           TYPE bapiret2_t,
      lt_ecc_mat_master   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_mat_master,
      lt_ecc_mat_temp     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_marc.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_matkey> TYPE gty_matkey.

    me->get_batch_count(
      EXPORTING
        it_tab     = gt_matkey
      IMPORTING
        ev_n_lines = lv_n_mat
        ev_n_batch = lv_batch_count
    ).

    DO lv_batch_count TIMES.
      CLEAR: gt_matkey_batch, lt_ecc_mat_master, lv_ind_frm, lv_ind_to.
      gt_matkey_batch = gt_matkey.
      lv_ind_frm      = gv_batch_size * ( sy-index - 1 ).
      lv_ind_to       = sy-index * gv_batch_size + 1.

      CASE sy-index.
        WHEN 1.
          DELETE gt_matkey_batch FROM lv_ind_to TO lv_n_mat.
        WHEN OTHERS.
          DELETE:
            gt_matkey_batch FROM lv_ind_to TO lv_n_mat,
            gt_matkey_batch FROM 1 TO lv_ind_frm.
      ENDCASE.

      me->clear_tables( ).

      me->fill_selection_matnr( ).

      me->execute_select_in_ecc(
        IMPORTING
          et_data       = lt_ecc_mat_temp
          et_ret        = lt_return
      ).
      IF lt_ecc_mat_temp IS NOT INITIAL.
        MOVE lt_ecc_mat_temp TO  lt_ecc_mat_master.
        SORT lt_ecc_mat_master BY matnr.
        DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_ecc_mat_master COMPARING matnr.

        SORT lt_ecc_mat_temp BY matnr werks.
        DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_ecc_mat_temp COMPARING matnr werks.
        APPEND LINES OF lt_ecc_mat_temp TO gt_marc.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND LINES OF:
        lt_ecc_mat_master TO gt_ecc_mat_master,
        lt_return         TO gt_return.

    ENDDO.
** Multiple plant issue Fix.
    IF gt_marc IS NOT INITIAL.
      SORT gt_marc BY  matnr werks.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_marc  COMPARING matnr werks.
      DELETE gt_marc WHERE beskz NE 'E'.
    ENDIF.
** Multiple plant issue Fix.
    IF gt_ecc_mat_master IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'No Material(s) found in ECC'(021) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.

    LOOP AT gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>.
      READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
        WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        CLEAR <lfs_matkey>-matid.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    DELETE gt_matkey WHERE matid IS INITIAL.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "get_material_master

  METHOD clear_tables.

    CLEAR: gt_tab_sel, gt_field_sel, gt_range_tab, gt_where_cond.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "clear_tables

*  fetch product hierarch master data: ZPRODHMST
  METHOD fetch_ecc_prod_hrrchy_master.

    DATA:
      lv_ind_frm        TYPE i,
      lv_ind_to         TYPE i,
      lv_n_mat          TYPE i,
      lv_batch_count    TYPE i,
      lt_ecc_mat        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_mat_master,
      lt_return         TYPE bapiret2_t,
      lt_ecc_prod_mast  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_prod_mast.


    lt_ecc_mat = gt_ecc_mat_master.
    SORT lt_ecc_mat BY matnr.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_ecc_mat COMPARING matnr.

    me->get_batch_count(
      EXPORTING
        it_tab     = lt_ecc_mat
      IMPORTING
        ev_n_lines = lv_n_mat
        ev_n_batch = lv_batch_count
    ).

    DO lv_batch_count TIMES.
      CLEAR: gt_ecc_mat_master_batch, lt_ecc_prod_mast, lv_ind_frm, lv_ind_to.
      gt_ecc_mat_master_batch = lt_ecc_mat.
      lv_ind_frm      = gv_batch_size * ( sy-index - 1 ).
      lv_ind_to       = sy-index * gv_batch_size + 1.

      CASE sy-index.
        WHEN 1.
          DELETE gt_ecc_mat_master_batch FROM lv_ind_to TO lv_n_mat.
        WHEN OTHERS.
          DELETE:
            gt_ecc_mat_master_batch FROM lv_ind_to TO lv_n_mat,
            gt_ecc_mat_master_batch FROM 1 TO lv_ind_frm.
      ENDCASE.

      me->clear_tables( ).

      me->fill_selection_prod_hierarchy( ).

      me->execute_select_in_ecc(
        IMPORTING
          et_data       = lt_ecc_prod_mast
          et_ret        = lt_return
      ).

      APPEND LINES OF :
        lt_ecc_prod_mast  TO gt_ecc_prod_mast,
        lt_return         TO gt_return.

    ENDDO.

    SORT gt_ecc_prod_mast BY matnr.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_ecc_prod_hierarchy_master

  METHOD fill_selection_prod_hierarchy.

    DATA:
      lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_mat_master,
      lt_selopt             TYPE rsdsselopt_t,
      lt_frange             TYPE rsds_frange_t.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_ecc_mat>   TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master,
      <lfs_selopt>    TYPE rsdsselopt,
      <lfs_frange>    TYPE rsds_frange,
      <lfs_range>     TYPE rsds_range,
      <lfs_range_tab> TYPE dmc_s_tabname_range.

    "" Field Selection
    APPEND 'MATNR' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'SPART' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'BISMT' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'PDH1' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'PDH2' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'PDH3' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'PDH4' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'PDH5' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'DESPDH1' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'DESPDH2' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'DESPDH3' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'DESPDH4' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'DESPDH5' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'MVGR2' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'MVGR3' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'MVGR4' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'MVGR5' TO gt_field_sel.

    "" Table Selection
    APPEND 'ZPRODHMST AS ZPRODHMST' TO gt_tab_sel.

    "" Range table selection
    UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_range_tab ASSIGNING <lfs_range_tab>.
    IF <lfs_range_tab> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_range_tab>-sign = 'I'.
      <lfs_range_tab>-option = 'EQ'.
      <lfs_range_tab>-low = 'ZPRODHMST'.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
    ENDIF.

*    lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master = gt_ecc_mat_master.
*    SORT lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master BY matnr.
*    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master COMPARING matnr.
    CLEAR: lt_selopt,lt_frange.
    UNASSIGN  <lfs_ecc_mat>.
*    LOOP AT lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_mat>.
    LOOP AT gt_ecc_mat_master_batch ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_mat>.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
      IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
        <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
        <lfs_selopt>-low = <lfs_ecc_mat>-matnr.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'MATNR'.
      <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR: lt_selopt, lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master.
*    lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master = gt_ecc_mat_master.
    lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master = gt_ecc_mat_master_batch.
    SORT lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master BY spart.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master COMPARING spart.
    UNASSIGN  <lfs_ecc_mat>.
    LOOP AT lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_mat>.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
      IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
        <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
        <lfs_selopt>-low = <lfs_ecc_mat>-spart.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'SPART'.
      <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
    ENDIF.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_range>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_where_cond ASSIGNING <lfs_range>.
    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_range>-tablename = 'ZPRODHMST'.
      <lfs_range>-frange_t  = lt_frange.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fill_selection_prod_hierarchy

  METHOD fill_selection_mat_grp.

    DATA:
*      lt_tmp_ecc_prod_mast  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_prod_mast,
      lt_selopt             TYPE rsdsselopt_t,
      lt_frange             TYPE rsds_frange_t.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_ecc_prodh> TYPE gty_ecc_prod_mast,
      <lfs_selopt>    TYPE rsdsselopt,
      <lfs_frange>    TYPE rsds_frange,
      <lfs_range>     TYPE rsds_range,
      <lfs_range_tab> TYPE dmc_s_tabname_range,
      <lfs_mat_grp>   TYPE any.

    APPEND iv_grp_type TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'BEZEI' TO gt_field_sel.

*    lt_tmp_ecc_prod_mast = gt_ecc_prod_mast.
*    SORT lt_tmp_ecc_prod_mast BY (iv_grp_type).
*    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_tmp_ecc_prod_mast COMPARING (iv_grp_type).

    UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
    IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
      <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
      <lfs_selopt>-low = sy-langu.
    ENDIF.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'SPRAS'.
      <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR  lt_selopt.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_ecc_prodh>.
*    LOOP AT lt_tmp_ecc_prod_mast ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_prodh>.
    LOOP AT gt_ecc_prod_mast_batch ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_prodh>.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
      IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
        <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_mat_grp>.
        ASSIGN COMPONENT iv_grp_type OF STRUCTURE <lfs_ecc_prodh> TO <lfs_mat_grp>.
        IF <lfs_mat_grp> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_selopt>-low = <lfs_mat_grp>.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = iv_grp_type.
      <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
    ENDIF.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_range>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_where_cond ASSIGNING <lfs_range>.
    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_range>-tablename = iv_tab_name.
      <lfs_range>-frange_t  = lt_frange.
    ENDIF.


    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_range_tab ASSIGNING <lfs_range_tab>.
    IF <lfs_range_tab> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_range_tab>-sign = 'I'.
      <lfs_range_tab>-option = 'EQ'.
      <lfs_range_tab>-low = iv_tab_name.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND iv_tab_name TO gt_tab_sel.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fill_selection_mat_grp

  METHOD fill_selection_matnr.

    DATA:
      lt_selopt       TYPE rsdsselopt_t,
      lt_frange       TYPE rsdsfrange_t_ssel.
*      lv_string       TYPE string,
*      lt_string       TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF string.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_matkey>    TYPE gty_matkey,
*      <lfs_param>     TYPE zapoparam,
      <lfs_selopt>    TYPE rsdsselopt,
      <lfs_frange>    TYPE rsdsfrange_s_ssel,
      <lfs_range>     TYPE rsdsrange_s_ssel,
      <lfs_range_tab> TYPE dmc_s_tabname_range.

    "" Field Selection
    APPEND 'MATNR' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'MTART' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'SPART' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'MEINS' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'WERKS' TO gt_field_sel.
    APPEND 'BESKZ' TO gt_field_sel.
*    APPEND 'DISPO' TO gt_field_sel.

    "" Table Selection
    APPEND 'MARA AS MARA' TO gt_tab_sel.
    APPEND 'INNER JOIN MARC AS MARC ON MARA~MATNR = MARC~MATNR' TO gt_tab_sel.

    "" Range table selection
    UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_range_tab ASSIGNING <lfs_range_tab>.
    IF <lfs_range_tab> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_range_tab>-sign = 'I'.
      <lfs_range_tab>-option = 'EQ'.
      <lfs_range_tab>-low = 'MARA'.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
    ENDIF.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_range_tab ASSIGNING <lfs_range_tab>.
    IF <lfs_range_tab> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_range_tab>-sign = 'I'.
      <lfs_range_tab>-option = 'EQ'.
      <lfs_range_tab>-low = 'MARC'.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR: lt_selopt,lt_frange.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_matkey>.
*    LOOP AT gt_matkey ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>.
    LOOP AT gt_matkey_batch ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
      IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
        <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
        <lfs_selopt>-low = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'MARA~MATNR'.
      <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
    ENDIF.
    "" Division filling
    CLEAR: lt_selopt.
    LOOP AT s_div[] INTO s_div.
      UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
      IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_selopt>-sign = s_div-sign.
        <lfs_selopt>-option = s_div-option.
        <lfs_selopt>-low = s_div-low.
        <lfs_selopt>-high = s_div-high.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'SPART'.
      <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
    ENDIF.

    "" Material Type Filling
    CLEAR: lt_selopt.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
    IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
      <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
      <lfs_selopt>-low = 'ZFRT'.
    ENDIF.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'MTART'.
      <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
    ENDIF.

    UNASSIGN <lfs_range>.
    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_where_cond ASSIGNING <lfs_range>.
    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
      <lfs_range>-tablename = 'MARA'.
      <lfs_range>-frange_t  = lt_frange.
    ENDIF.

*    "" DISPO Filling
*    CLEAR: lt_selopt,lt_frange.
*    READ TABLE gt_param ASSIGNING <lfs_param>
*      WITH KEY param3 = 'MAT_TYPE'.
*    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_param> IS ASSIGNED.
*      SPLIT <lfs_param>-value1 AT ',' INTO TABLE lt_string.
*      LOOP AT lt_string INTO lv_string.
*        UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
*        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
*        IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
*          <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
*          <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
*          <lfs_selopt>-low = lv_string.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDLOOP.
*    ENDIF.
*
*    UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
*    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
*    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
*      <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'DISPO'.
*      <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
*    ENDIF.
*
    "" Material Type Filling
*    CLEAR: lt_selopt.
*    UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
*    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
*    IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
*      <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
*      <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
*      <lfs_selopt>-low = 'E'.
*    ENDIF.
*
*    UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
*    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
*    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
*      <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'BESKZ'.
*      <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
*    ENDIF.
*    UNASSIGN <lfs_range>.
*    APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_where_cond ASSIGNING <lfs_range>.
*    IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
*      <lfs_range>-tablename = 'MARC'.
*      <lfs_range>-frange_t  = lt_frange.
*    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fill_selection_matnr

  METHOD execute_select_in_ecc.

    DATA:
      lv_string TYPE string,
      lt_data TYPE soli_tab,
      lo TYPE REF TO cl_trex_json_deserializer.

*    call rfc in ECC to execute the select query
    CALL FUNCTION 'Z_APO_FM_DYN_SELECT_QUERY' DESTINATION gw_rfc-rfcdest
      EXPORTING
        it_tab_selection   = gt_tab_sel
        it_field_selection = gt_field_sel
        it_range_tabnames  = gt_range_tab
        it_where_cond      = gt_where_cond
      IMPORTING
        et_return          = et_ret
        et_data            = lt_data.

    CALL FUNCTION '/OSP/REPORT_TO_STRING'
      EXPORTING
        i_t_in   = lt_data
      IMPORTING
        e_string = lv_string.

    IF lv_string IS INITIAL.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    CREATE OBJECT lo.
    TRY.
        lo->deserialize(
          EXPORTING
            json = lv_string
          IMPORTING
            abap = et_data
        ).
      CATCH cx_trex_serialization.
        MESSAGE 'Error: JSON to table conversion'(053) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDTRY.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "execute_select_in_ecc

  METHOD fetch_log_location.

    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF lty_et_loc,
        sessionid TYPE /sapapo/snpsession,
        line_no	TYPE /sapapo/snpopt_line_no,
        locid	TYPE /sapapo/locid,
      END OF lty_et_loc.

    DATA:
      lt_et_loc TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF lty_et_loc.

    SELECT sessionid line_no locid
      FROM /sapapo/snpophlo
      INTO TABLE lt_et_loc
      WHERE sessionid = gs_snpop_log_info-snpsession.
*    delete adjacent not required, only unique location will be
*    selected in above query
    IF lt_et_loc IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT loc~locid loctype locno descr40
        FROM /sapapo/loc AS loc
        INNER JOIN /sapapo/loct AS loct
          ON loc~mandt = loct~mandt AND loc~locid = loct~locid
          AND spras = sy-langu
        CLIENT SPECIFIED
        INTO TABLE gt_loc
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_et_loc
        WHERE loc~mandt = sy-mandt AND loc~locid = lt_et_loc-locid.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        SORT gt_loc BY locid.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_log_location

  METHOD fetch_bucket_details.

*-> fetching bucket definition of the selected SNP Optimizer log
    SELECT bct~sessionid line_no bct~bucke valto valfr datefr dateto
      FROM /sapapo/snpopbct AS bct
      INNER JOIN /sapapo/snpopbuc AS buc
        ON bct~mandt = buc~mandt AND bct~sessionid = buc~sessionid AND bct~bucke = buc~bucke
      INTO TABLE gt_snpopbct
      WHERE bct~mandt = sy-mandt AND bct~sessionid = iv_session_id.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT gt_snpopbct BY sessionid bucke.
    ELSE.
      MESSAGE 'No buckets found for the selected SNP OP Log'(022) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_bucket_details

  METHOD fetch_param_data.

*    less no of field in table
    SELECT *
      FROM zapoparam
      INTO TABLE gt_param
      WHERE param1 = 'SNP' AND param2 = sy-cprog AND
            active_flag = abap_true.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT gt_param.
      me->set_planning_book( ).
    ELSE.
      MESSAGE 'Please maintain Planning Book & Data View in Param Table'(014) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.

*    less no of field in table
    SELECT *
      FROM zbg_div
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_bg_div
      WHERE div IN s_div[].
    IF sy-subrc <> 0 .
      REFRESH gt_bg_div.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_param_data

  METHOD process_buckets.

    DATA:
      lt_tmp_buckets    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_bucket,
      lt_tmp_buckets_fr TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_bucket,
      lt_tmp_buckets_to TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_bucket.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_bct>       TYPE gty_snpopbct,
      <lfs_bucket>    TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_bucket_fr> TYPE gty_bucket,
      <lfs_bucket_to> TYPE gty_bucket.

*->  Setting bucket description for the field output and collecting the same bucket to on if
    LOOP AT gt_snpopbct ASSIGNING <lfs_bct>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket>.
      IF <lfs_bucket> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_bucket>-bct = <lfs_bct>.
        <lfs_bucket>-cal_month = <lfs_bct>-datefr+0(6).
        <lfs_bucket>-bucket_desc  = <lfs_bucket>-bucket_id    = 'M' && <lfs_bct>-datefr+4(2).
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    lt_tmp_buckets_to = lt_tmp_buckets_fr = lt_tmp_buckets = gt_buckets.
    SORT lt_tmp_buckets_fr BY bucket_id datefr.
    SORT lt_tmp_buckets_to BY bucket_id ASCENDING dateto DESCENDING .
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_tmp_buckets COMPARING bucket_id.
    gt_buckets = lt_tmp_buckets.

*->  getting the no. of buckets
    gv_n_buckets = lines( gt_buckets ).
    IF gv_n_buckets IS INITIAL.
      MESSAGE 'Bucket not found'(023) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.

*->  assigning the start & end date of the bucket
    UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket>.
    LOOP AT gt_buckets ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket>.
*->    assigning start of buckets
      UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket_fr>.
      READ TABLE lt_tmp_buckets_fr ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket_fr>
        WITH KEY bucket_id = <lfs_bucket>-bucket_id.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_bucket_fr>  IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_bucket>-valfr = <lfs_bucket_fr>-valfr.
        <lfs_bucket>-datefr = <lfs_bucket_fr>-datefr.
      ENDIF.
*->    assigning end date of buckets
      UNASSIGN <lfs_bucket_to>.
      READ TABLE lt_tmp_buckets_to ASSIGNING <lfs_bucket_to>
        WITH KEY bucket_id = <lfs_bucket>-bucket_id.
      IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_bucket_to>   IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_bucket>-valto = <lfs_bucket_to>-valto.
        <lfs_bucket>-dateto = <lfs_bucket_to>-dateto.
        <lfs_bucket>-bucke_to = <lfs_bucket_to>-bucke.
      ENDIF.

*->    calculating no of calender days in the current bucket
      <lfs_bucket>-cal_days = <lfs_bucket>-dateto - <lfs_bucket>-datefr + 1.

    ENDLOOP.

    SORT gt_buckets BY sessionid bucke.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "process_buckets

  METHOD fetch_production_qty.
    TYPES : BEGIN OF lty_sapapo_snpopiek,
            sessionid TYPE /sapapo/snpsession,
            inkey     TYPE /sapapo/guid,
            exkey     TYPE /sapapo/snpexkey,
            END OF lty_sapapo_snpopiek.

    DATA : lt_snpopiek TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF lty_sapapo_snpopiek.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS : <lfs_snpopiek> TYPE lty_sapapo_snpopiek,
                    <lfs_snpoppro> TYPE gty_snpoppro.
*    DATA:
*      lt_tmp_snpoppma    TYPE TABLE OF gty_snpoppma.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.

    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
    EXPORTING
      i_text               = 'Fetching Production Quantity...'(006)    " Progress Text (If no message transferred in I_MSG*)
      i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
      i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
      i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

*    IF gt_matkey IS NOT INITIAL.

*      fetch ET_PROMAT data
    SELECT sessionid proid matid locid outin
      FROM /sapapo/snpoppma
      INTO TABLE gt_snpoppma
*        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_matkey
      WHERE sessionid = gs_snpop_log_info-snpsession.
*        AND matid = gt_matkey-matid.
*      IF sy-subrc = 0.
*        lt_tmp_snpoppma = gt_snpoppma.
*        SORT lt_tmp_snpoppma BY sessionid proid.
*        DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_tmp_snpoppma COMPARING sessionid proid.
*        IF lt_tmp_snpoppma IS NOT INITIAL.

*      fetch ET_PROMO data
    SELECT sessionid line_no proid locid matid
      FROM /sapapo/snpoppmo
      INTO TABLE gt_snpoppmo
      WHERE sessionid = gs_snpop_log_info-snpsession.
*            FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_tmp_snpoppma
*            WHERE sessionid = lt_tmp_snpoppma-sessionid AND proid = lt_tmp_snpoppma-proid.

*          fetch IT_PROMO data
    SELECT sessionid proid valfr valto produ
      FROM /sapapo/snpoppro
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_snpoppro "Corresponding added 16.08.2021
      WHERE sessionid = gs_snpop_log_info-snpsession.
    IF gt_snpoppro[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SORT gt_snpoppro BY sessionid proid.
      SELECT sessionid inkey exkey
             FROM /sapapo/snpopiek
             INTO TABLE lt_snpopiek
             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_snpoppro
             WHERE sessionid = gt_snpoppro-sessionid
               AND inkey     = gt_snpoppro-proid.
      IF lt_snpopiek[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        SORT lt_snpopiek BY sessionid inkey.

        LOOP AT gt_snpoppro ASSIGNING <lfs_snpoppro>.

          UNASSIGN <lfs_snpopiek>.
          READ TABLE lt_snpopiek ASSIGNING <lfs_snpopiek> WITH KEY sessionid = <lfs_snpoppro>-sessionid
                                                                   inkey     = <lfs_snpoppro>-proid BINARY SEARCH.
          IF <lfs_snpopiek> IS ASSIGNED.
            IF <lfs_snpopiek>-exkey CS 'CONVERT'.
              <lfs_snpoppro>-ind_con = 'C'.
            ELSE.
              <lfs_snpoppro>-ind_con = 'P'.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

        ENDLOOP.

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
*            FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_tmp_snpoppma
*            WHERE sessionid = lt_tmp_snpoppma-sessionid AND  proid = lt_tmp_snpoppma-proid.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_production_qtty

*  fetch custom table table based on selection screen
  METHOD fetch_ztable_data.

    gv_total_steps = 4.

    gv_processed_steps = gv_processed_steps + 1.
    cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
      EXPORTING
        i_text               = 'Fetching Generated Material balance Data...'(077)
        i_processed          = gv_processed_steps                " Number of Objects Already Processed
        i_total              = gv_total_steps                    " Total Number of Objects to Be Processed
        i_output_immediately = abap_true
    ).

    SELECT *
      FROM zapo_matbal_hrd
      INTO TABLE gt_matbal_hrd
      WHERE sessionname EQ p_orun AND div IN s_div.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SELECT *
        FROM zapo_matbal_item
        INTO TABLE gt_matbal_item
        WHERE sessionname = p_orun AND div IN s_div.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        MESSAGE 'No Data Found'(037) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      MESSAGE 'No Data Found'(037) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.

    ""BOC By Kalpesh/Indu 25.07.2024 AD1K916987
    CLEAR : gt_matbal_hrd_t[].
    gt_matbal_hrd_t[] = gt_matbal_hrd[].
    SORT : gt_matbal_hrd_t BY matnr.
    DELETE gt_matbal_hrd_t WHERE matnr IS INITIAL.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_matbal_hrd_t COMPARING matnr.
    IF gt_matbal_hrd_t[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      CLEAR : gt_mar[].
      SELECT mandt
             matnr
             matkl
             FROM /sapapo/matkey CLIENT SPECIFIED
             INTO TABLE gt_mar
             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_matbal_hrd_t
             WHERE mandt = sy-mandt
             AND   matnr = gt_matbal_hrd_t-matnr.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        SORT : gt_mar BY matnr.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    ""EOC By Kalpesh/Indu 25.07.2024 AD1K916987

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_ztable_data

*  display salv
  METHOD display_alv.

    DATA :
      lox_salv_msg  TYPE REF TO cx_salv_msg,
      lv_error_str  TYPE string,
      lv_key        TYPE salv_s_layout_key,
      lo_functions  TYPE REF TO cl_salv_functions_list,
      lo_disp       TYPE REF TO cl_salv_display_settings,
      lv_title      TYPE lvc_title,
      lo_layout     TYPE REF TO cl_salv_layout,
      lo_columns    TYPE REF TO cl_salv_columns,
      lo_column     TYPE REF TO cl_salv_column.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
      <lfs_final> TYPE ANY TABLE.

*    lt_final = gt_final.
    UNASSIGN <lfs_final>.
    ASSIGN lo_outtab->* TO <lfs_final>.
    IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
      IF <lfs_final> IS INITIAL.
        MESSAGE 'No Data Found'(037) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ELSE.
        SORT <lfs_final>.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      MESSAGE 'Err' TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.

    TRY.
        CALL METHOD cl_salv_table=>factory
          IMPORTING
            r_salv_table = go_salv
          CHANGING
            t_table      = <lfs_final>.

      CATCH cx_salv_msg INTO lox_salv_msg.
        lv_error_str = lox_salv_msg->get_text( ).
        MESSAGE lv_error_str TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDTRY.

* PF Status for records deletion
    CASE abap_true.
      WHEN r_del.

        go_salv->set_screen_status(
          EXPORTING
            report        = sy-cprog    " ABAP Program: Current Main Program
            pfstatus      = 'ZMATBAL_STATUS'
            set_functions = go_salv->c_functions_all

        ).

        TRY.
            go_events = go_salv->get_event( ).
            SET HANDLER me->on_user_command FOR go_events.
          CATCH cx_salv_not_found.
            MESSAGE 'Error: Salv not Found'(038) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
            LEAVE  LIST-PROCESSING.
        ENDTRY.
      WHEN r_gen.

        TRY.
            go_events = go_salv->get_event( ).
            SET HANDLER me->on_link_click FOR go_events.
          CATCH cx_salv_not_found.
            MESSAGE 'Error: Salv not Found'(038) TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
            LEAVE  LIST-PROCESSING.
        ENDTRY.
    ENDCASE.

    lo_functions = go_salv->get_functions( ).
    lo_functions->set_all( ).

*** ALV Layout Save Option
    lo_layout = go_salv->get_layout( ).

    lv_key-report = sy-repid.
    lo_layout->set_save_restriction( if_salv_c_layout=>restrict_none ).
    lo_layout->set_key( lv_key ).
    IF p_layout IS NOT INITIAL.
      lo_layout->set_initial_layout( p_layout ).
    ENDIF.

***  Set SALV Selection Mode
    go_sel = go_salv->get_selections( ).
    go_sel->set_selection_mode( if_salv_c_selection_mode=>row_column ).

*** Set SALV display properties
    lo_disp = go_salv->get_display_settings( ).
    lo_disp->set_striped_pattern( abap_true  ).
    lv_title = sy-title && ':' && p_orun.
    lo_disp->set_list_header( lv_title  ).

    lo_columns = go_salv->get_columns( ).

    me->set_salv_col_settings( ).

    me->set_aggregation( ).

    go_salv->display( ).

  ENDMETHOD.                    "display_alv

*  set salv column setting
  METHOD set_salv_col_settings.

    DATA:
      lox_salv_not_fnd  TYPE REF TO cx_salv_not_found,
      lv_error_str      TYPE string,
      lv_fieldname      TYPE lvc_fname,
      lv_field_descr    TYPE scrtext_l,
      lo_col            TYPE REF TO cl_salv_column_list,
      lo_columns        TYPE REF TO cl_salv_columns_table,
      lt_cols           TYPE salv_t_column_ref,              " Added by KPIT_095
      lw_cols           LIKE LINE OF lt_cols.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_reg> TYPE gty_reg_off_desc.

***    set column properties
    lo_columns = go_salv->get_columns( ).
    lo_columns->set_key_fixation( ).
    lo_columns->set_optimize( ).

    TRY.

        lo_col ?= lo_columns->get_column( 'SESSIONNAME' ).
        lo_col->set_long_text( 'Optimiser Log Name'(084) ).
        lo_col->set_medium_text( 'Optimiser Log'(085) ).
        lo_col->set_key( ).

        lo_col ?= lo_columns->get_column( 'PLANT_DESC' ).
        lo_col->set_long_text( 'Plant Description'(024) ).
        lo_col->set_medium_text( 'Plant. Desc.'(025) ).
        lo_col->set_short_text( 'PlantDesc'(026) ).
        lo_col->set_key( ).

        lo_col ?= lo_columns->get_column( 'SRC_PLANT' ).
        lo_col->set_long_text( 'Production Plant'(027) ).
        lo_col->set_medium_text( 'Prod Plant'(028) ).
        lo_col->set_short_text( 'Plant'(029) ).
        lo_col->set_key( ).

        lo_col ?= lo_columns->get_column( 'SUPPLY_PLANT' ).
        lo_col->set_long_text( 'Supply Location'(030) ).
        lo_col->set_medium_text( 'Suply Loc'(031) ).
        lo_col->set_short_text( 'Sup Loc'(032) ).
        lo_col->set_key( ).

        lo_col ?= lo_columns->get_column( 'MATNR' ).
        lo_col->set_key( ).

        lo_col ?= lo_columns->get_column( 'CLSG_STK_IND' ).

        lo_col->set_long_text( 'Safety Stock Shortfall'(090) ).
        lo_col->set_medium_text( '' ).
        lo_col->set_short_text( '' ).

      CATCH cx_salv_not_found INTO lox_salv_not_fnd.
        lv_error_str = lox_salv_not_fnd->get_text( ).
        MESSAGE lv_error_str TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
    ENDTRY.

*--> Get Column List
    lo_columns = go_salv->get_columns( ).
    lt_cols = lo_columns->get( ).

    UNASSIGN <lfs_reg>.
    LOOP AT gt_reg_off_desc ASSIGNING <lfs_reg>.
      TRY.
          lv_fieldname = 'D_' && <lfs_reg>-reg_off.
          CLEAR lw_cols.
          READ TABLE lt_cols INTO lw_cols WITH KEY
                                  columnname = lv_fieldname.
          IF sy-subrc <> 0.
            CONTINUE.
          ENDIF.

          lo_col ?= lo_columns->get_column( lv_fieldname ).
          lv_field_descr = 'D-' && <lfs_reg>-txtsh.
          lo_col->set_long_text( lv_field_descr ).
        CATCH cx_salv_not_found INTO lox_salv_not_fnd.
          lv_error_str = lox_salv_not_fnd->get_text( ).
          MESSAGE lv_error_str TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      ENDTRY.

      TRY.
          lv_fieldname = 'S_' && <lfs_reg>-reg_off.
          lo_col ?= lo_columns->get_column( lv_fieldname ).
          lv_field_descr = 'S-' && <lfs_reg>-txtsh.
          lo_col->set_long_text( lv_field_descr ).
        CATCH cx_salv_not_found INTO lox_salv_not_fnd.
          lv_error_str = lox_salv_not_fnd->get_text( ).
          MESSAGE lv_error_str TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
      ENDTRY.
    ENDLOOP.

*    lt_col_list = lo_columns->get( ).
*    CLEAR ls_col_ref.
*    ls_decimals = '0'.
*    LOOP AT lt_col_list INTO ls_col_ref.
*      ls_col_ref-r_column->set_decimals( '0' ).
*    ENDLOOP.


  ENDMETHOD.                    "set_salv_sol_settings

  METHOD set_aggregation.

    DATA:
      lv_fieldname TYPE fieldname,
      lo_aggr  TYPE REF TO cl_salv_aggregation,
      lo_aggrs TYPE REF TO cl_salv_aggregations.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_reg> TYPE gty_reg_off_desc.

    lo_aggrs = go_salv->get_aggregations( ).

    LOOP AT gt_reg_off_desc ASSIGNING <lfs_reg>.
      TRY.
          lv_fieldname = 'D_' && <lfs_reg>-reg_off.
          lo_aggr = lo_aggrs->add_aggregation(
              columnname  = lv_fieldname
              aggregation = if_salv_c_aggregation=>total
          ).
        CATCH cx_salv_data_error.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
        CATCH cx_salv_not_found.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
        CATCH cx_salv_existing.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
      ENDTRY.
      TRY.
          lv_fieldname = 'S_' && <lfs_reg>-reg_off.
          lo_aggr = lo_aggrs->add_aggregation(
              columnname  = lv_fieldname
              aggregation = if_salv_c_aggregation=>total
          ).
        CATCH cx_salv_data_error.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
        CATCH cx_salv_not_found.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
        CATCH cx_salv_existing.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
      ENDTRY.
    ENDLOOP.
    TRY.
        lo_aggr = lo_aggrs->add_aggregation(
            columnname  = ''
            aggregation = if_salv_c_aggregation=>total
        ).
      CATCH cx_salv_data_error.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
      CATCH cx_salv_not_found.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
      CATCH cx_salv_existing.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
        lo_aggr = lo_aggrs->add_aggregation(
            columnname  = 'TOT_DMD'
            aggregation = if_salv_c_aggregation=>total
        ).
      CATCH cx_salv_data_error.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
      CATCH cx_salv_not_found.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)

    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
        lo_aggr = lo_aggrs->add_aggregation(
            columnname  = 'TOT_SUPPLY'
            aggregation = if_salv_c_aggregation=>total
        ).
      CATCH cx_salv_data_error.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
      CATCH cx_salv_not_found.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
      CATCH cx_salv_existing.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.

        ).
      CATCH cx_salv_data_error.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
      CATCH cx_salv_not_found.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
      CATCH cx_salv_existing.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
        lo_aggr = lo_aggrs->add_aggregation(
            columnname  = 'CAPTIVE_SUPPLY'

        ).
      CATCH cx_salv_data_error.    " ALV: General Error Class (Checked During Syntax Check)

*  salv user command event
  METHOD on_user_command.

    DATA:
      lv_ans TYPE char1.

    CASE e_salv_function.
      WHEN 'DELETE'.

        CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
          EXPORTING
            text_question  = 'Delete Log Entries ?'(050)
            text_button_1  = 'Yes'
            icon_button_1  = 'No'
            default_button = '1'
          IMPORTING
            answer         = lv_ans
          EXCEPTIONS
            text_not_found = 1
            OTHERS         = 2.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0 AND lv_ans = '1'.
          cl_progress_indicator=>progress_indicate(
            EXPORTING
              i_text               = 'Deleting Generated Material Balance Data...'(081)
              i_output_immediately = abap_true
          ).

          DELETE zapo_matbal_hrd FROM TABLE gt_matbal_hrd.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            DELETE zapo_matbal_item FROM TABLE gt_matbal_item.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              MESSAGE 'Selected Log entries are deleted successfully'(051) TYPE 'S' .
              LEAVE TO SCREEN 0.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
    ENDCASE.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "ON_USER_COMMAND

*  f4 help
  METHOD f4_help.

    DATA:
      lv_window_title TYPE string,
      lw_layout_key TYPE salv_s_layout_key,
      lw_layout_info TYPE salv_s_layout_info.

    CASE iv_field.
      WHEN 'P_LAYOUT'.
        lw_layout_key-report = sy-cprog.
*        generating f4 help for layout field
        lw_layout_info = cl_salv_layout_service=>f4_layouts(
            s_key    = lw_layout_key
*            layout   =
*            restrict = IF_SALV_C_LAYOUT=>RESTRICT_NONE
        ).
        p_layout = lw_layout_info-layout.
      WHEN 'P_FILE'.
        lv_window_title = 'Select Folder'(072).
        cl_gui_frontend_services=>directory_browse(
          EXPORTING
            window_title         = lv_window_title    " Title of Browsing Window
*            initial_folder       =     " Start Browsing Here
          CHANGING
            selected_folder      = p_file    " Folder Selected By User
          EXCEPTIONS
            cntl_error           = 1
            error_no_gui         = 2
            not_supported_by_gui = 3
            OTHERS               = 4
        ).
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
                     WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
        ENDIF.
    ENDCASE.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "f4_help

  METHOD modify_screen.
    LOOP AT SCREEN.
**       boc: un comment this below for before prod movement
      IF screen-group1 = 'DEV'.
        screen-active = 0.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
**       eoc: un comment this below for before prod movement
      CASE abap_true.
        WHEN r_del OR r_disp OR c_dev.
          IF screen-group1 = 'D01'.
            screen-active = 0.
            MODIFY SCREEN.
          ENDIF.
      ENDCASE.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.                    "modify_screen

  METHOD fetch_loc_detail_customer.
    TYPES: BEGIN OF lty_loc_temp,
           locno TYPE /sapapo/locno,
           locid TYPE /sapapo/locid,
           END OF lty_loc_temp.
** Data Declaration
    DATA:lt_snpodmd TYPE TABLE OF gty_snpopdmd,
          lv_loc    TYPE kunnr,
          lv_suffix TYPE char5,
          lv_suffix_2 TYPE char5,
          lt_kna1   TYPE TABLE OF gty_kna1,
          lt_return TYPE bapiret2_t,
          lt_loc_temp TYPE TABLE OF lty_loc_temp,
          lt_loc_det  TYPE TABLE OF gty_loc_det.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:<lfs_loc> TYPE gty_loc_kn,
                  <lfs_kna1> TYPE gty_kna1,
                  <lfs_params> TYPE zapoparam,
                  <lfs_snpopdmd> TYPE gty_snpopdmd,
                  <lfs_mat_master> TYPE gty_ecc_mat_master,
                  <lfs_matkey>  TYPE gty_matkey,
                  <lfs_loc_tmp> TYPE lty_loc_temp,
                  <lfs_loc_det> TYPE gty_loc_det.

    IF gt_snpopdmd IS NOT INITIAL.

      lt_snpodmd = gt_snpopdmd .
      SORT lt_snpodmd BY locid .
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_snpodmd COMPARING locid .

**Fetch Location Number Details
      SELECT locid
             locno
        FROM /sapapo/loc
        INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE lt_loc_temp
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_snpodmd
        WHERE locid = lt_snpodmd-locid
          AND loctype = '1010'.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        LOOP AT gt_snpopdmd ASSIGNING <lfs_snpopdmd>.
          APPEND INITIAL LINE TO  gt_loc_kn ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>.
          MOVE-CORRESPONDING <lfs_snpopdmd> TO <lfs_loc>.
        ENDLOOP.
        SORT gt_loc_kn BY locid matid.
        DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_loc_kn COMPARING locid matid.

** Segrgate the lono no
        LOOP AT gt_loc_kn ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>.
          CLEAR: lv_loc, lv_suffix, lv_suffix_2.

          "Get Location No based on Loc Id
          READ TABLE lt_loc_temp ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_tmp> WITH KEY locid = <lfs_loc>-locid.
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_loc_tmp> IS ASSIGNED.
            <lfs_loc>-locno = <lfs_loc_tmp>-locno.
            " Split locno based on underscore
            SPLIT <lfs_loc>-locno AT '_' INTO lv_loc lv_suffix lv_suffix_2.

            "If locno contains 'EX' pass value from param table
            IF lv_suffix_2 = 'EX'.
              APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_loc_det ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_det>.
              <lfs_loc_det>-kunnr = <lfs_loc>-locno.
              READ TABLE gt_param ASSIGNING <lfs_params> WITH KEY param4 = lv_suffix_2.
              IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_params> IS ASSIGNED.
                <lfs_loc>-vtweg = <lfs_params>-value1.
                <lfs_loc_det>-vkbur = <lfs_params>-value1.
                UNASSIGN <lfs_params>.
              ENDIF.
              lv_loc = <lfs_loc>-locno.

              " If there is no underscore collet in temp table
            ELSEIF lv_suffix IS INITIAL.
              APPEND lv_loc TO gt_kunnr_suffix.
            ELSE.

              " Other than above read with suffix.
              READ TABLE gt_param ASSIGNING <lfs_params> WITH KEY param4 = lv_suffix.
              IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_params> IS ASSIGNED.
                <lfs_loc>-vtweg = <lfs_params>-value1.
                UNASSIGN <lfs_params>.
              ELSE.
                lv_loc = <lfs_loc>-locno.
                APPEND lv_loc TO gt_kunnr_suffix.
              ENDIF.

            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
          <lfs_loc>-locno_2 = lv_loc.
*          IF lv_suffix IS INITIAL.
*            APPEND lv_loc TO gt_kunnr_suffix.
*          ELSE.
*            READ TABLE gt_param ASSIGNING <lfs_params> WITH KEY param4 = lv_suffix.
*            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_params> IS ASSIGNED.
*              <lfs_loc>-vtweg = <lfs_params>-value1.
*              UNASSIGN <lfs_params>.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
*          READ TABLE gt_snpopdmd ASSIGNING <lfs_snpopdmd> WITH KEY locid = <lfs_loc>-locid.
*          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_snpopdmd> IS ASSIGNED.
*          <lfs_loc>-matid = <lfs_loc>-matid.
          READ TABLE gt_matkey  ASSIGNING <lfs_matkey>  WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matid = <lfs_loc>-matid .
          IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_matkey> IS ASSIGNED.
            READ TABLE gt_ecc_mat_master ASSIGNING <lfs_mat_master> WITH TABLE KEY skey1 COMPONENTS matnr = <lfs_matkey>-matnr.
            IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_mat_master> IS ASSIGNED.
              <lfs_loc>-spart = <lfs_mat_master>-spart.
              <lfs_loc>-matnr = <lfs_mat_master>-matnr.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.

** Check if the RFC is active
        CALL FUNCTION 'ZAPO_GET_RFC'
          EXPORTING
            iv_system             = 'ECC'
*           IV_ALL_BSG            = ' '
          TABLES
            t_rfc                 = gt_rfc
          EXCEPTIONS
            rfc_dest_not_found    = 1
            invalid_param         = 2
            no_data_found         = 3
            own_logical_not_found = 4
            OTHERS                = 5.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.

** Fetch the customer Details from ECC without suffix
          IF gt_kunnr_suffix IS NOT INITIAL.
            SORT gt_kunnr_suffix BY kunnr.
            DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_kunnr_suffix COMPARING kunnr.

            me->clear_tables( ).

            me->fill_selection_customer( 'KNA1' ).

            me->execute_select_in_ecc(
              IMPORTING
                et_data       = lt_kna1
                et_ret        = lt_return
            ).
*              LOOP AT lt_kna1 ASSIGNING <lfs_kna1>.
*                LOOP AT gt_loc_kn ASSIGNING <lfs_loc> WHERE locno_2 = <lfs_kna1>-kunnr .
*                  IF <lfs_kna1>-land1 = 'IN'.
*                    <lfs_loc>-vtweg = '10'.
*                  ELSE.
*                    <lfs_loc>-vtweg = '15'.
*                  ENDIF.
*                ENDLOOP.
*              ENDLOOP.
            LOOP AT gt_loc_kn ASSIGNING <lfs_loc>  .
              READ TABLE lt_kna1 ASSIGNING <lfs_kna1> WITH KEY kunnr  = <lfs_loc>-locno_2 .
              IF sy-subrc = 0 AND <lfs_kna1> IS ASSIGNED.
                IF <lfs_kna1>-land1 = 'IN'.
                  <lfs_loc>-vtweg = '10'.
                ELSE.
                  APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_loc_det ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_det>.
                  <lfs_loc_det>-kunnr = <lfs_loc>-locno_2.
                  <lfs_loc_det>-vkbur =  'EXP'.

                ENDIF.
              ENDIF.
            ENDLOOP.
          ENDIF.

** Fetch the sales office Details from KNVV
          IF gt_loc_kn IS NOT INITIAL.

            me->clear_tables( ).

            me->fill_selection_customer( 'KNVV' ).

            me->execute_select_in_ecc(
              IMPORTING
                et_data       = gt_kunnr_final
                et_ret        = lt_return
            ).
            LOOP AT gt_kunnr_final ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_det>.
              READ TABLE gt_loc_kn  WITH KEY locno_2 = <lfs_loc_det>-kunnr
                                             vtweg = <lfs_loc_det>-vtweg
                                             spart = <lfs_loc_det>-spart TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS.
              IF sy-subrc NE 0 .
                CLEAR <lfs_loc_det>-kunnr.
              ENDIF.
            ENDLOOP.
            DELETE gt_kunnr_final WHERE kunnr IS INITIAL.
            IF lt_loc_det IS NOT INITIAL.
              APPEND LINES OF lt_loc_det TO gt_kunnr_final.
            ENDIF.
            IF gt_kunnr_suffix IS NOT INITIAL.
              SORT gt_kunnr_suffix.
              DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM  gt_kunnr_suffix COMPARING ALL FIELDS.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.


    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "fetch_loc_detail_customer

  METHOD fill_selection_customer.


    DATA:
    lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF gty_ecc_mat_master,
    lt_selopt             TYPE rsdsselopt_t,
    lt_frange             TYPE rsds_frange_t.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <lfs_ecc_mat>   TYPE gty_kna1,
      <lfs_selopt>    TYPE rsdsselopt,
      <lfs_frange>    TYPE rsds_frange,
      <lfs_range>     TYPE rsds_range,
      <lfs_range_tab> TYPE dmc_s_tabname_range,
       <lfs_ecc_mat2> TYPE gty_kna1,
       <lfs_loc_kn>   TYPE gty_loc_kn.

    IF iv_tabname = 'KNA1'.
      "" Field Selection
      APPEND 'KUNNR' TO gt_field_sel.
      APPEND 'LAND1' TO gt_field_sel.

      "" Table Selection
      APPEND 'KNA1 AS KNA1' TO gt_tab_sel.
      "" Range table selection
      UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_range_tab ASSIGNING <lfs_range_tab>.
      IF <lfs_range_tab> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_range_tab>-sign = 'I'.
        <lfs_range_tab>-option = 'EQ'.
        <lfs_range_tab>-low = 'KNA1'.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR: lt_selopt,lt_frange.
      UNASSIGN  <lfs_ecc_mat>.

      LOOP AT gt_kunnr_suffix ASSIGNING <lfs_ecc_mat>.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
        IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
          <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
          <lfs_selopt>-low = <lfs_ecc_mat>-kunnr.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
      IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'KUNNR'.
        <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_range>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_where_cond ASSIGNING <lfs_range>.
      IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_range>-tablename = 'KNA1'.
        <lfs_range>-frange_t  = lt_frange.
      ENDIF.

    ELSE.

      APPEND 'KUNNR' TO gt_field_sel.
      APPEND 'VTWEG' TO gt_field_sel.
      APPEND 'SPART' TO gt_field_sel.
      APPEND 'VKBUR' TO gt_field_sel.

      "" Table Selection
      APPEND 'KNVV AS KNVV' TO gt_tab_sel.
      "" Range table selection
      UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_range_tab ASSIGNING <lfs_range_tab>.
      IF <lfs_range_tab> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_range_tab>-sign = 'I'.
        <lfs_range_tab>-option = 'EQ'.
        <lfs_range_tab>-low = 'KNVV'.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_range_tab>.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR: lt_selopt,lt_frange.
      UNASSIGN  <lfs_ecc_mat>.

      LOOP AT gt_loc_kn ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_kn>.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
        IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
          <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
          <lfs_selopt>-low = <lfs_loc_kn>-locno_2.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      SORT lt_selopt BY low.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_selopt COMPARING low.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
      IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'KUNNR'.
        <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
      ENDIF.
      CLEAR:lt_selopt[].
      LOOP AT gt_loc_kn ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_kn>.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
        IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
          <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
          <lfs_selopt>-low = <lfs_loc_kn>-vtweg.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      SORT lt_selopt BY low.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_selopt COMPARING low.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
      IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'VTWEG'.
        <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR:lt_selopt[].

      LOOP AT gt_loc_kn ASSIGNING <lfs_loc_kn>.
        UNASSIGN <lfs_selopt>.
        APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_selopt ASSIGNING <lfs_selopt>.
        IF <lfs_selopt> IS ASSIGNED.
          <lfs_selopt>-sign = 'I'.
          <lfs_selopt>-option = 'EQ'.
          <lfs_selopt>-low = <lfs_loc_kn>-spart.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      SORT lt_selopt BY low.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_selopt COMPARING low.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_frange>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lt_frange ASSIGNING <lfs_frange>.
      IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_frange>-fieldname  = 'SPART'.
        <lfs_frange>-selopt_t   = lt_selopt.
      ENDIF.

      UNASSIGN <lfs_range>.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_where_cond ASSIGNING <lfs_range>.
      IF <lfs_frange> IS ASSIGNED.
        <lfs_range>-tablename = 'KNVV'.
        <lfs_range>-frange_t  = lt_frange.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.


    CLEAR: lt_selopt, lt_tmp_ecc_mat_master.
  ENDMETHOD.                    "fill_selection_customer

ENDCLASS.                    "lcl_material_balance IMPLEMENTATION
